Abstract: Sandbagging models strategically underperform on evaluations while retaining the capabilities being measured. The evaluations that guide frontier-model deployment and governance then understate what these models can do. To understand the mechanism, we propose a causal model of how sandbagging is carried in the residual stream. Early layers write the sandbagging intent onto a single axis of the stream, and a later layer reads that axis and commits the answer. We study three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Mistral-7B) and four ways of installing a sandbagging lock (prompting, fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and circuit breaking). Each lock decides from the prompt whether to sandbag, and the fine-tuned and circuit-broken locks answer honestly whenever a password appears. The causal model predicts a window of layers, after the last sandbagging write and before the answer commit, in which a single-layer reference graft of the sandbagging axis to its honest value restores the full capability. The single-layer graft recovers the capability in 28 of the 33 runs of the prompted, fine-tuned, and RL-trained locks, with a median held-out recovery of 96%. The circuit-broken lock rewrites the whole state through a band of layers, and the single-layer graft fails at every layer. We therefore introduce a second intervention, context grafting, which replays the password's cached key/value activations so that every layer's attention reads them as additional context. Context grafting provably and empirically restores the full capability on all three models, and the recovery is surprisingly insensitive to the exact password content. More broadly, an auditor can use this causal model to design interventional auditing techniques for sandbagging models.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证知识图谱表示对话为实体和关系是否能提升召回率这一假设。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have been proposed as a structured alternative to flat retrieval-augmented generation for long-term agent memory, on the assumption that representing conversations as entities and relations improves recall. We evaluate that assumption directly. Our framework extracts each conversational turn into typed nodes and attributed edges, answers questions from a two-hop subgraph, and periodically prunes nodes that score low on a weighted combination of recency, access frequency, degree centrality, and age. On LongMemEval, the graph does not outperform a flat vector baseline at a matched candidate-generation budget of five retrieval roots: token F1 is $0.417$ against $0.468$, and a paired bootstrap over 500 questions gives
$Δ= -0.050$ (95\% CI $[-0.085, -0.016]$). The gap is widest on questions that require recalling a specific prior assistant turn, where judged correctness falls from $0.911$ to $0.607$, suggesting that decomposing a turn into entities discards the surface form these questions depend on. The forgetting module is more successful. Applied once to a persistent 27{,}021-node graph, it removes 9.8\% of nodes and 9.5\% of stored bytes; token F1 is unchanged ($+0.001$, 95\% CI $[-0.015, +0.016]$) and judged correctness falls by $1.6$ points, with the 95\% interval bounding any loss at $3.8$ points ($[-0.038, +0.006]$). Because our extractor is a single small model evaluated on one benchmark, these results characterise this extraction-based pipeline rather than graph-structured memory in general. Code: https://github.com/skhanzad/Selective-Amnesia

</details>

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Stein去噪算子的投影梯度引导扩散更新方法，在推理时仅用预训练去噪器和梯度评估即可优化任务目标，并保持数据几何结构，理论证明在多种约束集上具有收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型使用外部目标梯度引导反向过程时，容易使生成样本偏离学习到的数据流形或约束集，导致样本无效或失真；需要一种能在优化目标的同时保持数据几何结构的推理时引导方法。

Method: 将目标梯度直接嵌入去噪步骤中，利用Stein去噪算子作为数据几何的近似投影，形成非精确投影梯度更新；在三个设置（线性流形、紧凸集、紧黎曼子流形）下分析其收敛性质。

Result: 理论上证明在所有三种设置下更新满足目标下降性和有限时间收敛；数值实验验证了理论解释，并显示该方法能在目标优化与几何保持之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 该投影梯度引导方法提供了一种简单有效的推理时引导策略，兼容预训练模型，兼顾目标优化与数据几何约束，具有理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used not only for sampling from learned data distributions, but also for generating samples that optimize task-specific objectives. A common approach is to guide the reverse diffusion process using gradients of an external objective. However, when the data distribution is supported on a structured feasible set, such as a manifold or a constraint set, gradient guidance can move samples away from the learned data geometry. In this paper, we study a simple projected-gradient-guided diffusion update based on the observation that the Stein denoising operator can act as an approximate projection onto the data geometry. The proposed update incorporates the objective gradient inside the denoising step, yielding an inference-time method that uses only a pretrained denoiser and gradient evaluations. We analyze this update as an inexact projected-gradient method for constrained optimization over learned feasible geometries. Our theory covers three settings: linear manifolds, compact convex feasible sets, and compact Riemannian submanifolds. In all these settings, we prove descent and finite-time convergence guarantees. Numerical experiments support the theoretical interpretation and illustrate how the proposed update balances objective descent with preservation of the learned geometry.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of grape berry temperature (Tb) is essential for enabling timely heat stress management in vineyards. Models were trained using environmental data from 2023 and 2024 at Prosser, WA, USA, and validated on 2025 summer data. FAM-LSTM was benchmarked against LSTM, GRU, RNN, and Random Forest (RF) across horizons ranging from 15 minutes to 72 hours (288 time steps). Two input scenarios were evaluated: nearest open-field weather station observations and in-vineyard microclimate measurements. FAM-LSTM consistently outperformed all benchmark models across all horizons and input scenarios. Incorporating in-vineyard microclimate data significantly improved forecasting accuracy at longer horizons. Using open-field data, FAM-LSTM achieved MAE and RMSE ranges of 0.58 to 1.70 deg C and 0.65 to 2.07 deg C, respectively. In-vineyard observations further improved performance, with MAE and RMSE in the ranges of 0.51 to 1.55 deg C and 0.71 to 1.87 deg C. Error analysis showed prediction uncertainty was highest during peak daytime periods (11:00 to 18:00) and increased progressively with forecast horizon. Overall, the FAM-LSTM framework offers robust Tb forecasting to support precision heat stress management in vineyards.

</details>


### [45] [On the Plasticity Collapse in Continual Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29513)
*Yingdan Shi,Xiang Xu,Kaize Ding,Alfred O. Hero,Ren Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文发现持续机器遗忘中普遍存在可塑性崩溃现象，即参数空间中的几何约束累积导致模型逐渐丧失遗忘能力，表现为前向失败（后续任务遗忘质量下降）与后向失败（已遗忘信息自发重现）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统需应对序列化的多次遗忘请求，而现有研究主要关注单次遗忘，缺乏对持续遗忘动态中模型长期可靠性的理论分析与实证验证。

Method: 通过理论分析遗忘操作的几何效应，提出参数子空间饱和机制解释可塑性崩溃，并在多种架构、数据集与方法上进行图像分类实验验证其普适性。

Result: 实验证实可塑性崩溃并非实现特例，而是持续遗忘固有现象；前向失败与后向失败在不同条件下稳定出现，且随遗忘次数增加而加剧。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了持续遗忘系统的关键可靠性障碍，强调需设计保持可塑性的遗忘算法，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Machine unlearning enables deep neural networks to selectively remove the influence of specific data in response to privacy and regulatory requirements. While prior work largely studies single-shot unlearning, real-world systems must accommodate continual unlearning, where multiple unlearning requests occur sequentially over time. In this work, we identify a fundamental limitation of this setting: plasticity collapse, a progressive breakdown in a model's ability to effectively forget. Through theoretical analysis of continual unlearning dynamics, we show that continual unlearning operations accumulate geometric constraints in parameter space, leading to saturated subspaces that restrict future updates. This structural effect induces two distinct failure modes: (1) Forward failure -- diminishing forgetting quality for subsequent tasks, and (2) Backward failure -- spontaneous re-memorization of previously forgotten information. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures, datasets, and methods in image classification confirm that plasticity collapse is not an artifact of specific implementations, but a pervasive phenomenon inherent to continual unlearning. Our findings reveal a critical barrier to the long-term reliability of machine unlearning systems and motivate the development of plasticity-preserving unlearning algorithms. Our code is available at https://github.com/TIML-Group/Continual-Machine-Unlearning-Plasticity-Collapse

</details>


### [46] [MedCache: Efficient and Temporally Valid Memory for Longitudinal Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29528)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Boyuan Zheng,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MedCache混合框架，通过时间有效记忆、重叠专科视图、查询路由和自适应多专家调用，提升纵向临床代理在跨就诊、跨专科证据检索与推理中的准确率和记忆效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床代理需跨多次就诊和专科维护演化的患者状态，但现有代理记忆设计缺乏系统研究，且多专业记录中的长上下文检索、跨时间聚合和跨专科推理具有独特挑战。

Method: 构建多就诊多专科患者记录基准，系统比较记忆的策展、组织、检索和记忆增强推理四个设计选择，并据此提出MedCache：构造时间有效的患者记忆、按重叠专科视图组织证据、路由查询到相关记忆，并自适应调用单或多专科代理。

Result: MedCache在推理准确率和记忆效率上优于强单代理和多代理基线，并跨模型骨干和外部数据集泛化。实验揭示时间有效性比保留更多历史更重要，专科因子化记忆可减少上下文但可能隐藏共享证据，多代理仅在专科需协作推理时有益。

Conclusion: 纵向临床代理的记忆设计应优先保证时间有效性并支持跨专科共享证据的可见性；自适应混合记忆与路由机制可高效平衡上下文长度和推理质量，为临床AI代理提供实用设计范式。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical agents must maintain an evolving patient state from evidence distributed across visits, time points, and specialties. However, how agent memory should be designed for this setting remains unclear. We introduce a benchmark of multi-visit, multi-specialty patient records that evaluates long-context evidence retrieval, cross-time evidence aggregation, and cross-specialty clinical reasoning. Using this benchmark, we systematically study four memory design choices: curation, organization, retrieval, and memory-augmented reasoning. We find that temporal validity is more important than simply retaining more history; specialty-factorized memory reduces context but can hide shared evidence; and multiple agents help when specialists must reason together, not merely when evidence comes from multiple memories. Guided by these findings, we propose \textit{MedCache}, a hybrid framework that constructs temporally valid patient memory, organizes evidence into overlapping specialty views, routes each query to relevant memories, and adaptively invokes one or multiple specialists. Experiments show that MedCache improves reasoning accuracy and memory efficiency over strong single-agent and multi-agent baselines, while generalizing across model backbones and external datasets.

</details>


### [47] [Which LLM for Which Work? Budgeted Model Allocation under Uncertain Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29560)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对固定AI预算下LLM工作负载分配问题，提出CASE方法，通过两次求解（估计表与最不利表）验证分配最优性，并利用因果/离策略估计修复代理指标偏差，实验表明测量误差比分配优化更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需在固定预算下分配LLM到重复工作负载，但质量表未知且难以估计：模型缺乏同任务对比、评分多为代理指标；现有方法分别处理因果性或评估有效性，但无法解决评分生成机制导致的表不确定性，且单纯增加评估不能消除该不确定性。

Method: 提出CASE（因果主动序列实验）框架：先以估计质量表求解背包问题，再以最不利质量表求解；若两次分配一致则认证最优，否则定位影响决策的模型-工作负载对并定向补充实验，迭代直至收敛。

Result: 在生产日志中，测量失败造成的损失大于分配优化误差；随机重评估无法消除该损失。在付费软件任务中，可用证据常不足以确定分配，而改进质量信息比在相同估计上优化分配带来更多节省。

Conclusion: 固定预算LLM分配的核心瓶颈是质量表不确定性而非优化算法；CASE提供精确认证及定向评估策略，且改进测量信息比进一步优化分配更有效，未来应侧重因果性评估设计。

Abstract: A company with a fixed artificial intelligence (AI) budget must decide which large language model (LLM) handles each recurring workload. What it lacks is the quality table, how well each model performs on each workload. Given that table, the decision is a multiple-choice knapsack problem and is routine to solve, so estimating it is the difficulty, and that estimation fails in two ways. Models are rarely compared on the same work, and the recorded score is usually a proxy rather than the outcome the company values. Causal and off-policy methods repair the first but condition on the second, while evaluator-validation methods estimate the second but stop short of the decision. Worse, buying more re-evaluation cannot settle the second: randomization governs which requests are scored, not how a score is produced, so the table stays uncertain however much evaluation is purchased. Yet the deployment decision may still be determined even when the table is not. We therefore ask whether one assignment stays optimal across every quality table consistent with the evidence. For the fixed-budget problem, this admits an exact two-solve certificate: solve once at the estimated table and once at a least-favourable table. Agreement certifies the assignment; disagreement identifies the model-workload pairs where further evidence can matter. We propose CASE (causal active sequential experimentation), which targets evaluation to those pairs and repeats the test as evidence accumulates. On a production log, the measurement failure is the larger of the two: correcting assignment exactly still leaves most of the loss, and randomized re-evaluation does not remove it. In our experiments, the available evidence often does not determine the assignment. On paid software tasks, better information about model quality yields more savings than further optimization of the assignment on the same estimates.

</details>


### [48] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ICWBench基准和两阶段训练方法，使LLM在不损害回答质量的前提下可靠执行上下文水印指令，显著提升检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未系统评估LLM遵循上下文水印指令的能力，且缺乏同时保证可检测性和回答质量的方法。

Method: 构建含三类可验证指令的ICWBench基准，提出自蒸馏（SDLP）加强化学习的两阶段训练，无需外部蒸馏、标注或预训练能力。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B上，平均TPR@1%FPR从0.100/0.337提升至0.974/0.968，同时维持高回答质量（困惑度和LLM评判均良好）。

Conclusion: 所提训练方法能有效赋予LLM可靠的上下文水印能力，为第三方调用提供实用接口，且不影响输出质量。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [49] [Asynchronous Cooperative Online Learning for Multi-Robot Control under Computational Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29562)
*Xiaobing Dai,Zewen Yang,Wei Ren,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统在不确定环境下的安全运行问题，提出一种考虑异步计算延迟和查询点差异的分布式高斯过程协同学习策略，并结合伴随多智能体系统设计分布式控制律，在无人水面艇仿真中验证了学习和控制性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式高斯过程聚合方法忽略了智能体间计算能力差异和预测任务不同导致的异构计算延迟及查询点差异，这些因素会降低协同学习精度和控制可靠性，亟需解决。

Method: 提出异步协同学习策略，显式建模预测精度、查询点变化和延迟效应；同时基于伴随多智能体系统设计分布式控制律，以保障期望控制性能。

Result: 在无人水面艇仿真中，该方法相比现有先进方法，在学习和控制性能上均获得显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提异步协同学习方法有效克服了延迟和查询异质性影响，提升了多智能体系统在不确定环境下的学习与控制安全性与性能。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe operation of multi-agent systems (MASs) under uncertain environments is crucial for cooperative robotic, where external disturbances and inaccurate dynamic models can significantly compromise performance and reliability. To address this challenge, calibrated machine learning models, particularly Gaussian process (GP) regression, are extensively employed due to their interpretable performance quantification. As the interconnected communication of MASs facilitates cooperative learning, agents are able to enhance learning performance by exchanging local GP inferences with their neighbors and aggregating the received information via distributed GP strategies. However, variations in computational power and prediction tasks among agents inevitably lead to heterogeneous computational delays and differences in query points, which are often overlooked in existing aggregation methods. To overcome these limitations, this work proposes an asynchronous cooperative learning strategy that explicitly accounts for prediction accuracy, query point variations and delay effects. Additionally, a distributed control law based on an adjoint MAS is developed to ensure the desired control performance. Simulations on unmanned surface vehicles validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, demonstrating substantial improvements in both learning and control performance compared to the state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>

*Thao Le,Michael Thielscher*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EmoLASP框架，将语言模型与回答集编程（ASP）结合用于对话情绪预测（VAD分数），在IEMOCAP基准上验证能提升性能，尤其对仅提示的LLM效果显著，且无需微调或长对话历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话情绪识别常依赖语言模型，但微调成本高且提示长对话历史不稳定、昂贵。

Method: EmoLASP将LLM/PLM的预测与ASP声明式推理相结合，利用逻辑规则约束VAD分数，从而提升一致性和性能。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，对六种LLM和两种PLM的实验显示，EmoLASP整体优于单独使用语言模型；对仅提示LLM提升最大，对已微调PLM在无历史时有益，有历史时增益很小。

Conclusion: EmoLASP展示了推理方法在降低微调和提示成本的同时提升情绪预测一致性的潜力，尤其适用于大规模LLM的零样本场景。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversations is increasingly tackled with language models, but these models can be unstable and expensive to fine-tune or to prompt with long dialogue histories. We propose EmoLASP, a framework that combines a language model with declarative reasoning via Answer Set Programming (ASP) to predict VAD scores (Valence-Arousal-Dominance) in conversations. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset (IEMOCAP) across six open-source LLMs (3B-120B) and two PLMs (BERT, RoBERTa) show that EmoLASP improves prediction performance compared to using the language model alone, even when the LLMs/PLMs are given no dialogue history in their prompts or input vectors. The gains are largest for prompt-only LLMs, which EmoLASP uses without any fine-tuning. However, for fine-tuned PLMs, the reasoner adds little once dialogue history is available. EmoLASP's LLM pipeline demonstrates the potential advantages of using a reasoning approach to ensure emotion prediction consistency and to reduce both the cost of fine-tuning and the cost of prompting with long dialogue histories.

</details>


### [51] [HoopMind: A Real-Time Neural Game-Tree System for Opponent-Aware Possession Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29563)
*Yibo Gong,Cong Guo,Jiacheng Ding*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 利用公开篮球数据构建半场攻防决策模型，通过ShotNet和深度受限expectimax搜索实现实时战术规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业球队的分析工具对学校教练不可及，探究公开数据能否缩小这一差距。

Method: 融合5个公开数据源形成423万次投篮数据集，用嵌入MLP（ShotNet）评估投篮价值，结合分支定界剪枝的深度受限expectimax搜索求解进攻决策树。

Result: ShotNet在留出赛季上优于区域防守基线和逻辑回归基线，概率校准良好；数据对齐率达99.5%-100%。

Conclusion: 公开数据足以支持轻量级实时战术分析系统，且所有训练离线完成，浏览器即可运行。

Abstract: School coaches prepare for opponents with game film and intuition. The analytics tools of professional teams stay out of reach. We ask how far public data can close this gap. Professional basketball is our case study, chosen for its data rather than the league. We fuse five public sources into one per-shot dataset of 4.23M shots over 21 seasons. The sources are shot locations, two play-by-play feeds, official matchup tracking, and player biometrics. Alignment across them is 99.5% to 100%. We also report two data pitfalls that are easy to miss. We then model a half-court possession as a sequential game. Shot values come from ShotNet, an embedding multilayer perceptron (MLP). On a held-out season it beats a zone-rate baseline and a logistic baseline, and its probabilities are well calibrated. A depth-limited expectimax search then solves the offensive decision tree, with branch-and-bound pruning to keep it real time. All training runs offline, so the online system stays light. A scouting planner and a playable simulator both run in a single browser page.

</details>


### [52] [Agent2UCB: Agentic System for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29063)
*Sheldon Yu,Rui Wang,Tong Yu,Sungchul Kim,Doga Dogan,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent2UCB，一种基于大语言模型和在线强化学习的智能体系统，用于自动优化内容在生成式搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews）中的可见性，并在GEO-Bench上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews、Perplexity）的兴起，内容优化从传统SEO转向GEO（生成式引擎优化）。然而，现有GEO方法缺乏自动化和自适应能力，难以针对不同内容选择最佳优化策略，且可能忽视对SEO质量的影响，亟需一个可持续反馈优化的系统。

Method: Agent2UCB系统为每个内容项评估九种GEO策略，利用基于UCB（上置信界）的bandit策略，结合LLM先验和在线奖励信号，自适应地选择最有效的优化方法。系统还提供轻量级、纯文本的SEO就绪度评估，涵盖可读性、主题覆盖和EEAT风格可信度，以监控优化带来的副作用。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准上的实验表明，Agent2UCB在保持SEO质量的同时，稳定提升了内容在生成式搜索引擎中的可见性，并展示了与基线方法的对比效果。

Conclusion: Agent2UCB展示了智能体驱动GEO的可行性和有效性，既提升生成式引擎可见性，又兼顾传统SEO质量，为内容创作者提供了可操作的工具，并开放了演示以支持实践应用。

Abstract: Large language model driven search engines such as Google AI Overviews and Perplexity have created new opportunities for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) the practice of refining content to increase its likelihood of being cited or summarized by generative systems. We demonstrate Agent2UCB, an agentic GEO system that autonomously improves content visibility through customized, feedback-driven optimization. For each content item, the system evaluates nine GEO strategies, identifies the most effective method, and accelerates selection using a bandit-based Agent2UCB policy that integrates LLM priors with online reward signals. To monitor side effects, the system also provides a lightweight, text-only SEO readiness evaluation covering readability, topical coverage, and EEAT-style credibility. Experiments on GEO-Bench show consistent visibility gains while preserving SEO quality. The demo allows users to choose the websites of interest, observe the optimization workflow, and compare GEO/SEO outcomes across methods.

</details>


### [53] [On the Resilience of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models to Hardware Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29598)
*Zachary Coalson,A M Aahad,Stella Doehring,Zane Ma,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 首次系统研究了文本到视频扩散模型在硬件级随机故障下的鲁棒性，发现单一故障最多可导致3.7%的性能下降，内存故障比计算故障更具破坏性，且7-28%的故障会引发可见伪影甚至语义改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频扩散模型广泛应用于自动视频生成，但其迭代去噪过程和时空依赖引入了独特故障模式，而现有研究缺乏对硬件级随机故障影响的系统性评估，难以保障部署可靠性。

Method: 对三种T2V模型和代表性基准进行大规模故障注入实验，覆盖计算故障与内存故障，并对比不同数值格式（如bfloat16）及指数位高层位的影响，评估语义正确性与感知质量的变化。

Result: 单一故障可致总体性能下降至3.7%，语义正确性受损大于感知质量；内存故障危害大于计算故障，高阶指数位尤为脆弱，bfloat16比替代格式更敏感；7-28%的故障产生可见伪影，包括新增物体等语义改动。

Conclusion: T2V系统在实际部署中存在显著可靠性风险，单点硬件故障足以改变输出语义，需进一步研究故障弹性提升方法。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of the resilience of text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models under random hardware-level faults. While T2V models are widely used for automated video generation due to their ability to produce high-quality, temporally coherent, and realistic videos, their iterative denoising process and spatiotemporal dependencies introduce unique failure modes. We perform an extensive fault-injection study covering both computational and memory faults across three T2V models and a representative benchmark. Our results show that (1) a single fault can degrade overall performance by up to 3.7\%, with semantic correctness more affected than perceptual quality; (2) memory faults are more damaging than computational faults, high-order exponent bits are particularly vulnerable, and the widely-used bfloat16 is more susceptible than alternative formats; and (3) 7-28\% of faults cause visible artifacts, including semantic changes such as added objects, suggesting that single faults are sufficient to alter output semantics. Our findings reveal reliability risks in deployed T2V systems and motivate further research on improving fault resilience. Code: \href{https://github.com/ztcoalson/T2V-Resilience}{https://github.com/ztcoalson/T2V-Resilience}.

</details>


### [54] [HANIA: Planner-Guided Multimodal Graph Evidence Selection for Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29088)
*Zafar Ali,Asad Khan,Nimbeshaho Thierry,Nabila Amir,Adam A. Q. Mohammed,Pavlos Kefalas*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HANIA框架，通过规划器引导的多模态图证据检索，在不微调的情况下提升问答鲁棒性并压缩证据预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态问答对噪声、不完整和弱依据证据敏感，长上下文易引入冗余并助长无依据生成，平面检索难以捕捉多步推理所需的关系。

Method: 使用冻结视觉-语言模型提取简洁且与问题相关的视觉证据（含显式弃权），构建输入引导的多模态图，并通过两群有限状态规划器协调描述性和关系性证据；采用覆盖感知剪枝，依据相关性、图置信度、概念覆盖和模态多样性保留紧凑证据集，最终将选定段落、视觉陈述和图三元组送入冻结的指令微调解码器。

Result: 在ScienceQA上达到竞争性答案准确率，同时在证据过滤质量、证据预算敏感性和效率方面表现良好，无需目标数据集微调或迭代检索。

Conclusion: 结构化证据规划与紧凑图引导检索能够支持高效且稳健的多模态问答，且不依赖额外训练或反复检索。

Abstract: Multimodal question answering remains sensitive to noisy, incomplete, and weakly grounded evidence. Long unstructured contexts can introduce redundancy and encourage unsupported generation, while flat retrieval may overlook relations needed for multi-step reasoning. We present HANIA, a planner-guided multimodal graph framework for evidence-grounded question answering. HANIA processes the supplied image and text using a frozen vision-language model to extract concise question-relevant visual evidence with explicit abstention. It then constructs an input-grounded multimodal graph and applies a two-group finite-state planner to coordinate descriptive and relational evidence. Coverage-aware pruning retains a compact evidence set based on relevance, graph confidence, concept coverage, and modality diversity. The selected passages, visual statements, and graph triples are provided to a frozen instruction-tuned decoder. We evaluate HANIA on ScienceQA using answer accuracy, evidence-filtering quality, evidence-budget sensitivity, and efficiency. The results show that structured evidence planning and compact graph-guided retrieval can support competitive multimodal question answering without target-dataset fine-tuning or iterative retrieval. The code is available at https://github.com/Zafar-southeast/HANIA.

</details>


### [55] [Wide Learning: Learning to Reach Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29608)
*Junzhou Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出‘有效认知范围’概念，指学习者在固定原始能力下，受自身状态、预算和可靠性约束能实际实现的证据生成实验族，并通过‘宽学习’刻画任务相关的学习诱导变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习评估在固定证据接口下进行，忽略了学习者状态对能获取何种证据的影响；本文旨在补充评估维度，关注学习如何改变可实现的证据范围而非仅从给定证据中提取信息。

Method: 形式化定义相对于学习者状态、部署预算、可靠性阈值和评估分布的有效认知范围；通过受控构造，在五个原始动作子系统中比较校准前后实现诊断性实验的概率，并测量公共通道与实现诊断的总变差及密封二元风险。

Result: 校准前，单次寻址实现诊断概率≤2^{-10}（低于0.95阈值）；校准后，持有外实现概率为1。公共通道总变差为0，而实现诊断的总变差为1，密封二元风险从约1/2降至0。

Conclusion: 学习能在原始能力和部署资源固定时改变有效认知范围，开启对学习系统的新评估视角：不仅评估从可用证据中推断的能力，还评估经验带来的将信息性证据纳入可及范围的能力。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually evaluated after an evidence interface has been fixed. A dataset, sensor suite, query language, action set, or experimental protocol determines which observations can be obtained, and learning is judged by what it extracts from them. We study a complementary capability. A learner's state can determine which evidence-generating experiments it can reliably realise under bounded resources, even when primitive affordances remain fixed. We call this learner-relative experiment family its effective epistemic reach, and use Wide Learning for task-relevant learning-induced changes in that family.We formalise effective reach relative to learner state, deployment budget, reliability threshold, and evaluation distribution. In a controlled construction, two hidden worlds have exactly the same public observation law. An informative diagnostic exists in a fixed five-primitive substrate. Before calibration, one address attempt realises it with probability at most $2^{-10} = 1/1024$, below a pre-specified 0.95 threshold; after calibration, held-out realisation is 1. Public-channel total variation is 0, whereas the realised diagnostic has total variation 1, and sealed binary risk moves from approximately 1/2 to 0. The construction establishes that learning can change effective epistemic reach even when primitive affordances and deployment resources are held fixed. It opens a complementary evaluation question for learning systems: not only what they infer from available evidence, but what informative evidence experience teaches them to bring within reach.

</details>


### [56] [EviAnchor: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Regional Visual Evidence Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29092)
*Sihang Jia,Shuliang Liu,Songbo Yang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EviAnchor，一种无需训练的单分支推理框架，通过区域证据锚点槽和决策条件路由，在生成全过程中保留并激活视觉证据，显著提升LVLM的视觉锚定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM常生成与视觉输入不符的内容，且前期实验发现视觉证据主要在解码器早中期层影响答案表示，后期层直接作用减弱，导致早期获取的视觉证据在后继生成中利用不足。

Method: EviAnchor包含两个关键组件：一是区域证据锚点槽，逐步将密集视觉token聚合成空间结构化表示；二是决策条件证据路由，根据当前决策状态强化对视觉锚点的访问，减少对文本上下文的过度依赖，随后恢复原生Transformer计算以整合视觉证据、问题语义和生成历史。

Result: 在POPE、CHAIR和MMHal-Bench基准上均取得一致的视觉锚定改进。

Conclusion: EviAnchor通过训练免费的方式有效缓解了视觉证据衰减问题，增强了LVLM生成过程中的视觉接地性，为提升多模态模型可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) frequently generate content unsupported by visual inputs. Preliminary experiments show that visual evidence is primarily incorporated into answer-side representations in early-to-middle decoder layers, while its direct influence progressively weakens in later layers. This attenuation suggests that visual evidence acquired earlier may be insufficiently utilized during subsequent generation. Based on this observation, we propose EviAnchor, a training-free and single-branch inference framework that preserves and reactivates visual evidence throughout generation. EviAnchor introduces Regional Evidence Anchor (REA) slots to progressively aggregate dense visual tokens into spatially structured representations. It then strengthens the current decision state's access to these visual anchors through decision-conditioned evidence routing, mitigating excessive dependence on textual context. Finally, the model resumes its native Transformer computation to integrate the retrieved visual evidence with question semantics and generation history. Experiments across POPE, CHAIR, and MMHal-Bench demonstrate consistent improvements in visual grounding.

</details>


### [57] [LLMODE: Aligning ODEs with LLMs via Gated Token Injection for Irregular Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29640)
*Di Zhang,Jingyang Zhang,Ziqian Wang,Chi Zhang,Yikun Ban,Ziwei Zhang,Ruijie Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LLMODE框架，利用图感知ODE编码器和固定预算感知重采样器，将不规则时空观测压缩为动态记忆令牌，并注入冻结的大语言模型，实现高效且强泛化的不规则时空预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM时空预测方法依赖规则采样的令牌序列，难以处理不规则观测，面临时间异步、表示空间错位和上下文窗口有限等问题。

Method: 首先用图感知ODE编码器将不规则图观测重建为连续时间潜轨迹；再用固定预算感知重采样器将变长轨迹压缩为固定数量的动态记忆令牌；同时编码统计描述符并重采样为上下文记忆令牌；最后通过双源门控交叉注意力将两类记忆注入冻结的LLM。

Result: 在三个城市数据集和两个物理动力学基准上取得有竞争力的整体性能，在稀疏或动态复杂的不规则采样下优势明显，并在未见城市区域上表现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLMODE通过令牌高效和双记忆注入机制，有效提升了冻结LLM在不规则时空预测中的实用性，尤其适用于数据稀疏和动态复杂的场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for spatio-temporal forecasting, but existing approaches often rely on regularly sampled token sequences and struggle with irregular observations because of temporal asynchrony, representation-space misalignment, and limited context windows. We propose LLMODE, a token-efficient framework for irregular spatio-temporal forecasting with a frozen LLM backbone. LLMODE first uses a graph-aware ODE encoder to reconstruct irregular graph observations as a continuous-time latent trajectory. A Fixed-Budget Perceiver Resampler then compresses this variable-length trajectory into a fixed number of dynamic memory tokens. In parallel, compact statistical descriptors are encoded and resampled into context memory tokens. A dual-source gated cross-attention module injects both memories into the frozen LLM, enabling controlled utilization of external spatio-temporal evidence. Experiments on three real-world urban datasets and two physical-dynamics benchmarks show competitive overall performance, with clearer advantages under sparse or dynamically complex irregular sampling. Additional evaluations on unseen urban regions further demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization without adaptation.

</details>


### [58] [A Target-Centric Survey of Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29667)
*Jiamin Song,Mengjie Zhao,Zijing Wang,Yongkang Liu,Qian Li,Shi Feng,Feiliang Ren,Daling Wang,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文围绕低比特量化训练（QAT）技术，提出以目标为中心的综述框架，系统梳理其理论基础、方法分类、跨目标差异与评估范式，并展望未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展导致巨大的内存占用和计算需求，QAT作为一种在训练中显式模拟量化效应的技术，有望在低比特精度下接近全精度模型表现，但领域内缺乏系统性的目标导向梳理。

Method: 采用目标为中心的视角，建立QAT方法的分类体系；跨目标对比误差特性、数值格式与策略可迁移性；总结QAT的评估范式及优化部署中的挑战。

Result: 归纳了不同目标下QAT方法的共性特征与关键差异，明确了当前评估框架的局限性，并识别出优化与部署环节的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: QAT在低比特LLM部署中具有重要潜力，但需进一步统一跨目标方法设计、完善评估体系，并针对优化与部署的挑战探索新策略。

Abstract: The rapid development of LLMs incurs prohibitive memory footprints and intensive computational demands. Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) techniques have emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by explicitly simulating quantization effects during model training, yielding low-bit models that achieve accuracy comparable to their full-precision counterparts. In this work, we provide a target-centric survey of QAT, aimed at clarifying both its theoretical foundations and its evolving implementation landscape. We systematically review existing QAT methods through a target-centric taxonomy and synthesize cross-target differences in error characteristics, numerical formats, and strategy transferability. We further summarize QAT evaluation paradigms and discuss challenges in optimization and deployment, outlining potential directions for future research.

</details>


### [59] [Creation begins with understanding: LLMs as strategy designers for privacy-preserving tabular data synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29674)
*Jinmeng Li,Quan Zhang,Hangting Ye,He Zhao,Firas Laakom,Dandan Guo,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: TabSSD 利用 LLM 设计合成程序而非直接生成记录，结合树结构摘要与 Python 执行，在 12 个数据集上以最低资源和最优综合排名实现高保真、高效用与低隐私风险的表格数据合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据共享受隐私法规限制，深度生成模型训练成本高且难审计，基于 LLM 的直接序列化方法则破坏表格结构并易泄露敏感数据，亟需一种低成本、透明且安全的替代方案。

Method: TabSSD 将变量依赖关系的树形摘要（而非原始记录）提供给 LLM，由其生成可在本地执行和评估的 Python 合成程序，支持人工引导优化且无需用户侧模型调参。

Result: 在 12 个数据集上，TabSSD 在统计保真度、预测效用和经验隐私风险六项指标中取得最佳平均排名（对比 10 种方法），并显著降低本地计算量和 token 消耗。

Conclusion: TabSSD 通过将 LLM 从记录生成转向流程设计，并依赖树摘要与本地执行，成功权衡了数据质量、隐私与资源效率，降低了透明表格合成领域的专业和基础设施门槛。

Abstract: Sharing tabular data in high-stakes domains is constrained by privacy regulations. Synthetic data offer a promising alternative, but deep generative models are costly to train and difficult to audit, while LLM-based methods often serialize records as text, obscuring tabular structure and exposing sensitive data. We introduce Tabular Synthesis Strategy Designer (TabSSD), which uses an LLM to design synthesis procedures rather than directly generate records. TabSSD provides the LLM with tree-derived summaries of variable dependence rather than raw records, which produces Python programs for local execution and evaluation. Across twelve datasets, TabSSD strikes a favourable balance among statistical fidelity, predictive utility, and empirical privacy risk, achieving the best average rank across six metrics among ten methods. Moreover, it substantially reduces local computation and token consumption relative to the compared methods. By enabling human-guided refinement and eliminating user-side model tuning, TabSSD lowers the expertise and infrastructure barriers to transparent tabular data synthesis.

</details>


### [60] [Last Step Matters: Early Uncertainty Cannot Predict Failure in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29685)
*Zongyue Li,Chengyue Yu,Lei Zang,Chenyi Zhuang,Linjian Mo,Leilei Gan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究评估了深度研究任务中的不确定性信号，发现言语置信度在轨迹完成时能可靠预测失败（AUROC 0.85），但在执行中途所有信号预测能力有限（AUROC 不超过 0.60），原因在于路径切换机制，并建议使用最终步置信度决定是否重启而非中途干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的早期失败预测对及时干预和降低成本至关重要，但现有不确定性信号在中途执行阶段是否有效尚未被探讨，因此需要系统评估其在深度研究任务中的判别能力。

Method: 在深度研究任务上，对比评估主流不确定性信号（如言语置信度和困惑度），在不同轨迹进度下计算AUROC，并分析失败预测失效的机制，最后实验对比中途干预与最终重启策略。

Result: 言语置信度在轨迹完成时AUROC达0.85，但在50%进度时所有信号AUROC均低于0.60；路径切换是导致早期信号失效的关键原因；最终步置信度重启策略比中途干预更有效。

Conclusion: 中间阶段的不确定性信号不足以可靠指导早期干预，实际应用中应优先采用最终步置信度进行重启决策，以提升长时程智能体的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Early failure prediction is important for long-horizon agents, as it enables timely intervention and can reduce inference and tool-use costs. Uncertainty quantification, such as verbal confidence and perplexity, offers a promising approach to detecting agent failures; however, it has not been explored whether these signals retain their discriminative power during the intermediate stages of long-horizon execution. We evaluate mainstream uncertainty signals on deep-research tasks and find that verbal confidence reliably distinguishes failures at trajectory completion, achieving a mean AUROC of 0.85, whereas all evaluated signals offer limited predictive value earlier in execution, with none exceeding a mean AUROC of 0.60 at 50% trajectory progress. We identify an underlying mechanism explaining this gap: path switching, where agents frequently abandon their current search direction in-trajectory, breaking the link between early signal and final outcome. These findings challenge the assumption that intermediate uncertainty can reliably guide early intervention. They also motivate a practical recommendation for agent harnesses in deep-research settings: use final-step confidence to decide whether to restart, an approach that our experiments find more effective than in-trajectory intervention.

</details>


### [61] [More Perspectives, Stronger Signals: Multi-Perspective Enhancement and Progressive Fusion for Multimodal Entity Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29139)
*Chenyi Xiong,Yan Zhang,Jing Hu,Ziyue Qin,Kui Xiao,Xiaopan Lyu,Xiaoju Hou,Zhifei Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出PrismF框架，通过多视角增强与渐进式融合，解决多模态知识图谱补全中的语义过度平滑和跨模态噪声过滤问题，在三个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态实体表示方法在稀疏或模糊条件下，存在模态内语义过度平滑和模态间噪声过滤失效的问题，限制了推理任务（如MMKGC）的效果。

Method: PrismF包含两个核心机制：1）多视角增强：将每个模态分解为互补视角，并施加解耦损失以防止表示坍缩，强化细粒度模态内语义；2）渐进式融合：动态校准模态间交互，突出信息性信号并抑制噪声，优化跨模态集成。

Result: 在三个公开基准上取得最佳综合性能，其中在KVC16K上MRR相对提升4.04%，Hits@1提升11.17%。

Conclusion: PrismF有效缓解了多模态表示学习中的关键问题，验证了多视角增强与渐进式融合策略的优越性，并公开代码以促进复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Learning effective multimodal entity representations is fundamental for reasoning tasks such as multimodal knowledge graph completion (MMKGC). However, existing methods often suffer from semantic over-smoothing within modalities and ineffective noise filtration across modalities, particularly under sparse or ambiguous conditions. To overcome these limitations, we propose PrismF, a unified framework that synergizes multi-perspective enhancement with progressive fusion to extract stronger signals from diverse inputs. PrismF enhances fine-grained intra-modal semantics through a multi-perspective mechanism that decomposes each modality into complementary views and constrains them with a decoupling loss to reduce representation collapse. Furthermore, it improves cross-modal integration through a progressive fusion strategy that dynamically calibrates inter-modal interactions, enabling the model to emphasize informative signals while suppressing noisy or unreliable ones. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks show that PrismF achieves the strongest overall performance, including relative improvements of 4.04% in MRR and 11.17% in Hits@1 on KVC16K. Our code can be found at https://github.com/HubuKG/PrismF.

</details>


### [62] [JudgePanel: A Compact Judge with Panel Deliberation via Adaptive Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29168)
*Yiyue Qian,Shinan Zhang,Huan Song,Hannah Marlowe*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出JudgePanel框架，让紧凑的Judge模型具备多智能体Panel deliberation能力，以单模型推理成本实现多智能体评估协议的质量，并通过自适应多奖励RL和轻量级领域适配模块提升效果与部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式因模型固有偏见而受限，多智能体评估虽能缓解但推理成本过高；需要一种兼顾质量与效率的评估方法。

Method: 基于强评估器集成的panel deliberation轨迹进行SFT训练；引入AdaReward自适应多奖励RL算法，动态调整奖励权重以应对不同目标饱和速率差异；设计轻量级领域适配模块用于快速适应新领域。

Result: 14B参数的JudgePanel在四个评估基准上超过高达70B的专用评估模型，表现出强位置一致性，并能以少量样本快速适配新领域。

Conclusion: JudgePanel首次实现单紧凑模型具备多智能体panel deliberation能力，验证了其有效性、可靠性和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative to human evaluation. However, single-model judges are limited by their inherent model biases, while multi-agent evaluation protocols that mitigate this through diverse deliberation are prohibitively expensive at inference time. To this end, we propose \textbf{\modelname}, which equips a compact \underline{Judge} model with multi-agent \underline{Panel} deliberation capability. Specifically, we first train on panel deliberation traces from an ensemble of strong evaluators, capturing structured patterns of discussion, disagreement, and resolution. To further improve judgment quality beyond SFT, we introduce \textit{AdaReward}, an adaptive multi-reward RL algorithm that dynamically rebalances reward component weights as different objectives saturate at different rates during RL training. For practical deployment, we further design a lightweight domain specialization module for rapid adaptation to new evaluation domains with few hundred labeled samples. As a result, (i) \textit{Novel}: the first framework to equip a single compact judge with multi-agent panel deliberation capability at single-model inference cost; (ii) \textit{Effective \& Reliable}: JudgePanel with a 14B backbone outperforms judge-specialized models up to 70B across four evaluation benchmarks, demonstrates strong position consistency, and rapidly specializes to new domains with few hundred samples.

</details>


### [63] [ECA-BLS: An Efficient Complex-Augmented Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29763)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Sajid,M. Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文首次提出复数增强宽度学习系统（CA-BLS）及其高效实数域等价实现（ECA-BLS），通过相位编码复数表示与宽线性建模，充分利用二阶统计量（协方差与伪协方差），在保持理论等价的前提下大幅降低计算成本，并在26个基准数据集上显著优于现有BLS与随机神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BLS变体仅处理实值数据，无法捕获真实数据中的非线性交互与二阶统计依赖；尚未有BLS模型充分利用复数域嵌入产生的完整二阶统计信息，这成为BLS研究的一个关键缺失维度。

Method: CA-BLS将实值输入变换为相位编码的复数表示，并采用宽线性建模（widely linear modeling），通过复数共轭增强同时利用协方差与伪协方差信息；为解决计算开销，提出ECA-BLS，将CA-BLS完全重构于实数域并保持其精确决策函数，严格理论证明两者数学等价。

Result: 在UCI与KEEL的26个基准数据集上，ECA-BLS在准确率、平均排名和统计显著性上持续优于经典BLS及最新随机神经网络，同时乘法运算减少高达75%、加法运算减少超过60%，且理论零损失。

Conclusion: 二阶统计增强建模是BLS研究中此前缺失的关键维度，CA-BLS/ECA-BLS提供了一种高效且理论上无损的实现方式，为该方向奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Broad Learning System (BLS) is an efficient alternative to deep architectures due to its fast training, analytical learning, and strong generalization under limited data. However, existing BLS variants are confined to real-valued representations, restricting their ability to capture nonlinear interactions and second-order statistical dependencies inherent in real-world data. Notably, no prior BLS model fully exploits the complete second-order statistics that naturally emerge when data are embedded in the complex domain. To address this limitation, this paper introduces the first complex augmented Broad Learning System (CA-BLS), which transforms real-valued inputs into phase-encoded complex representations and adopts widely linear modeling to jointly leverage covariance and pseudo-covariance information via complex conjugate augmentation. This enables effective modeling of latent nonlinearities, coherence structures, and second-order dependencies inaccessible to conventional BLS formulations. To mitigate the additional computational cost of complex augmentation, an Efficient Complex Augmented BLS (ECA-BLS) is further developed, reformulating CA-BLS entirely in the real domain while preserving its exact decision function, achieving up to 75\% fewer multiplications and over 60\% fewer additions. A rigorous theoretical analysis proves the mathematical equivalence between CA-BLS and ECA-BLS, ensuring zero theoretical loss. Extensive experiments on 26 benchmark datasets from the UCI and KEEL repositories demonstrate that ECA-BLS consistently outperforms classical BLS and recent state-of-the-art randomized neural networks in accuracy, average rank, and statistical significance, establishing augmented second-order modeling as a critical and previously missing dimension of BLS research.

</details>


### [64] [Structure Aware Neural Architecture Search for Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29817)
*Petr Babkin,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个联合优化数据簇与专家分配的NAS框架，通过广义EM算法求解，在图像和时间序列混合任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE设计未显式对齐专家与数据结构，NAS很少用于MoE，缺乏联合优化分配和架构的方法。

Method: 将问题转化为簇感知的似然最大化，用广义EM过程求解，其中不可处理的专家质量项由自适应精化的替代模型提供，并证明收敛条件。

Result: 在异构图像分类中准确恢复95%的潜在域划分（无需域标签），并在图像和时间序列基准上优于不使用标签信息的MoE和NAS基线。

Conclusion: 显式搜索数据簇与专家的对齐能提升MoE性能，且理论保证收敛性，为无标签异构任务提供有效方案。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has so far rarely been applied to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, and existing MoE designs leave the alignment between experts and the structure of the data to emerge on its own. We propose an architecture search framework that makes this alignment an explicit search variable: the assignment of data clusters to experts is optimised jointly with the per-expert architectures. We cast the joint problem as a cluster-aware likelihood maximisation, show that it coincides with the incomplete-data maximum likelihood of a latent-variable mixture, and solve it by a generalised Expectation-Maximisation procedure whose otherwise intractable expert-quality term is supplied by an adaptively refined surrogate. We prove that the iterates converge whenever the surrogate errors are summable, and that at every limit point no candidate the search produces improves the true objective. On a heterogeneous image-classification mixture the method recovers the underlying domain partition on 95% of clusters without ever observing domain labels, and on that benchmark and a four-domain time-series forecasting one alike it outperforms the MoE and NAS baselines that likewise use no label information.

</details>


### [65] [Designing for the Next Click: Bandits for Real-Time Page Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29850)
*Bhavtosh Rath,Harshith Narasimhamurthy,Bob Eisinger,Cole Stiegler,Adnan Awow,Amit Pande*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于上下文老虎机的电商页面布局实时优化系统，在保留人工设计意图的同时，通过LinUCB策略动态选择每会话最优布局，并在线上A/B测试中取得正向提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台通过机器学习实现个性化体验，但页面布局仍依赖静态规则和人工设计，缺乏实时优化能力，难以动态匹配用户需求。

Method: 采用上下文老虎机框架，以LinUCB为策略，利用用户、商品和类目特征，在每次会话中平衡探索与利用，选择最优布局；系统架构支持低延迟推理和持续模型更新，并集成于大规模web服务栈。

Result: 在主要零售平台的入口商品页线上A/B测试中，相比强启发式基线，会话级性能指标获得正向提升。

Conclusion: 上下文老虎机能有效优化产品发现的视觉和结构方面，提升用户参与度，为网页学习型设计提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: E-commerce platforms increasingly personalize user experiences through machine learning, yet page layout decisions remain dominated by static rules and manual curation. We present a scalable bandit-based system that optimizes product page layouts in real time while preserving human control over design intent. A contextual bandit model dynamically selects the most effective layout for each session using user, item, and category-level features. The system leverages a LinUCB-based policy to balance exploration and exploitation as it learns from live user interactions. The architecture is designed for seamless integration into large-scale web serving stacks, supporting low-latency inference and continuous model updates. The system was first tested on entry product pages. In online A/B deployments on a major retail platform, our approach achieved positive lifts in session-level performance metrics over a strong heuristic baseline. Our results demonstrate that contextual bandits can effectively optimize visual and structural aspects of product discovery for user engagement, providing a scalable path toward learning-to-design the web.

</details>


### [66] [Uncertainty-Driven Replay Memory for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29860)
*Sheeraja Rajakrishnan,Alexander G. Ororbia,Travis Desell,Daniel E. Krutz*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于不确定性驱动的经验回放缓冲区（UDRM），通过优先存储高不确定性转移来提升RL智能体的训练奖励与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法主要利用时序差分误差或转移分布更新回放缓冲区，忽略了不确定性信息对探索与泛化的潜在价值，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 设计不确定性驱动的记忆更新机制，在训练过程中利用模型的不确定性估计动态筛选并存储更不确定的转移样本，从而偏差缓冲区内容以强化学习。

Result: 实验表明UDRM在训练期间能获得比现有不确定性感知RL框架更高的奖励。

Conclusion: 不确定性驱动的回放缓冲区能有效提升RL训练性能，为利用不确定性改善经验利用提供新思路。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation provides promising capabilities for reinforcement learning (RL) agents. Notably, estimating uncertainty can reduce the training time and enable agents to obtain greater rewards over time by exploiting information related to whether an action would facilitate exploration of portions of an environment that are well-known versus those that are relatively unknown. In this work, we propose a novel formulation of the experience replay buffer commonly used in RL that we call uncertainty-driven replay memory (UDRM), which entails an update scheme for internally stored memories based on uncertainty estimates obtained by an RL model during training. In contrast to existing forms of RL, which typically use temporal difference error or the distribution of transitions to update the replay memory buffer and train RL controllers, our scheme biases the memory buffer to store more uncertain transitions that will improve an RL agent's generalization throughout training. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed uncertainty-aware replay buffer enables an RL agent to obtain higher rewards during training compared to other existing uncertainty-aware RL frameworks.

</details>


### [67] [Towards an Expressivity-Normalized Energy-Demand Comparison of ANNs and SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29869)
*Miriam Kranzlmüller,Pascal Esser,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一个分析框架，在匹配表达能力的前提下比较全连接ReLU ANN与整合发放SNN在时间序列数据上的理论能效，导出归一化效率比和明确阈值，刻画事件驱动计算抵消SNN时间开销的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN常被视为ANN的节能替代，但其优势严重依赖网络结构和数据特性，缺乏在表达能力匹配条件下进行理论能效比较的框架。

Method: 将推理能耗模型与表示表达能力理论界关联，推导表达归一化效率比，并给出网络宽度、脉冲稀疏性和ANN深度缩放方面的显式阈值。

Result: 分析刻画了不同机制区间，表明ANN仅在特定条件下在表达归一化效率上超过SNN。

Conclusion: 提供了容量感知的设计原则，用于构建节能的时间序列网络，并明确了SNN优势的适用边界。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are often regarded as energy-efficient alternatives to artificial neural networks (ANNs), yet their advantage depends critically on both network architecture and data properties. We develop an analytical framework to compare fully-connected ReLU ANNs and integrate-and-fire SNNs for time-series data with respect to their theoretical energy efficiency at matched expressive capacity. By relating an inference-energy model to theoretical bounds on representational expressivity, we derive an expressivity-normalized efficiency ratio and explicit thresholds in network width, spike sparsity, and ANN depth scaling. Our analysis characterizes the regimes in which event-driven computation offsets the temporal overhead of SNNs, providing capacity-aware principles for designing energy-efficient temporal networks. It shows that ANNs exceed SNNs in expressivity-normalized efficiency only in specific regimes.

</details>


### [68] [Dynamic Important Example Mining for Reinforcement Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29252)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Shizhen Zhao,Yang-Tian Sun,Tianjia Liu,Chirui Chang,Shaofeng Zhang,Samm Sun,Xiuzhe Wu,Ruobing Xie,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DIEM框架，通过梯度对齐重要性估计和约束批重加权，在强化微调中动态调整样本使用，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心的RFT方法依赖静态或启发式样本选择，忽略策略学习的非平稳动态，导致次优更新。

Method: DIEM在每个优化步骤集成两个组件：梯度对齐重要性估计器（近似样本对策略改进的边际贡献）和约束批重加权方案（最大化总效用同时保持梯度幅度以稳定优化）。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，DIEM持续优于强静态和动态基线。

Conclusion: DIEM提供了一种自适应数据利用的自动化框架，有效提升RFT推理能力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) is increasingly used to strengthen the reasoning abilities of large models, yet its effectiveness is bound by how training data are selected and used. Most data-centric RFT methods rely on static or heuristic sample selection, implicitly assuming a sample's value is fixed over training. This overlooks the non-stationary dynamics of policy learning and can lead to suboptimal updates. We propose Dynamic Important Example Mining (DIEM), a principled and fully automated framework that makes data utilization adaptive throughout RFT. DIEM integrates two components into each optimization step: (i) a gradient-alignment importance estimator that efficiently approximates each sample's marginal contribution to policy improvement; and (ii) a constrained batch reweighting scheme that maximizes aggregate utility while preserving the update's gradient magnitude to stabilize optimization. Across several reasoning benchmarks, DIEM consistently outperforms strong static and dynamic baselines. The code will be released via https://github.com/hrtan/DIEM.

</details>


### [69] [RACER: Reinforced Agent Collaboration for Explainable Reasoning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29263)
*Yuwei Lou,Hao Hu,Yuzhou Jiang,Zongfei Zhang,Liang Wang,Jincai Liu,Jidong Ge,Xianping Tao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的多智能体协作框架RACER，通过语义感知动作剪枝、教师引导强化学习、跨任务共享记忆和注意力驱动的多路径知识精炼，提升大语言模型在知识图谱上的推理性能，在CommonsenseQA和OpenBookQA上平均提升5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂多跳推理中易产生幻觉且依赖单一智能体路径提取和固定提示，缺乏适应性并面临巨大搜索空间，现有KG增强方法未能充分解决这些问题。

Method: RACER框架包括：语义感知动作剪枝和教师引导的强化学习用于高效提取高质量推理路径；跨任务累积共享记忆图配合注意力驱动的多路径知识精炼模块；四个角色（GraphAgent、TemplateAgent、AnswerAgent、CriticAgent）的多智能体协作系统动态优化提示并评估答案。

Result: 在CommonsenseQA和OpenBookQA数据集上，RACER平均比现有最先进KG增强LLM基线提高5%，展现鲁棒性和高可解释性。

Conclusion: RACER通过强化学习与多智能体协作显著增强KG上的复杂推理能力，提供了高效、可解释的推理方案，为未来知识增强LLM研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucination and struggle with complex reasoning tasks requiring multi-hop domain knowledge. While integrating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) provides a structured and verifiable information source, current KG-enhanced LLM paradigms usually rely on single-agent path extraction and fixed prompting, lacking adaptability and facing huge search spaces. To address these challenges, we propose RACER, a Reinforced Agent Collaboration framework for Explainable Reasoning on knowledge graphs. RACER employs a semantic-aware action pruning and teacher-guided reinforcement learning mechanism to efficiently extract high-quality reasoning pathways from large-scale KGs. Furthermore, to mitigate single-path generation pitfalls, we introduce a cross-task accumulated shared memory graph paired with an attention-driven multi-path knowledge refinement module. Finally, RACER orchestrates these components through a four-role multi-agent collaboration system (GraphAgent, TemplateAgent, AnswerAgent, and CriticAgent) to dynamically refine prompts and evaluate answers. Extensive experiments on CommonsenseQA and OpenBookQA datasets demonstrate that RACER significantly outperforms state-of-the-art KG-enhanced LLM baselines with an average improvement of 5\%, offering robust and highly interpretable reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [70] [INTERVenE: Temporal-Abstraction-Interval Based Transformers for Short-Horizon Medical Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29901)
*Shahar Oded,Yuval Shahar*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出INTERVenE，一种基于知识的时间抽象（KBTA）的Transformer架构，在MIMIC-IV上优于现有基线，兼顾预测性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重症监护EHR数据稀疏且不规则，现有模型忽视时间的临床意义且缺乏透明度，需要一种能保留临床时间含义并支持透明决策的预测方法。

Method: 采用区间级KBTA将原始数据转化为命名临床概念令牌流，构建两种Transformer变体：自回归解码器（INTERVenE-Ar）生成未来轨迹及逐步骤风险，双向编码器（INTERVenE-Enc）进行单次风险和时间事件预测。

Result: 在57,078次MIMIC-IV入院中，INTERVenE-Enc的AUPRC_w为0.672（较最强基线提升0.041），AUROC_w为0.901，LOS MAE为44.4小时；INTERVenE-Ar在生成式任务中达到AUROC_w 0.854和AUPRC_w 0.587。消融实验证实性能提升跨结构化离散化迁移。

Conclusion: KBTA作为可解释输入表示，使逐令牌归因直接对应临床概念，实现高性能与临床透明性的兼容，为ICU预测模型提供新方向。

Abstract: Electronic Health Record (EHR) prediction models in the intensive care unit must learn from sparse and irregular measurements while preserving the clinical meaning of time and supporting transparent decision-making. We present INTERVenE, a family of Transformer architectures whose input is an interval-based, knowledge-based temporal abstraction (KBTA), a token stream of named clinical concepts (states, trends, events, contexts) drawn from a curated medical ontology, rather than an unnamed bin index or a raw measurement triplet. This naming layer is what we ask KBTA to do: it makes the model's per-token attributions resolve to clinical concepts by construction. INTERVenE offers two complementary variants: an auto-regressive decoder that generates future abstraction trajectories with a per-step risk readout (localizing \emph{when} and \emph{after which events} risk rises), and a bidirectional encoder for single-pass joint risk and time-to-event prediction. Evaluated on 57,078 MIMIC-IV admissions against GRU-D, STraTS, and KarmaLego, INTERVenE-Enc reaches a support-weighted AUPRC$_w$ of 0.672, improving by 0.041 over the strongest neural baseline with non-overlapping 95\% bootstrap CIs, while also taking the best AUROC$_w$ (0.901) and length-of-stay MAE (44.4\,h). INTERVenE-Ar (AUROC$_w$ $0.854$, AUPRC$_w$ $0.587$ under the same evaluation contract - a strictly harder generative readout) provides a complementary token-level risk trajectory. An input-representation ablation confirms the lift transfers across structured discretizations, positioning KBTA-based intervals as the interpretable substrate that makes per-token attributions resolve to meaningful clinical concepts within the deployed model.

</details>


### [71] [EpaCache: Error-Propagation-Aware Caching for Accelerating Diffusion-Based Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29264)
*Yuhan Liu,Zongwei Hong,Jinglun Li,Linze Li,Shen Zhang,Yao Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EpaCache，一种训练无关的缓存策略，通过追踪误差传播轨迹，将缓存预算分配给下游影响更小的时间步，从而在图像和视频生成中显著改善延迟与保真度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于缓存的加速方法仅依赖局部时序变化来决策缓存复用，忽略了缓存误差在后续时间步中的累积传播，导致关键时间步的精度损失。

Method: EpaCache引入误差传播感知机制，建模每个时间步的缓存复用对后续生成轨迹的全局影响，并据此自适应分配复用预算，优先在低下游影响的时间步进行缓存。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上，推理时间从11.7s降至11.3s，PSNR从21.4提升至22.8；在HunyuanVideo上实现2.63倍加速，SSIM从0.891提升至0.905，优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: EpaCache通过考虑轨迹级误差传播，实现了更优的缓存策略，为扩散模型的高效推理提供了新的训练无关方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based visual generative models deliver strong image and video synthesis quality but incur high inference costs because sequential samplers repeatedly evaluate large networks. Caching-based methods reduce inference latency by reusing intermediate computations across adjacent timesteps. However, existing cache controllers rely primarily on local temporal variation and overlook the trajectory-level consequences of cache reuse. We introduce Error-Propagation-Aware Cache (EpaCache), a training-free caching policy that adaptively allocates the reuse budget on timesteps with lower downstream impact. Experiments on image and video synthesis models demonstrate that EpaCache consistently improves the latency--fidelity trade-off over existing caching methods. On FLUX.1-dev, EpaCache outperforms the prior state-of-the-art caching method in both latency and fidelity, reducing inference time from $11.7$ s to $11.3$ s while improving PSNR from $21.4$ to $22.8$. On HunyuanVideo, EpaCache achieves a $2.63\times$ speedup over uncached inference and improves SSIM from $0.891$ to $0.905$ over the prior state-of-the-art method at matched latency.

</details>


### [72] [Diffusion-Based Inverse Design of Dielectric Resonator Metasurfaces for Shaping Smart Electromagnetic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29907)
*M. Tsukerman,K. Grotov,D. Vovchuk,P. Ginzburg*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出条件扩散框架，用于从目标角散射模式逆向设计介质谐振器超表面，生成多种候选几何结构，性能优于传统优化和确定性神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超表面逆向设计是非线性、高维且解不唯一的病态问题，现有方法难以高效生成可行且多样的设计方案。

Method: 基于T矩阵模拟数据训练条件扩散模型，学习给定散射模式下的几何条件分布，而非确定性映射。

Result: 最佳设计平均百分比误差1.39%，优于CMA-ES（4.1%，耗时10小时），推理时间仅约1分钟；对分布外光谱的误差分布也优于确定性基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型为超表面逆向设计提供高效、鲁棒的解决方案，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Future wireless systems are expected to transform the surrounding space from a passive propagation medium into a smart electromagnetic environment, where engineered surfaces control wave propagation, support wireless sensing, and create programmable electromagnetic fingerprints. A key challenge in realizing this vision is the inverse design of metasurfaces for tailored electromagnetic propagation. While forward analysis evaluates the response of a known geometry, the inverse task starts from a prescribed scattering signature and seeks a physically realizable structure that produces it. This inverse task is inherently nonlinear and often high-dimensional, while candidate solutions may be non-unique and provide no direct indication of practical realizability. Here, we introduce a conditional diffusion framework for inverse design of dielectric resonator metasurfaces from target angular scattering patterns. Trained on T-matrix simulated geometry-response pairs, the model learns a conditional distribution of geometries instead of a deterministic mapping, enabling multiple candidate designs for the ill-posed inverse problem. The best generated metasurface achieves a mean percentage error of 1.39%, outperforming CMA-ES optimization (4.1% after 10 h) while requiring only about one minute for after-training inference. The model also produces lower error distributions than deterministic neural baselines for out-of-distribution spectra, highlighting the potential of diffusion models for efficient metasurface design.

</details>


### [73] [On the Recoverability of Private Information Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29943)
*Shicheng Hu,Runzhi Tian,Ziqiao Wang,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种白盒审计框架，通过构造含假隐私信息的合成数据集，发现现有5种机器遗忘方法无法真正删除敏感信息，简单的‘逆贪心’解码可恢复被遗忘内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法是否真正擦除敏感信息仍不明确，缺乏统一框架量化其持久性，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 构建包含假隐私信息的合成数据集，设计白盒审计框架，通过逆贪心解码（每步选概率最低词）评估5种现有遗忘方法的信息残留程度。

Result: 所有被评估的遗忘方法均未能完全消除敏感信息，逆贪心解码能有效恢复声称已遗忘的隐私数据。

Conclusion: 当前机器遗忘方法可靠性不足，需开发更稳健的技术确保LLM部署中的隐私保护。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can memorize sensitive information, raising serious privacy concerns. Machine unlearning offers a potential solution to remove such information, but it remains unclear whether existing methods truly erase it or merely hide it within the model. A key challenge is quantifying the persistence of sensitive data under a unified evaluation framework. To address this, we construct a synthetic dataset containing fake private information and propose a white-box auditing framework to systematically assess whether claimed-forgotten information is genuinely removed. Using this framework, we evaluate five existing unlearning methods and find that a simple "inverse greedy" decoding -- selecting the least likely token at each step -- can recover supposedly forgotten private information. Our results reveal that current unlearning approaches often fail to fully eliminate sensitive information, highlighting the need for more reliable methods to ensure privacy in deployed LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [MMPCBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models on Proactive Critique of Flawed Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29286)
*Jinzhe Li,Gengxu Li,Jinnan Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MMPCBench框架，系统评估多模态大模型（MLLMs）的主动批判能力，发现模型在检测、诊断和修复用户错误输入方面存在明显缺陷，并揭示推理过程中正确认知与最终输出间的“一致性差距”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多聚焦于理想场景或简单拒答行为，忽略了对错误输入的主动识别与修复能力，而该能力对交互式助手的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 构建细粒度错误分类体系（4大类12子类），设计分层评估协议（错误检测、诊断、解决）及对齐感知指标，衡量内部推理与最终回答的一致性，并在14个主流MLLM上进行测试。

Result: 主流模型在主动批判上表现薄弱，尤其对细微视觉异常处理不佳；普遍存在“一致性差距”，即推理模型内部能正确识别错误但最终输出时抑制正确见解以迎合指令。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs的主动批判能力不足，需改进推理与输出对齐机制，以提升其作为交互助手的可靠性与真实性。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) evolve into sophisticated interactive assistants, their reliability depends not only on following instructions but also on validating them. We define Proactive Critique as the model's autonomous ability to identify, analyze and fix faulty user inputs without extra prompts. However, evaluations mainly test models under ideal circumstances or simple refusal behaviors, largely ignoring active error processing. To fill this gap, we propose MMPCBench, a comprehensive framework for evaluating MLLMs' proactive critique competence. It features a fine-grained taxonomy of 4 primary error types spanning 12 subcategories, ranging from cross-modal contradictions to missing visual premises. We adopt a hierarchical evaluation protocol to measure models' error detection, diagnosis and resolution performance, and apply alignment-aware metrics to assess the coherence between internal reasoning and final responses. Tests on 14 mainstream MLLMs show obvious weaknesses in proactive critique, especially in dealing with subtle visual anomalies. Notably, we identify a pervasive "consistency gap": reasoning models can often correctly identify and analyze errors during internal reasoning yet suppress these valid insights in final outputs to prioritize response compliance. The code and data is available at https://github.com/ALIENS32/MMPCBench.

</details>


### [75] [Accelerating Unified Multimodal Models with Core-Expansion Routing and Unified Computation Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29291)
*Wengyi Zhan,Chenqian Yan,Songwei Liu,Mingbao Lin,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CE-Router，利用任务共享核心评分器和进度条件生成扩展，实现统一多模态模型的高效推理，保留98.03%理解性能并获得1.93倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型在理解与生成任务中存在大量冗余计算，现有方法未充分考虑两类任务在token重要性上的结构差异。

Method: 通过token重要性探测发现非对称核心-扩展结构，设计CE-Router包含共享核心评分器与进度条件生成扩展，结合生成分解和跨任务核心对齐训练；推理时配合统一计算调度实现层跳过、FFN剪枝、扩散头缓存复用和去噪早退。

Result: 在两个代表性统一多模态架构上，理解性能保持98.03%，端到端推理速度提升1.93倍，生成任务也获得一致的质量-效率改进。

Conclusion: 验证了任务共享核心加条件扩展的稀疏计算模式能有效兼顾统一多模态模型的理解与生成性能，为高效多模态推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models jointly support understanding and generation, but incur substantial redundant computation across tokens, layers, and generation timesteps. Through token-importance probing, we identify an asymmetric core-expansion structure: understanding exhibits a stable importance component, while generation largely shares this component but requires progress-dependent corrections. We therefore propose CE-Router, which uses a task-shared core scorer and progress-conditioned generation expansions, optimized through generation decomposition and cross-task core alignment. At inference, CE-Router compacts token computation and supplies a learned routing signal to Unified Computation Scheduling, which coordinates layer skipping, FFN pruning, diffusion-head cache reuse, and denoising-step early exit. Experiments on two representative UMM architectures demonstrate consistent quality--efficiency improvements across both tasks, retaining 98.03\% of dense understanding performance with a 1.93$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [76] [The Intervention Gap in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29998)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 规划时干预保真度是可测量的世界模型属性，需通过捕获门控匹配干预审计直接验证，而非依赖奖励拟合或任务锚定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估依赖奖励误差或任务性能，无法揭示模型在开放循环干预中的行为偏差，尤其在操作符错误诊断和任务变量转移上存在盲区。

Method: 提出捕获门控匹配干预审计，比较模型想象轨迹与真实环境干预对任务变量的影响，并分析后验分布、集成分歧及支持感知评分等实践因素。

Result: 任务锚定模型在操作符保留上劣于自监督模型；Cheetah中想象干预效果低于无效应预测；失败源于任务方向旋转与增益过剩而非特征坍缩，且模式具有条件性和种子依赖性。

Conclusion: 干预保真度必须直接、捕获优先地审计模型原生接口，避免依赖代理指标导致误导性结论。

Abstract: Planning-time intervention fidelity is a distinct, measurable property of a learned world model: whether the model's own open-loop transitions move task variables the way matched environment interventions do. In the settings we test, it is neither revealed by reward fit nor ensured by task-anchored training. Across released TD-MPC2 checkpoint sizes, episode return falls as an operator-error diagnostic on task observables grows, while reward-prediction error stays small and nearly flat, and a self-supervised world model trained without task signal preserves the same operator substantially better than a task-anchored model on the shared task. A capture-gated matched-intervention audit then localizes what fails. On Cheetah, three LeWorldModel checkpoints capture the current task query and support decodable real intervention effects; however, their imagined five-step effects are worse than predicting no effect and worse than an environment-endpoint oracle. The failure is task-direction rotation with excess gain, not feature collapse. This severe pattern is conditional: five PreJEPA seeds retain an oracle-relative deficit without it, Finger Spin experiments extend the deficit beyond locomotion with heterogeneous severity across seeds, and shared-bank effect geometry is both candidate- and support-dependent. We also test practice-side questions. In DreamerV3 the posterior distribution, not its sample, carries the current query; ensemble disagreement ranks error only near training support; and a frozen support-aware score degrades held-out error ranking in both tested transfer directions while native disagreement remains informative in both. We conclude that intervention fidelity must be audited directly, capture-first, on the model's native interface.

</details>


### [77] [Formal Concept Analysis with Three Types of Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29311)
*Zhenghua Pan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出FCACOI，将矛盾否定、对立否定和中间否定引入形式概念分析，并基于逻辑LCOI+PLCOI建立属性蕴含推理的语义基础，同时探讨属性约简框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典FCA仅处理对象与属性间的正关系，缺乏否定机制，无法表达矛盾、对立及中间状态等复杂语义。

Method: 通过定义三类否定（矛盾、对立、中间）及其形式背景、Galois连接、形式概念与概念格，构建FCACOI；引入ICOI-衍推作为属性蕴含推理的语义蕴含，并基于LCOI+PLCOI逻辑建立推理关联；提出两种属性约简研究框架。

Result: 证明了FCACOI中的属性蕴含推理与LCOI+PLCOI逻辑推理的一致性，形式化推理规则在FCACOI中有效，并通过实例展示其应用能力，同时比较了两种属性约简框架的特点。

Conclusion: FCACOI借助更丰富的逻辑与语义，将FCA从仅描述肯定关系的理论扩展为能同时描述肯定、矛盾、对立及中间状态的理论，为复杂否定推理提供了逻辑基础。

Abstract: Classic Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) primarily focuses on the positive relationships between objects and attributes and does not have mechanisms for handling negation.To overcome this limitation, we introduce three types of negation concepts (contradictory negation, opposite negation, intermediary negation) into FCA.Based on the set SCOI and logic LCOI+PLCOI with these three types negation, we define formal context, Galois connection operators, formal concept and concept lattice with three types of negation,this leads to the proposal of a FCACOI: Formal Concept Analysis with contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation.For the reasoning in FCACOI, this paper focuses on attribute implication reasoning. Based on the logic LCOI+PLCOI and its semantics, we introduce the notion of ICOI-entailment as the semantic implication for attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI. Through ICOI-entailment, a connection is established between attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI and inference in the logic LCOI+PLCOI, it indicate that formally proven inference rules (theorems) in LCOI+PLCOI are valid in the attribute implication reasoning of FCACOI, LCOI+PLCOI provides a logical foundation for attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI. To illustrate the capability of attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI, we discuss its application in a concrete example. Moreover, we explore attribute reduction of the formal context in FCACOI, propose two research frameworks for attribute reduction from different perspectives, and compare their characteristics.We believe that, based on richer logic and semantics, FCACOI elevates FCA from a theory that describes affirmations to one that can describe affirmations and its contradiction(either this or that), opposition(extreme negation) and intermediary (transitional states between oppositions).

</details>


### [78] [Multiclass Linear Perceptrons with Multiplicative Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30028)
*Dmitri Rachkovskij,Evgeny Osipov,Olexander Volkov,Daswin De Silva,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有乘法边际机制的多类线性感知机分类器（MMPerc），通过要求真实类别得分超过其他类别得分的指定比例而非固定加性阈值，来避免因数据范数差异导致的边际失效问题，并在线性和非线性可分数据上推导了损失函数和错误界，实验表明其性能优于标准感知机、SVM和Ridge分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准感知机使用固定加性边际，其效果受类别权重向量和数据范数影响，导致分类置信度不稳定；而乘法边际机制能自适应地按比例约束得分差距，避免对得分绝对大小的依赖，同时保持感知机的简单性和计算效率。

Method: 设计了多种MMPerc架构和算法变体，推导了相应的损失函数和错误界（覆盖线性可分和不可分情况），并分析了偏置项、边际阈值选择和训练模式等关键设计因素。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，MMPerc通常优于标准感知机及经典基线（如SVM和Ridge分类器），且具备简单、计算高效的特点。

Conclusion: 鉴于其简洁性、极简设计和计算效率，MMPerc适用于传统机器学习任务、深度神经网络的线性评估、超维计算/向量符号架构集成，以及资源受限应用场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a family of multiclass linear Perceptron classifiers with a multiplicative margin mechanism (MMPerc), as an alternative to standard margin-free and additive margin Perceptrons. The multiplicative formulation enforces classification confidence by requiring the true class score to exceed that of competing classes by a specified fraction of itself, rather than by a fixed additive threshold. This avoids dependence on score magnitudes arising from varied norms of data and class weight vectors. We propose several architectural and algorithmic variants of MMPerc, derive associated loss functions and mistake bounds for both linearly separable and non-separable data, and analyze key design considerations, including bias, margin threshold selection, and training modes. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that MMPerc classifiers typically outperform the standard Perceptron, as well as classic baselines such as Support Vector Machines and Ridge classifiers. Owing to their simplicity, minimalistic design, and computational efficiency, MMPerc classifiers are promising candidates for conventional machine learning tasks, linear evaluation of Deep Neural Networks, integration with Hyperdimensional Computing / Vector Symbolic Architecture representations, and deployment in resource-constrained applications.

</details>


### [79] [Extending TotalSegmentator: Predicting Patient and Acquisition Characteristics from CT and MR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29348)
*Jakob Wasserthal,Joshy Cyriac,Michael Bach,Kimia Mozahheb Yousefi,Minh-Son To,Máté Sik,Cédric Hémon,Thomas Weikert,Martin Segeroth*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了基于3D ResNet-10的快速开源模型，用于从CT和MR图像中预测患者特征（体重、身高、年龄、性别）及采集参数，内部测试集上表现良好，外部CT验证和推理速度均具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者细节和采集元数据对于临床决策、图像质量控制和自动化研究流程至关重要，但影像归档中这些信息可能缺失或不可靠，因此需要直接从图像中预测这些特征。

Method: 分别训练CT和MR的3D ResNet-10集成模型，CT模型使用57,291例、MR模型使用43,200例临床检查，预测体重、身高、年龄、性别、对比剂存在、椎体覆盖和噪声，CT额外预测扫描仪厂商、管电压、管电流、卷积核和注射后时间，MR预测序列类别。在内部CT（n=501）、MR（n=636）和外部CT（n=54）上评估，并与分割衍生的XGBoost基线比较。

Result: 内部CT MAE体重3.90 kg、身高3.68 cm、年龄4.42年，性别F1=0.990；MR相应MAE为4.34 kg、4.62 cm、7.13年，F1=0.970。CNN在所有四个核心目标上优于基线（调整后P≤0.042）。CT对比剂F1=0.963，MR序列F1=0.953，MR对比剂F1=0.823。外部CT MAE体重4.45 kg、身高4.05 cm、年龄5.17年，性别F1=0.971。CPU推理CT需20秒，MR需12秒。

Conclusion: 每个模态的一个3D多任务模型能够快速从异质性CT和MR检查中恢复患者和采集特征，模型可在TotalSegmentator中获取。

Abstract: Background: Patient details and acquisition metadata are important for clinical decisions, image quality control, and automated research pipelines, but may be missing or unreliable in imaging archives.
  Purpose: To develop and evaluate a fast open-source model that predicts patient and acquisition characteristics directly from CT and MR images.
  Materials and Methods: Separate 3D ResNet-10 ensembles for CT and MR were trained on 57,291 and 43,200 clinical examinations acquired from 2011 to 2025. Both predicted weight, height, age, sex, contrast presence, vertebral coverage, and image noise. The CT model additionally predicted scanner manufacturer, tube voltage, tube current, convolution kernel, and post-injection time; the MR model predicted sequence class. Performance was evaluated on internal CT (n=501) and MR (n=636) test sets and an external CT dataset (n=54).
  Results: Internal CT MAEs were 3.90 kg, 3.68 cm, and 4.42 years for weight, height, and age, with sex F1=0.990; corresponding MR results were 4.34 kg, 4.62 cm, 7.13 years, and F1=0.970. The CNN outperformed a segmentation-derived XGBoost baseline for all four core targets in both modalities (adjusted P<=.042). F1 scores were 0.963 for CT contrast, 0.953 for MR sequence, and 0.823 for MR contrast. External CT MAEs were 4.45 kg, 4.05 cm, and 5.17 years, with sex F1=0.971. CPU inference required 20 seconds for CT and 12 seconds for MR.
  Conclusion: One 3D multitask model per modality can rapidly recover patient and acquisition characteristics from heterogeneous CT and MR examinations. Models are available in TotalSegmentator: https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator

</details>


### [80] [Selection, Representation, and Execution in Sparse Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30070)
*Abdul Qadir Ibrahim,Martin Burger*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对傅里叶神经算子（FNO），系统区分了表示稀疏、参数稀疏、理论计算量降低和实际运行时间缩短四类目标，并通过实验证明它们互不等价：网格粗化虽降低理论计算量却不减少延迟，参数减少83%仍比稠密基线慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏化研究常假设稀疏表示可同时带来模型更小和推理成本更低，但FNO中参数稀疏并不直接对应计算稀疏，因为底层变换和稠密计算可能保持不变；评估网格改动还会引入额外开销。因此需单独检验每种稀疏化路径的实际收益，以厘清目标的错位。

Method: 设计经验研究框架，将稀疏FNO按表示、参数、理论操作数和实测运行时间四个层面分别定义，并逐一测试从一种稀疏形式过渡到另一种时的效果。具体操作包括：改变执行网格以降低理论计算量，引入校正项恢复精度，以及进行高达83%的参数剪枝。

Result: 执行网格粗化不降低实测延迟；添加校正项虽恢复精度却增加运行时间；即使参数减少83%，在常规执行下仍慢于稠密基线。表明各类稀疏化收益无法自动传导至实际加速。

Conclusion: 提出“有用稀疏”的严格定义：部署的算子必须保持求解精度，且其缩减后的支持结构必须映射到真正更快的执行路径，否则稀疏化只具有存储或理论意义，而无实际运行时收益。

Abstract: Sparse representations are often expected to make models smaller and also reduce inference cost. For Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs), these objectives are not equivalent or do not always align: removing parts of the learned operator can leave the underlying transforms and dense computations unchanged, while changing the grid on which the model is evaluated can introduce overhead of its own. We therefore distinguish sparsity in the representation, in the stored parameters, in the theoretical operation count, and in measured runtime, and present an empirical study of several routes toward sparse FNOs that tests each transition between them separately. Coarsening the execution grid reduces the theoretical cost without reducing measured latency, and adding a correction term recovers accuracy at the cost of making the model slower. Even an 83\% parameter reduction remains slower than the dense baseline under ordinary execution. These results motivate a stricter definition of useful sparsity: the deployed operator must preserve solution accuracy and map its reduced support to a genuinely cheaper execution path.

</details>
