Experiments across multiple datasets, victim models, and large language models show that {\OURS} consistently outperforms strong standalone baselines and simple manually constructed chains. These results suggest that attacker composition is not merely an implementation choice, but a practical optimization target for improving hard-label black-box text attacks.

</details>


### [192] [JITterFlip: Uncovering Fault Attack Surfaces in JIT-Compiled LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29745)
*Tairui Wang,Zhi Zhang,Yansong Gao,Xin Zhang,Qingni Shen,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出JITterFlip，首个针对GPU LLM推理主机端JIT服务控制平面的比特翻转攻击，通过篡改CPU驻留的服务决策而非模型计算，实现乱码输出与正确输出的海绵攻击，且能绕过现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFA大多针对模型参数或直接实现模型计算的代码，需要模型特定知识或仅能导致推理失效；云环境中的JIT编译控制平面尚未被攻击，存在安全盲区。

Method: JITterFlip通过决策引导的故障易感代码分析，在大型JIT编译器栈中定位可利用的CPU驻留分支代码，利用单比特翻转修改服务决策，跨越CPU-GPU边界影响GPU推理结果。

Result: 在文本与多模态LLM上，乱码输出PPL比值达15.45倍至2.48e6倍；海绵攻击延迟放大2.03倍至181.90倍；端到端Rowhammer下PPL放大达7.23e6倍或延迟放大124.97倍，且保持输出精确性，可绕过近期防御。

Conclusion: JITterFlip揭示了JIT服务控制平面是LLM推理的新型攻击面，攻击具有跨模型迁移性和强破坏力，急需针对主机端控制平面的防护措施。

Abstract: LLMs are widely deployed through cloud-hosted inference services, where Just-in-Time (JIT) compilation is used to reduce recurring framework and GPU-launch overhead. JIT serving introduces a host-side control plane that selects compiled artifacts and orchestrates their execution on the GPU. Meanwhile, the shared cloud setting has motivated a growing body of bit-flip attacks (BFAs) against LLM/DNN inference. Most existing BFAs target model parameters or weights and require model-specific knowledge. A smaller body of work reduces this dependency by faulting executable code, yet still corrupts code that directly implements model computation, limiting their attack effect to inference depletion.
  We present JITterFlip, the first BFA targeting the host-side JIT serving control plane of GPU-based LLM inference. By faulting CPU-resident serving decisions rather than model computation, JITterFlip enables both gibberish output generation and a correct-output sponge attack. To identify exploitable targets in a large JIT compiler stack, JITterFlip develops a decision-guided fault-vulnerable code analysis.
  Across four text and multimodal LLM workloads, the identified vulnerable code faults exhibit cross-model transferability, produce gibberish outputs with PPL ratios of $15.45\times$ to $2.48{\times}10^{6}\times$, and demonstrate correct-output sponge attacks with latency amplification of $2.03\times$ to $181.90\times$. JITterFlip also bypasses recent BFA defenses for LLMs while retaining both attack effects. Last, we demonstrate end-to-end Rowhammer attacks across four LLMs: a single bit flip in CPU-resident branch code propagates across the CPU-GPU boundary to disrupt GPU-executed inference without direct access to GPU memory, reaching up to $7.23{\times}10^{6}\times$ PPL amplification or $124.97\times$ latency amplification while preserving the exact generated output.

</details>


### [193] [Building the Truman Show: A TrustZone-Based Framework for Lightweight Out-of-band Kernel Security Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29758)
*Zhenling Duan,Pan Dong,Renshuang Jiang,Xiaoxiang Fang,Bao Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出LOOM，一种基于ARM TrustZone的轻量级带外操作系统监控架构，通过硬件隔离、语义重构和双阶段危害预防机制，实现对内核攻击的高效检测与缓解，且在飞腾D2000平台上开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内核内防护机制与内核同特权级，共享攻击面，易被攻破；隔离方案虽增强安全但存在语义鸿沟，影响系统可见性和威胁检测时效。

Method: 利用TrustZone硬件隔离建立独立于内核的可信监控环境；在安全世界设计轻量级语义重构机制，选择性捕获进程控制块和内核模块等关键对象状态；引入结合原子内存保护与中断驱动自适应代理的双阶段危害预防机制；使用地址转换缓存优化重复访问；构建平台、功能与辅助三层协同架构。

Result: 在飞腾D2000平台上的原型实现表明，LOOM开销可忽略不计，同时保持强监控能力；基于CVE案例的安全能力分析显示其能检测和缓解多种内核攻击。

Conclusion: LOOM有效平衡了隔离安全性与系统可见性，为操作系统内核监控提供了一种安全高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing number of vulnerabilities in operating systems, together with sophisticated kernel-level threats (e.g., rootkits), has weakened the effectiveness of traditional in-kernel protection mechanisms. Since these defenses operate at the same privilege level as the kernel, they share the same attack surface and can be bypassed once the kernel is compromised. Isolation-based security approaches provide stronger protection by separating security logic from the kernel, but strict isolation often introduces semantic gaps that limit system visibility and hinder timely threat detection. In this paper, we present LOOM, a lightweight out-of-band operating system monitoring architecture built on ARM TrustZone. By leveraging TrustZone's hardware-enforced isolation, LOOM establishes a tamper-resistant monitoring environment independent of the kernel. To bridge the semantic gap, we design a lightweight semantic reconstruction mechanism in the Secure World. It selectively captures the states and behavioral patterns of critical kernel objects, such as process control blocks and kernel modules. Additionally, LOOM introduces a dual-stage hazard prevention mechanism that combines atomic memory protection with an interrupt-driven adaptive agent to detect and mitigate kernel rootkit activities. An address translation cache is further incorporated to optimize repeated address access and reduce monitoring overhead. Overall, we develop a multi-layered collaborative architecture with platform, functional, and auxiliary layers for secure and efficient kernel monitoring. A prototype of LOOM has been implemented on the Phytium D2000 platform. Experimental results indicate that LOOM incurs negligible overhead while maintaining a strong monitoring capability. Furthermore, a security capability analysis based on CVE cases demonstrates that LOOM can detect and mitigate various kernel attacks.

</details>


### [194] [HSMLog: Small Language Model-Assisted Hardware Security Module Log Anomaly Detection with Behavioral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29773)
*Chia-Hsuan Wu,Dar-Hsin Dustin Wu,Rui Fang,Yi-Ting Lee,Chia-Chih Lin,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出HSMLog，一种两阶段HSM日志异常检测框架，结合小语言模型与检索增强的行为分析，在工业日志上实现高精度异常告警与事件分诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSM日志异常源于事件序列、密钥、对象状态、会话及时间模式之间的复杂关系，而非孤立事件；现有方法将检测与HSM特有的证据验证和报告分离，缺乏针对性。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段1用小语言模型从滑动窗口的HSM结构化事件中识别候选告警，并结合HSM操作规则进行策略引导评估；阶段2检索告警窗口之前的策略和历史可疑密钥记录，结合相关日志上下文，支持保守的候选复核与事件分析。

Result: 在真实工业HSM背景日志（含与工业伙伴共同定义的异常场景）上，精确率98.97%，召回率96.00%，异常事件覆盖率98.66%，F1分数97.46%。

Conclusion: HSMLog在所研究环境中有效实现异常告警和事件分诊，展示了检索增强行为分析对HSM日志异常检测的价值。

Abstract: Hardware Security Module (HSM) logs capture security-critical behavior, but anomalies emerge from relationships across event sequences, keys, object states, sessions, and temporal patterns rather than isolated events. Existing methods separate detection from HSM-specific evidence validation and reporting. In this paper, we present HSMLog, a two-stage framework for HSM log anomaly detection with retrieval-grounded behavioral analysis. In Stage 1, a small language model (SLM) identifies candidate alerts from sliding windows of structured HSM events and performs policy-guided assessment using HSM-specific operational rules. In Stage 2, retrieved policies and historical suspicious-key records strictly predating the alert window, together with candidate-related log context, support conservative candidate review and incident analysis. Evaluated on real industrial HSM background logs augmented with anomaly scenarios co-defined with industrial partners, HSMLog achieves 98.97% precision, 96.00% recall, 98.66% anomalous-event coverage, and a 97.46% F1 score, demonstrating effective anomaly alerting and incident triage in the studied setting.

</details>


### [195] [Influence Is Not Authority: When Causal Guardrail Signals Make Legitimate Tool Use Look Like an Attack in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29942)
*Tanzim Ahad,Ismail Hossain,Md Jahangir Alam,Sai Puppala,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 当前基于影响的护栏无法可靠区分用户授权操作与恶意未授权操作，即使动作和效果相同，仅数据来源不同也会引发误判，导致效用下降和延迟增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏在外部工具信息场景下，对合法授权操作和恶意未授权操作的区分存在根本性缺陷，这会导致良性操作被不必要地干预，降低系统实用性和响应速度。

Method: 通过授权等价审计，对24个基准案例衍生出96个条件，在保持授权状态、具体动作和预期效果固定的前提下，仅改变所需值的来源（用户或工具结果），并使用Llama和Gemma评分器及语义/影子护栏进行架构级评估。

Result: 良性数据源变化导致的平均分数偏移大于实际授权变化；无语义监控时攻击成功率0%、效用28%，有监控时分别为16%和60%；影子护栏放行所有良性运行，但未授权运行自动通过率57.5%，高于授权运行的29.2%。

Conclusion: 因果信号反映动作形成过程但无法可靠编码授权状态，参考构建和路由机制对安全决策至关重要，当前护栏设计需重构以解决此局限性。

Abstract: The key limitation of current state-of-the-art influence-based guardrails is that they do not reliably distinguish a legitimate, user-authorized action from a malicious, unauthorized action when both rely on external tool information. This ambiguity can cause benign actions to trigger unnecessary verification and intervention, reducing utility and adding latency. We expose this limitation through an authorization-equivalence audit of 96 conditions derived from 24 base cases. Within matched source comparisons, we hold authorization, the exact committed action, and its intended effect fixed, changing only whether a required value comes from the user or a legitimate tool result. Although the action remains unchanged, this harmless relocation shifts the causal signal toward the attack region in all 24 cases under both Llama and Gemma scorers. Matched unauthorized controls show that the signal remains attack-sensitive, yet the benign relocation produces a larger average score shift than the actual change in authorization. Architecture-level evaluation shows how this mismatch propagates through guardrail designs. With a semantic monitor, attack success is 0% and utility is 28%, compared with 16% and 60% without it. A shadow-based guardrail allows every tested harmless run, yet does not reject matched unauthorized actions more often overall: 57.5% of unauthorized runs pass automatically before reaching the later security check, compared with 29.2% of authorized runs. These results show that the studied causal signal reveals what shaped an action without reliably encoding whether the action was authorized, and that reference construction and routing are integral to the effective security decision.

</details>


### [196] [A New Algebraic Algorithm for LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29977)
*Luca Campa,Massimo Fumiani,Arnab Roy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种结合线性代数与S-多项式（Gröbner基）的新代数算法求解Search-LWE，实现多项式级别的复杂度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Gröbner基方法依赖半正则性假设和正则度复杂度界，缺乏直接且更优的复杂度分析。

Method: 融合线性代数技术和Gröbner基计算中的S-多项式方法，并给出直接复杂度分析以避免半正则性假设。

Result: 相较于现有Gröbner基方法，算法在求解Search-LWE时获得多项式复杂度改进。

Conclusion: 新算法为Search-LWE提供了更高效的代数解法，且复杂度分析更严格、适用性更广。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem, introduced by Regev in 2005, is central to modern cryptography and post-quantum security. The algorithms to solve the search version of the problem, Search-LWE, can be broadly categorised into algebraic, combinatorial and lattice-based.
  In this work we propose a new algebraic algorithm for the Search-LWE problem. At a high level, the algorithm combines linear-algebraic techniques with S-polynomial-based methods from Groebner basis computation. We provide a direct complexity analysis of our algorithm, avoiding semi-regularity assumptions and complexity bounds derived from the degree of regularity. Our algorithm achieves a polynomial improvement in complexity over prior results that use Groebner basis methods to solve Search-LWE.

</details>


### [197] [Breaking Ambient Trust: In-Network Per-Process Access Control Against Lateral Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29979)
*Osama Bajaber,Bo Ji,Peng Gao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: NetZone提出了一种网络内访问控制机制，通过为每个用户进程绑定轻量级AccessScope并随流量传播，实现跨主机的进程级权限约束，有效防御APT横向移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络防御无法在进程级别跨网络跟踪用户移动，而是对主机内所有进程授予环境信任，导致主机被攻陷后恶意进程继承受害者权限，扩大攻击者访问范围并促进横向移动。

Method: 引入AccessScope抽象，编码用户身份可访问的主机集合并嵌入进程出站流量中；在数据平面通过可编程交换机与eBPF协同设计，结合网络内优化和轻量级持久化技术，实现线速检测和权限持续绑定。

Result: 广泛评估表明，NetZone能有效防御复杂攻击场景，且不引入明显开销，支持高流量线速处理并保持极低延迟。

Conclusion: NetZone通过进程级、跨主机的访问控制填补了现有防御空白，证明了网络内动态权限验证在抵御高级持续性威胁中的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: Enterprise networks remain vulnerable to Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs), where adversaries gain an initial foothold and move laterally across the network, accumulating access permissions hop by hop to reach critical targets. Existing network defenses cannot track user movement at the process level across the network; instead, they grant ambient trust to all processes within a host. As a result, once a host is compromised, malicious processes inherit the victim's permissions, thereby expanding the attacker's access scope and enabling further lateral movement. To address this gap, we present NetZone, an in-network access control that confines each user process to a fixed access scope that persists as the user moves across the network. NetZone introduces a new abstraction, called AccessScope, which represents a lightweight access capability bound to the user's processes. Each AccessScope encodes the set of hosts a user identity is authorized to access and is embedded in the process's outgoing network traffic for validation before reaching its destination. As users pivot across hosts, AccessScope propagates with their traffic, rebinds to the receiving process, and persists across hosts. This ensures that regardless of network location, the user's processes are consistently governed by their bound AccessScope and their access permissions remain unchanged. To handle the high volume of network traffic generated by processes, we develop a data-plane co-design that integrates programmable switches with eBPF. NetZone employs a set of in-network optimizations and lightweight AccessScope persistence techniques to inspect the embedded AccessScope on the fly, enabling line-rate processing of high traffic volumes with negligible latency overhead. Our extensive evaluations show that NetZone can effectively defend against sophisticated attack scenarios without introducing noticeable overhead.

</details>


### [198] [ActReal: System-Level Mobile Agents Challenge Mobile Automation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30038)
*Mingshuo Wang,Hanqing Guo,Huining Li,Yuliang Fu,Jing Xu,Chenhan Xu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出ActReal框架，利用系统级代理同时控制触摸和IMU信号，绕过现有自动化检测，攻击成功率77.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测依赖触摸轨迹、时间及触摸-IMU耦合，但系统级代理可联合伪造时间对齐的触摸和六轴IMU信号，暴露防御漏洞。

Method: ActReal将语义动作转换为任务有效的触摸与IMU事件，采用真实轨迹适配和物理引导的IMU生成。

Result: 平均事件级攻击成功率77.5%，即使检测器联合观察触摸和IMU，成功率仍达71.1%。

Conclusion: 系统级移动代理可有效规避基于物理信号的自动化检测，需加强此类高级攻击的防御。

Abstract: System-level mobile agents are evolving from fixed scripts into adaptive systems that continuously observe interfaces, reason, and adjust their actions, allowing automated attacks to navigate dynamic UIs and complete complex tasks. Existing applications detect automation using touch trajectories, action timing, and the physical coupling between touch and inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals. However, a privileged system-level agent executor can control both touchscreen input and application-visible sensor delivery, enabling it to jointly generate time-aligned touch and six-axis IMU signals and evade these defenses. We present ActReal, a physical-action attack framework for system-level mobile agents. ActReal converts semantic agent actions into task-valid touch and IMU events using genuine-trajectory adaptation and physics-guided IMU generation. ActReal achieves a mean event-level attack success rate of 77.5\%; even when detectors jointly observe touch and IMU, its attack success rate remains 71.1\%.

</details>


### [199] [Reachability-Based Capability Confinement for LLM Agents under Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30041)
*Wujie Xiong,Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Weidong Shi,Lei Xu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: SkillGuard是一个基于harness的强制执行层，通过将不可信数据进入上下文视为污染，并利用技能影响图和可控性签名动态限制代理后续能力，在不增加模型调用开销的情况下显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要分类不可信内容或授权操作，未能解决不可信数据进入状态后代理未来权限应如何变化的问题，导致攻击者可通过外部技能输出影响后续特权操作。

Method: SkillGuard采用内联参考监视器，构建技能影响图表示安全相关转换，通过可控性签名指定参数控制范围，并在污染发生后使用二元、分数或分数流策略计算加权能力限制，无需额外语言模型推理。

Result: 在AgentDojo四个套件上，SkillGuard在Tool Knowledge攻击下将攻击成功率降至零（三个套件）并显著降低Slack上的成功率；在组合攻击中优于或匹配最强基线，同时保持较高良性效用，且无额外模型调用或token开销。

Conclusion: SkillGuard提供了一种有效的harness级防御机制，通过污染感知的能力动态限制，在保障安全的同时维持实用性能，为代理安全提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents place outputs from external skills into their execution context, allowing attacker-controlled data to influence later privileged actions. Existing defenses mainly classify untrusted content or authorize proposed operations. They do not directly address how an agent's future authority should change once untrusted data enters its state. We present SkillGuard, a harness-level enforcement layer that treats this event as contamination and restricts future capabilities to disconnect the resulting state from deployer-defined forbidden states. Given sound skill summaries and policies, SkillGuard represents security-relevant transitions with a Skill Impact Graph, specifies admissible control over skill parameters via steerability signatures, and mediates invocations with an inline reference monitor. Following contamination, it computes weighted capability restrictions using binary, fractional, or fractional-flow strategies without auxiliary language-model inference. We evaluate SkillGuard on four AgentDojo suites with two backend LLMs, Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama3.3-70B, against an LLM-only No Defense baseline and three defenses at different system layers: Spotlighting, CaMeL, and AttriGuard. We construct a compositional attack benchmark in which each attack combines observations individually insufficient to induce target violation and evaluate the same baselines on it. Under AgentDojo's Tool Knowledge attacks, SkillGuard eliminates attack success on three of four suites for both backends and reduces it to 4.8% and 14.3% on Slack. Against compositional attacks, it outperforms every baseline on Llama and matches the strongest baseline on Gemini at higher benign utility. Fractional-flow restriction preserves substantially more capabilities than binary restriction at the same attack success rate. Across both settings, SkillGuard adds no model calls or token overhead.

</details>


### [200] [Zero-Knowledge Predicate Proofs Between AI Agents: A Measured, Cross-Protocol Gateway and the Source-Integrity Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30083)
*Ashok Subbabhatta Gopalakrishna*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出并实现了Zero-Knowledge Proof Gateway，在MCP和Agent2Agent协议上以零知识证明替代原始数据交换，实现代理间可验证的数据最小化，并融合可信执行环境认证解决证明与记录系统绑定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理间信任机制非原始数据过度共享即不可验证的自然语言声明，后者易受提示注入攻击；现有加密策略方案停留在模拟阶段，且缺乏与记录系统的绑定。

Method: 设计ZKP Gateway，代理间交换治理谓词的零知识证明而非数据本身；在MCP和Agent2Agent协议上实现自定义证明槽位；融合enclave attestation与Bulletproofs证明，实现双向绑定到特定二进制与记录值。

Result: 32位阈值谓词证明6.2毫秒、验证1.0毫秒，608字节Bulletproofs证明在单vCPU运行；通过11项对抗实验和19项协议检查；部署至Kubernetes并验证网络隔离；零售订单限额案例证明GDPR数据最小化原则可执行。

Conclusion: 证明零知识证明在代理间可实际部署并满足性能与安全要求，同时解决证明与系统记录绑定的开放问题，为AI平台提供合规且抗注入的信任基础设施。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI platforms move quickly from staging to production, but the way agents establish trust remains rudimentary: an agent either transmits raw data to a peer or accepts that peer's natural-language self-report that a value complies with policy. The first over-shares; the second is unverifiable and is exactly the channel prompt injection attacks. Prevailing responses emphasise identity, visibility, and post-hoc detection, and recent proposals for cryptographically enforced agent policy have been evaluated in simulation rather than execution. We take provable data minimisation between agents from proposal to running system. In our Zero-Knowledge Proof Gateway, agents exchange proofs of governance-defined predicates over private data rather than the data itself, so exposure is prevented by design rather than detected afterwards; because no interoperability protocol can carry such a proof, we propose a slot and implement it on both MCP and Agent2Agent from one endpoint. A 32-bit threshold predicate proves in 6.2 ms and verifies in 1.0 ms with a 608-byte Bulletproofs proof on one commodity vCPU; eleven adversarial experiments and nineteen protocol checks pass; and the system is deployed to Kubernetes with empirically verified network isolation. Our case study proves a retail client order is within its limit without revealing the amount, instantiating the GDPR data-minimisation principle as an enforced technical measure of the kind EU law now names explicitly. We then address the limitation no comparable work resolves: a predicate proof binds a statement to a committed value, never to the system of record. We give a construction fusing an enclave attestation with the proof in both directions, so verifying one artifact certifies jointly that the predicate holds and that the value was read by a specific measured binary, and test it against a mock authority.

</details>


### [201] [A Simple Transformer Pipeline for Full-Key Side-Channel Attacks on Uncropped Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30105)
*Jimmy Gammell,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一个开源Transformer实现，用于未裁剪轨迹的全密钥侧信道攻击，仅调整输入输出层，在多个数据集上达到竞争性能且训练高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习侧信道分析多聚焦单字节目标和手动裁剪轨迹，可能丢失可利用泄漏；缺乏针对未裁剪轨迹的全密钥攻击的简单Transformer基线。

Method: 采用标准Transformer编码器作为主干，仅修改输入和输出层以适应侧信道设置，并公开实现、训练配方和预训练权重。

Result: 在未裁剪的ASCADv1f、ASCADv1r和CHES-CTF-2018上达到与先前报道相当的性能，显存小于10GB，单张A6000训练时间最多3.34小时。

Conclusion: 证明了简单Transformer基线在未裁剪全密钥攻击中的有效性，为后续研究提供了开源基准。

Abstract: Deep learning-based side-channel analysis has historically focused on single-byte targets and manually cropped traces, which risks discarding exploitable leakage. While recent work has proposed specialized architectures and resampling techniques to address this gap, the literature lacks a simple transformer baseline for simultaneous full-key attacks on uncropped traces. We present an open-source transformer implementation for uncropped full-key attacks which uses the standard transformer encoder backbone, adapting only the input and output layers to the side-channel setting. We release our implementation, training recipes, and pretrained weights for uncropped ASCADv1f, ASCADv1r, and CHES-CTF-2018 which achieve performance competitive with previously-reported results, while using less than 10GB of VRAM and requiring at most 3.34 hours of training on a single NVIDIA A6000.

</details>


### [202] [RL-FAT: Reinforcement Learning for Fair Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29247)
*Tejaswini Medi,Levan Mikeladze,Margret Keuper*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RL-FAT，一种基于强化学习的公平对抗训练框架，通过策略梯度与公平强调损失，在保持整体鲁棒精度的同时显著降低类别间鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对抗训练虽提升平均鲁棒精度，但掩盖了类别间鲁棒性严重不均的问题，部分类别在攻击下仍高度脆弱，影响视觉任务中跨类别的可靠鲁棒性。

Method: 将模型预测分布视为策略，结合基于正确性的预测奖励与类别级价值估计计算类别优势，用于策略梯度优化；同时引入公平强调对抗损失，对高对抗损失的类别施加更强训练压力，以自适应缓解类别间鲁棒性差异。

Result: 实验表明，相比标准对抗训练基线，RL-FAT在保持有竞争力的鲁棒精度的同时，显著降低了类别间鲁棒性不均衡。

Conclusion: RL-FAT通过强化驱动的自适应与公平强调正则化，实现了鲁棒精度与类别间公平性的兼顾，为对抗训练中的公平性问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks remain highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, and adversarial training (AT) has become a widely used approach for improving robustness. However, improvements in average robust accuracy often mask substantial class-wise disparities: while some classes become more robust, others may remain disproportionately vulnerable under attack. This imbalance raises an important adversarial fairness concern, particularly in vision tasks where reliable robustness is expected across all categories. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{RL-FAT}, a reinforcement-learning-inspired fair adversarial training framework that uses policy-gradient based feedback from adversarial predictions. RL-FAT interprets the prediction distribution as a policy and combines correctness-based prediction rewards with class-wise value estimates to compute class-specific advantages for policy-gradient optimization. This enables the model to adaptively focus on class-wise misclassification. Furthermore, we introduce a fairness-emphasis adversarial loss that assigns stronger training pressure to classes with high adversarial loss, thereby mitigating class-wise robustness disparity. By combining reinforcement-driven adaptation with fairness-emphasis regularization, RL-FAT improves adversarial robustness while promoting a more balanced robustness distribution across classes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive robust accuracy and substantially reduces class-wise robustness imbalance compared with standard adversarial training baselines.

</details>


### [203] [FRAC-MAS: A Safe and Explainable Multi-Agent System for Fracture Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28662)
*Hardik Iyer,Tirath Bhathawala,Mihir Panchal,Ying-Jung Chen,Kiran Bhowmick,Pankaj Sonawane,Meera Narvekar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: FRAC-MAS是一个将四种视觉模型集成与共形预测相结合的代理AI系统，用于骨折检测，通过多代理工作流提供可解释、安全的诊断，并显著提升报告可理解性和临床分诊安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在骨折检测中性能高但缺乏可解释性，限制临床采用；现有系统未能兼顾统计严谨性、独立验证和患者友好沟通，亟需可审计的人机协同决策框架。

Method: 采用四模型堆叠集成结合共形预测生成差分诊断，多代理流程执行独立验证、检索临床指南并生成患者友好报告；通过消融研究比较单代理与多代理评论机制，并进行患者偏好测试评估报告质量。

Result: 多代理评论将86.6%病例归为高置信自确认组，不确定案例升级处理，优于单代理基线；患者偏好研究显示FRAC-MAS报告相比Llama、MedGemma和Gemini更易理解。

Conclusion: 多代理评论与共形保证结合能提升放射学分诊安全性并保持临床监督，验证了协作代理架构在医疗安全关键场景中作为可审计人机决策支持系统的潜力。

Abstract: Fracture detection and its clinical interpretability see notable improvements when deep vision models are integrated with agentic AI architectures. While deep learning models achieve high diagnostic performance, their black-box nature limits clinical adoption. We propose FRAC-MAS, an agentic AI system for automated, explainable, and safe bone fracture detection. The framework combines a stacked ensemble of four vision models with conformal prediction to produce statistically grounded differential diagnoses, while a multi-agent workflow performs independent verification, retrieves clinical guidelines, and generates patient-friendly reports. A pipeline-depth ablation study confirms that our multi-agent critic triages 86.6% of cases into a high-confidence auto-confirmed cohort while escalating uncertain cases, outperforming a single-agent baseline. Patient preference studies against Llama, MedGemma, and Gemini further demonstrate significantly more comprehensible clinical reports. These results suggest that integrating multi-agent critics with conformal guarantees enables safer radiology triage while preserving clinician oversight. More broadly, FRAC-MAS demonstrates how cooperative agentic architectures can serve as auditable, human-in-the-loop decision support systems for safety-critical healthcare. Our code is available at https://github.com/hardik1712/FRAC-MAS, and the website is available at https://frac-mas.vercel.app.

</details>


### [204] [Drishti: AI-Led Human-Directed Vulnerability Auditing for 5G Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30112)
*Sriram Ramachandran,Levente Csikor,Dinil Mon Divakaran*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Drishti框架，将AI与人工结合用于开源漏洞审计，在5G核心网软件中发现三个关键漏洞，包括一个已修复的CVE和两个新问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源漏洞候选生成已不再稀缺，但验证与影响评估成为瓶颈，尤其在5G核心网等关键基础设施软件中，验证涉及验证、可达性、影响和修复完整性四类成本。

Method: Drishti包含四个组件：反模式目录用于验证、关键路径分类用于可达性、同心验证用于影响评估、补丁审查用于修复完整性，形成AI主导、人工引导的审计流程。

Result: 在Open5GS和free5GC审计中发现：Open5GS NRF多部分解析器的预认证NULL解引用（已修复并申请CVE）；free5GC NGAP解码器的ASN.1-PER内存放大（2字节输入6.2秒OOM杀死AMF）；CVE-2025-69248的修复补丁存在死代码缺陷。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Candidate generation for open-source vulnerabilities is no longer scarce. AI-assisted code review now produces defect candidates cheaply, and industry programs pair them with expert human triage. The remaining scarcity is validation and impact assessment, and the gap is largest in critical-infrastructure software like 5G cores. Here, validation has four costs: verification, reachability, impact, and fix-completeness. We present Drishti, an AI-led human-directed vulnerability audit framework with four components, one per cost: (i) an anti-pattern catalog for verification, (ii) critical-path triage for reachability, (iii) concentric validation for impact, and (iv) patch-review for fix-completeness. Across audits of Open5GS and free5GC, Drishti produced three findings. The first is a pre-authentication NULL-dereference in the Open5GS NRF multipart parser, fixed upstream with a CVE requested. The second is an ASN.1-PER memory amplification in the free5GC NGAP decoder. A 2-byte input from a rogue gNodeB OOM-kills the AMF in 6.2 seconds. The third is a defective patch on CVE-2025-69248 whose defense-in-depth check is dead code before authentication.

</details>


### [205] [Balancing Privacy, Utility, and Safety in LLM Alignment through Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30141)
*Dishu Yang,Jingjing Liu,Jize Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出隐私压力偏好混合（P3M）数据组合协议，通过向DPO中加入合成隐私偏好对，在不修改目标函数或引入正式隐私机制的情况下，降低了基于金丝雀的记忆化信号和成员推断攻击性能，但该效果并非在所有数据源中一致，且不能作为正式隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化广泛应用于大语言模型与人类偏好对齐，但偏好数据的构成可能影响与隐私相关的记忆化。现有研究缺乏对数据组合是否能在不改变优化目标或添加正式隐私机制的条件下降低记忆化信号的系统探讨。

Method: 提出P3M协议，固定帮助性和无害性偏好数据，变化隐私偏好数据的比例（0.5、1.0、2.0），使用Gemma 3 270M-IT（5个随机种子）和4位量化的Gemma 2 2B-IT（3个种子）进行DPO训练，评估金丝雀后缀对数似然和成员推断攻击指标（AUROC、AUPRC）。

Result: 隐私偏好混合降低了两种模型设置下的平均金丝雀后缀对数似然，在2B混合源评估中，隐私感知配置的AUROC为0.596-0.629，AUPRC为0.541-0.575，而基线分别为0.804和0.790；但成员可区分性降低不统一，隐私比例与无害性准确率关系因模型而异，帮助性准确率基本稳定。

Conclusion: P3M应视为一种轻量级实证协议，用于考察隐私-效用-安全之间的权衡，而非正式隐私保证或针对提取攻击的防御手段；其有效性受数据源和模型设置影响，需要谨慎解释。

Abstract: Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models with human preferences, but preference-data composition may also influence privacy-relevant memorization. We examine whether adding synthetic privacy-preference pairs to Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is associated with lower canary-based memorization signals without modifying the objective or introducing a formal privacy mechanism. We propose Privacy-Pressure Preference Mixing (P3M), a data-composition protocol that varies the amount of privacy-preference data while keeping helpfulness and harmlessness preference data fixed. We evaluate a non-privacy Baseline and privacy-mixing ratios of 0.5, 1.0, and 2.0 using Gemma 3 270M-IT across five random seeds and validate the same four conditions using 4-bit-quantized Gemma 2 2B-IT across three seeds. Overall, under the tested conditions, privacy-preference mixing is associated with lower mean canary suffix log-likelihood proxy values across both model settings and lower aggregate membership-inference attack performance relative to the Baseline in the mixed-source 2B evaluation. Specifically, across the privacy-aware 2B configurations, the mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) ranges from 0.596 to 0.629, and the mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) ranges from 0.541 to 0.575, compared with 0.804 and 0.790, respectively, for the Baseline. However, the reduction in membership distinguishability does not hold uniformly across data sources. Moreover, the relationship between the privacy ratio and harmlessness preference accuracy varies by model setting, whereas helpfulness preference accuracy remains broadly stable. These findings suggest that P3M should be viewed as a lightweight empirical protocol for examining privacy-utility-safety trade-offs rather than as a formal privacy guarantee or a defense against extraction attacks.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond the Answer Key: Robustness Evaluation of Large Language Models for Step-Level Mathematical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28725)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一个线性方程基准，用于评估LLM作为验证器时的鲁棒性，发现模型对规范解表现好但对等价扰动解表现差，错误定位和误拒率问题显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学评估主要关注最终答案准确性，忽略了模型作为验证器时对非规范但有效解题轨迹的判断能力，导致无法全面反映模型的过程验证鲁棒性。

Method: 设计了一个受控的线性方程基准，要求模型判断最终答案正确性、逐步轨迹正确性以及首个错误步骤，并用多种开源LLM在规范与扰动轨迹上进行评估，同时测试SFT、蒸馏和测试时计算的影响。

Result: 基座模型在规范轨迹上表现良好，但在扰动轨迹上性能显著下降，有效扰动轨迹的误拒率达75.6-85.3%；微调和蒸馏只能部分改善，且效果因模型而异，可能牺牲规范性能。

Conclusion: 过程级验证的鲁棒性仍是挑战，评估者鲁棒性应与求解准确性分开衡量，即使在简单代数领域且具有精确ground truth的情况下也是如此。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as graders, verifiers, and process auditors, but most mathematical evaluations still emphasize final-answer accuracy. This can obscure whether a model can verify a non-canonical but valid solution trace. We introduce a controlled linear-equation benchmark for evaluating LLMs in the evaluator role. Each instance asks the model to judge final-answer correctness, step-level trace correctness, and the first incorrect step. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art open LLMs reveals a significant robustness gap: models that accurately evaluate canonical solutions often fail when presented with perturbed but logically equivalent variants. Across GPT-OSS 20B, Qwen3-14B, and Phi-4-Reasoning, base models perform well on canonical traces but degrade substantially on perturbed traces, especially for error localization. On valid perturbed traces, base-model false-rejection rates reach 75.6-85.3%, showing strong sensitivity to canonical solution form. Supervised fine-tuning, distillation, and test-time compute improve robustness in some settings, but gains are model dependent and can trade off against canonical performance. The results show that reliable process-level verification remains challenging, and evaluator robustness should be measured separately from solver accuracy, even in a simple algebraic domain with exact ground truth.

</details>


### [207] [A Spectral Identifiability Threshold for Dissipative Rate Recovery from Truncated Liouvillian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29302)
*Yujun Ji,Somyajit Chakraborty*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 研究了六比特Lindblad模型中，仅保留最慢的非稳态谱模式时，振幅阻尼和退相干速率是否可恢复；发现布居模式不含退相干信息，均匀退相干可识别所需模式数有下界D=2^n，n=4,5,6时达到该下界，n=3时高于下界，且最小二乘在n=6时误差远低于表格学习方法，但该优势在谱扰动或横向场破坏对易结构时减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的耗散过程可能产生重叠的动力学特征，Liouvillian谱总结松弛行为，但不清楚需要多少谱信息才能区分底层耗散速率，本研究旨在量化保留模式数与耗散参数可恢复性之间的关系。

Method: 对六量子比特Lindblad模型（谱可解析推导）进行理论分析，仅保留最慢的非稳态模式，测试不同保留数量下各耗散速率的可恢复性；比较最小二乘与四种表格学习方法，并加入谱扰动和横向场破坏对易结构的鲁棒性测试。

Result: 布居模式不含退相干信息导致均匀退相干可识别的模式数下界为D=2^n；n=4,5,6时恢复阈值达到此下界，n=3时高于下界；n=6时最小二乘平均联合绝对误差约10^-9，远优于表格方法的4.355×10^-4；但谱扰动或横向场破坏时优势减弱。

Conclusion: 保留谱信息的数量和结构可独立于估计器决定耗散参数是否可恢复；结论适用于无噪声模拟器谱而非测量谱。

Abstract: Open quantum systems lose energy and phase coherence through different dissipative processes, but these processes can produce overlapping dynamical signatures. The Liouvillian spectrum summarizes how such a system relaxes, yet it is not obvious how much of that spectrum is needed to distinguish the underlying dissipation rates. We study this question for amplitude damping and dephasing in a six-qubit Lindblad model whose spectrum can be derived analytically. We retain only the slowest non-steady spectral modes and ask how many are required before each dissipative rate becomes recoverable. We show that population modes contain no dephasing information, which creates a lower bound of D = 2^n retained modes for uniform dephasing identifiability in the relevant rate regime. The measured recovery threshold reaches this bound at n = 4,5,6, while n = 3 remains above it. At n = 6, least squares achieves a mean joint absolute error of order 10^-9, compared with 4.355 x 10^-4 for four tabular learning methods. Robustness tests show that this advantage weakens when the spectra are perturbed and when a transverse field breaks the commuting structure. These results show that the amount and structure of retained spectral information can determine whether dissipative parameters are recoverable, independently of the estimator used. The present conclusions apply to noise-free simulator spectra rather than measurement-derived spectra.

</details>


### [208] [Understanding Stage-Wise Utility-Risk Trade-offs in LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30177)
*Chuanchao Zang,Zijian Cao,Xiangtao Meng,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xinyu Gao,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出可控框架MemGauge，分离评估LLM智能体记忆写入、管理和检索阶段的效用与投毒风险，发现各阶段呈现不同权衡模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估孤立测量记忆效用或攻击风险，未揭示记忆各阶段设计选择对效用-风险权衡的影响。

Method: MemGauge在匹配的干净和中毒条件下分别控制写入准入、管理策略和检索暴露，对11个LLM和两个基准进行受控评估。

Result: 发现三种特征：写入阶段阈值式风险跃迁、管理阶段策略相关的局部解耦、检索阶段效用与风险耦合增长；四种现有记忆系统的阶段级测量与这些特征定性一致。

Conclusion: 针对记忆操作的投毒风险存在差异，需采用阶段感知的方式评估和控制LLM智能体记忆。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core capability of LLM agents, enabling personalization and long-horizon interaction. However, memory mechanisms that retain, transform, or expose more information can affect both benign utility and susceptibility to memory poisoning. Existing evaluations typically measure memory utility or attack risk in isolation under fixed configurations, providing limited insight into how stage-specific design choices reshape their trade-off. We present \textsc{MemGauge}, a controllable framework that separately varies writing admission, management policy, and retrieval exposure under matched clean and poisoned conditions. Across 11 LLMs and two long-term memory benchmarks, controlled evaluations reveal three distinct profiles: a threshold-like risk transition during writing, policy-dependent local decoupling during management, and coupled growth of utility and risk during retrieval. We further apply analogous stage-level measurements to four existing memory systems and observe diagnostic associations qualitatively consistent with these profiles. These results show that targeted poisoning risk varies across memory operations and motivate stage-aware evaluation and control of LLM-agent memory.

</details>


### [209] [PermitGPT: A Unified Generative-AI Pipeline for Construction Hazard Forecasting, Permit Prediction, and Community Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28728)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Farjana Aktar,Akif Islam,Momen Khandoker Ope,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出PermitGPT，一个统一生成式AI框架，将非结构化施工许可描述转化为安全、许可和社区影响三类结构化决策输出，基于纽约市多源数据对齐构建90,000训练样本，微调三个开源模型，并评估其效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市建设治理需早期决策，但相关证据分散于市政与监管数据源，缺乏统一工具来整合并生成结构化决策支持信息。

Method: 对纽约市建筑局、OSHA和311服务请求进行时空对齐，通过规则与人工抽查生成90,000提示-响应对；使用参数高效微调三个开源语言模型（Gemma-3-1B、Llama-3.2-3B、Mistral-7B），在2,833测试样本上评估。

Result: Gemma-3-1B推理最快（3.07样本/秒，内存低）；Llama-3.2-3B在词汇重叠上最优（BLEU=0.0091）；Mistral-7B语义对齐最强（BERTScore-F1=0.7747）；低BLEU归因于开放结构化生成，需结合语义与定性评估。

Conclusion: PermitGPT是AI辅助施工治理的初步探索，展示了多模型互补性，并指出需加强任务级评估和真实世界验证。

Abstract: Urban construction governance requires early decisions that connect workplace safety, permitting requirements, and community impact, yet the relevant evidence is often scattered across separate municipal and regulatory data sources. This paper presents PermitGPT, a unified generative artificial intelligence framework for converting unstructured construction permit descriptions into structured decision-support outputs across three domains: safety hazard identification, permit requirement specification, and community impact assessment. To address data fragmentation, we spatially and temporally align records from the New York City Department of Buildings, Occupational Safety and Health Administration, and NYC 311 service requests, producing 90,000 structured prompt-response pairs derived through rule-based alignment and domain-informed spot checking. We fine-tune three open-weight language models using parameter-efficient adaptation and evaluate them on 2,833 held-out test cases. The results show complementary model behavior: Gemma-3-1B provides the most efficient inference at 3.07 samples per second with low memory usage, Llama-3.2-3B gives the highest lexical overlap for regulatory-style outputs with a BLEU score of 0.0091, and 4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 achieves the strongest semantic alignment with a BERTScore-F1 of 0.7747. Because the task involves open-ended structured generation, low BLEU values are interpreted alongside semantic metrics and qualitative output structure rather than as standalone indicators of utility. Overall, PermitGPT provides an initial step toward AI-assisted construction governance while identifying directions for stronger task-level evaluation and real-world validation.

</details>


### [210] [SIR: Self-improving Red-teaming for Compute Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30207)
*Chen Xiong,Zhiyuan He,Pin-Yu Chen,Stjepan Picek,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SIR黑盒攻击方法，通过可复用原则库和迭代反馈循环，显著提升对操作系统级CUA的间接提示注入攻击成功率，且攻击原则可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA安全基准使用手工编写的固定注入，低估了自适应对手的威胁，需要更真实的攻击评估方法。

Method: SIR由两部分组成：从小型纯语言原则库组合隐蔽注入；迭代反馈循环诊断受害者失败轨迹，将绕过方法蒸馏为新策略并跨任务重用。攻击在操作系统层面进行，使用确定性预言机（检查文件系统、服务、权限状态）而非LLM评判。

Result: 在三个前沿CUA上测试，原则组合加反馈将攻击成功率从手工基线的4%提升至24%（Claude Opus 4.8），从0%提升至28%（Gemini 3.5 Flash），同时良性任务仍可完成；针对一种模型发现的原则可零反馈迁移至不同架构。

Conclusion: 自适应攻击能显著威胁CUA安全，现有防御面临实际风险；原则的可迁移性表明需要更鲁棒的对策。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) are vision-language models that perceive a screen and act on a real operating system through mouse, keyboard, and terminal, and they are increasingly deployed to automate everyday digital tasks. Because they can be exposed to untrusted content while operating, they are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which an adversary plants instructions in content the agent will read and redirects it toward actions that violate the user's intent. Existing CUA safety benchmarks evaluate fixed injections written by hand, which may underestimate the risk posed by an adaptive adversary. We present SIR, a black box IPI attack that (i) composes stealthy injections from a small library of reusable principles stated in plain language and (ii) wraps composition in an iterative feedback loop that diagnoses the victim's failed trajectories and distills the bypasses into new, named strategies that are reapplied across tasks. Unlike prior red teaming of web agents, we target CUAs at the operating system level and score attacks with a fully deterministic oracle, using checks on filesystem, service, and permission state rather than an LLM judge. On experiment, we evaluate three frontier CUAs. Composing principles with feedback raises the attack success rate over a baseline written by hand, for example from 4% to 24% on Claude Opus 4.8 and from 0% to 28% on Gemini 3.5 Flash, while the benign task still completes. Principles discovered against one model further transfer to a different architecture with no additional feedback.

</details>


### [211] [Extracting Knowledge from Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30288)
*Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Xinyu Gao,Yingkai Dong,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: ToolSiphon是一种针对LLM智能体的查询型知识提取攻击，利用目标判别信号和响应事实信号，在工具选择不确定性和参数压缩等挑战下，平均恢复74.3%的源记录，并有效对抗现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过工具调用访问知识库，但可能面临工具介导的知识提取风险，即攻击者可以从智能体输出中逐步重建工具背后的知识源。已有工作未系统研究工具调用引入的独特挑战，如工具选择不确定性和工具参数压缩。

Method: 提出ToolSiphon攻击方法，包含两个互补信号：目标判别信号（通过工具对比分析实现）用于引导查询指向目标工具；响应基础事实信号（通过证据链反馈实现）用于缓解参数压缩并逐步扩大提取覆盖范围。

Result: 在三种知识型工具和六个领域数据集上，ToolSiphon平均恢复74.3%的源记录，文本恢复率达83.2%，语义相似度90.2%。即使没有非目标工具的粗粒度信息，也能恢复66.3%的记录。在代表性防御和三个真实智能体平台上仍有效。

Conclusion: ToolSiphon展示了LLM智能体工具调用中知识提取攻击的严重威胁，强调了需要更 robust 的防御机制来保护工具背后的知识源。

Abstract: LLM agents commonly use knowledge-based tools and access their underlying files, databases, and search indexes through tool invocation. This integration improves agents' ability to provide domain-specific services but also introduces the risk of tool-mediated knowledge extraction: source content exposed to an agent for legitimate responses may be progressively recovered from its outputs, enabling reconstruction of the knowledge source behind a target tool. This paper systematically investigates this risk and identifies two challenges introduced by tool invocation: tool-selection uncertainty, where an agent may invoke a competing tool instead of the target tool, and tool-argument compression, where fine-grained query information may be lost when the agent generates tool arguments. To tackle these challenges, we propose ToolSiphon, a query-only extraction attack that introduces two complementary signals: a target-discriminative signal, implemented through Tool Contrastive Analysis, to steer queries toward the target tool; and a response-grounded factual signal, implemented through Evidence Chained Feedback, to mitigate argument compression and progressively expand extraction coverage. Across three types of knowledge-based tools and six domain-specific datasets, ToolSiphon recovers 74.3% of source records on average when coarse-grained information about non-target tools is available, with 83.2% textual recovery and 90.2% semantic similarity. Even without such information, it recovers 66.3% of source records. ToolSiphon also remains effective against representative defenses and on three real-world agent platforms.

</details>


### [212] [A Roadmap to Available ICS Datasets and Testbeds for Cybersecurity Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30332)
*Ebtesam J. Alqahtani,Mohammad Hammoudeh*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了ICS网络安全数据集、测试床和数字孪生的现状，通过分类法分析其架构与能力，指出存在标准化缺失、现代攻击场景不足等问题，并给出研究建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICS是关键基础设施的核心，但OT与IT融合及工业4.0技术扩大了攻击面，而缺乏合适数据集和实验环境阻碍了安全方案的开发与测试。

Method: 提出多种分类法，对现有数据集、测试床和数字孪生的架构、特性、能力及优缺点进行系统分析。

Result: 发现持续性问题：缺乏标准化基准数据集、现代攻击场景不足、基于真实运行流量的数据集有限，以及AI驱动安全方案难以验证。

Conclusion: 该路线图总结了当前研究，识别了研究空白，并为创建新型工具提供了具体建议。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS) are the backbone of many critical infrastructure sectors; however, their growing level of connectivity, long lifespan and integration with the Information Technology (IT) environment introduces numerous cybersecurity challenges. The merging of Operational Technology (OT) and IT along with the deployment of Industry 4.0 technologies increases the attack surface of ICS environments, which in turn makes them more vulnerable to advanced cyber threats. Therefore, many researchers have shown interest in the field of cybersecurity of ICS. The topics of intrusion detection, anomaly detection, threat intelligence, attack simulation and resilience assessment of ICS have received much attention. Nevertheless, the development and testing of cybersecurity solutions for ICS remains to be challenging due to the lack of appropriate datasets and experimental environment. The main objective of this paper is to provide the roadmap of existing ICS cybersecurity datasets, testbeds and digital twins. This paper presents various taxonomies along with systematic analysis of architecture, characteristics, capabilities, pros and cons of these tools. The results of the analysis demonstrate the presence of persistent problems such as lack of standardized benchmarking datasets, lack of modern attack scenarios, insufficient number of datasets based on real operational traffic and difficulty in validating artificial intelligence-driven cybersecurity solutions. In addition to summarizing current research on ICS cybersecurity datasets and testbeds, this roadmap provides the identification of research gaps and recommendations on creation of new tools.

</details>


### [213] [KORD: Breaking the Key-Generation Bottleneck in Dealerless FSS via Protocol--Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30379)
*Yijing Peng,Lin Liu,Yujie Xue,Shaojing Fu,Shaoqing Li,Yaohua Wang,Rongmao Chen,Yang Guo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: KORD通过协议-硬件协同设计，利用专用芯片建立信任根，将去中心化FSS密钥生成从多轮交互压缩为单轮，通信量降低四个数量级，密钥生成时间占比从96%降至11.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSS依赖可信经销商生成函数密钥，扩大信任边界且阻碍部署；而去经销商化协议虽消除此依赖，但通信开销大且交互轮数随输入位宽线性增长，密钥生成成为主要瓶颈。

Method: KORD采用协议-硬件协同设计：一对专用芯片通过相互认证建立共同信任根，在该安全边界内重构生成协议，将交互压缩为单轮（与GGM深度无关）；同时设计跨密钥调度方案，交错独立GGM树遍历，保持高计算吞吐。

Result: 相比最先进的分布式FSS协议，KORD在多种FSS构建块上将每密钥生成通信量减少7633–70274倍；布线后分析显示204MHz下每秒生成1275万个32位DPF密钥，仅用21.5K LUT，AES通道利用率达99.8%。在私有ResNet-18推理中，密钥生成占端到端时间比例从96%以上降至11.9%。

Conclusion: KORD通过硬件信任根与协议重构，彻底解决了去中心化FSS密钥生成的通信和轮次瓶颈，使密钥生成不再是隐私计算的主要开销，为FSS实际部署扫清了关键障碍。

Abstract: Function secret sharing (FSS) has become a core primitive in privacy-preserving computation. However, each FSS invocation requires a fresh pair of function keys generated by a trusted dealer , expands the system's trust boundary and hinders practical deployment. Existing dealerless protocols eliminate this dependency, but incur substantial communication and a number of interaction rounds that grows linearly with the input bit-width, making key generation a major bottleneck.
  This paper present KORD, a protocol--hardware co-design that dramatically reduces the cost of dealerless FSS key generation. At its core is a pair of special-purpose chips that establish a common root of trust through mutual attestation and, within it, reconstruct FSS keys---eliminating the need for a dealer. This root of trust further forms a security boundary within which KORD restructures the generation protocol, collapsing the interaction of prior dealerless protocols into a single round, independent of GGM depth. A cross-key scheduling scheme then interleaves independent GGM-tree traversals, sustaining high computational throughput. KORD reduces per-key-generation communication by 7,633--70,274$\times$ over the state-of-the-art distributed FSS protocol across a comprehensive suite of FSS building blocks. Post-route analysis projects 12.75 million 32-bit DPF keys per second at 204 MHz using 21.5K LUTs, with 99.8% AES lane utilization. On private ResNet-18 inference, KORD cuts the share of end-to-end time spent on key generation from over 96% to 11.9%.

</details>


### [214] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) in Computational Pathology: Definitions, Taxonomy, and Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28820)
*Shubham Innani,Suhang You,Adam Shephard,Bhakti Baheti,Francesco Ciompi,Joe Yeong,Nasir Rajpoot,Michael Feldman,Solene Florence Kammerer-Jacquet,Dimitrios Makris,Geert Litjens,Anne L. Martel,Jana Lipkova,April Khademi,Spyridon Bakas,for the MICCAI SIG-CompPath*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文旨在通过引入病理学中心词汇、建立跨方法家族与三维正交轴（阶段、类型、范围）的分类法，以及构建任务驱动框架，系统化计算病理学中的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法，并识别临床部署中的五大关键差距及提出可行步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学中XAI文献因术语不一致、方法家族重叠、验证随意及现有综述碎片化而分散，阻碍了临床采纳中对安全性、问责制和监管监督的信任与验证需求。

Method: 提出三项正式化举措：1) 引入包含七个核心术语的病理学专用词汇；2) 构建涵盖方法家族及阶段、类型、范围三个正交轴的分类法；3) 建立任务驱动框架，将五种临床问题映射至推荐方法、方法评估及部署情境。

Result: 识别出当前XAI能力与临床部署之间的五大关键差距，并针对每项差距提出可操作的推进步骤。

Conclusion: 通过系统化XAI方法并明确差距与路径，可推动XAI在计算病理学中从研究走向可信赖的临床实际应用。

Abstract: Computational pathology (CompPath) is transforming medicine by leveraging artificial intelligence (AI) algorithms to support diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment prediction from gigapixel whole-slide images. Clinical adoption is progressing, but is constrained by concerns about safety, accountability, and regulatory oversight in high-stakes clinical environments. Explainable AI (XAI) systems hold promise for building trust and enabling verification, yet the literature remains fragmented due to inconsistent terminology, overlapping methodological families, ad hoc validation, and current reviews. This review aims to formalize XAI methods in CompPath through the: i) introduction of a pathology-centric vocabulary comprising seven core terms; ii) development of a taxonomy across methodological families and three orthogonal axes (stage, type, scope); and iii) establishment of a task-driven framework that maps five clinical questions to recommended methods, method evaluation, and deployment context. Five key gaps between current XAI capabilities and clinical deployment are identified, and actionable steps are proposed to advance XAI for CompPath.

</details>


### [215] [Attesting Outputs and Delegation Ancestry in Multi-Agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30387)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出双层证明设计，解决多代理动态委托中的字节归属与跨部署者边授权验证问题，使用共同签名DAG抵抗子密钥泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凭证只约束身份，不绑定后续输出字节，也无法证明动态创建的跨部署者边的双向授权，且缺乏统一威胁模型下的比较。

Method: 可信运行时对输出哈希签名记录字节，祖先证据记录边授权；比较签名链表、Merkle链变体与共同签名DAG，并实现组合验证器。

Result: 共同签名DAG在子密钥泄露下拒绝未授权父绑定；Apple M1 Pro上祖先检查24.3-499.2微秒/跳，本地工作流813.1毫秒均值延迟，AWS三可用区1000条路径全部验证通过，颁发3.651毫秒，完整验证5.015毫秒。

Conclusion: 共同签名DAG提供更强的安全保证，验证开销可接受，适用于无共享权威的动态委托场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent applications delegate work across independently operated deployers. After an incident, a verifier must answer two questions: which deployer released the reported bytes, and whether each cross-deployer edge was authorized. Credentials establish who may act, but need not bind them to later output bytes or prove both deployers authorized a dynamically created edge. We present a two-layer attestation design for dynamic delegation without a shared authority, public log, or precommitted workflow. A trusted deployer runtime signs a hash of each released output; this records released bytes but does not prevent prompt injection. Ancestry evidence records edge authorization. Under a unified threat model, we compare a signed linked list, a Merkle-chain variant, and a co-signed DAG. The primitives are standard; the contribution is deployer-side binding and the evidence needed for the two questions. After child-key compromise, the single-signer designs permit an unauthorized parent binding, whereas the co-signed DAG rejects it because the parent must authorize the edge. Fixed adversary matrices and regression tests validate the composed verifier. On an Apple M1 Pro, ancestry-only checks take 24.3-499.2us per hop. In a live local multi-service workflow, a parent discovers the child's A2A Agent Card; the child calls an MCP tool and releases local-LLM output: all 30 signed-DAG tasks passed complete verification, while a controlled child-key-only claim was rejected; its mean end-to-end latency was 813.1ms versus 770.8ms without evidence. In a complementary three-availability-zone AWS deployment, all 1,000 valid co-signed-DAG paths verified; issuance averaged 3.651ms and complete verification 5.015ms. The cloud result excludes TLS/mTLS, KMS, and model-serving latency.

</details>


### [216] [Why Are LLM Backdoor Defenses Fragmented? A Feature-Level Explanation with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30403)
*Yizhe Zeng,Chenxu Niu,Wei Zhang,Hao Huang,Yunpeng Li,Dongxu Han,Dan Du,Cheng Hong,Hequn Xian,Yuling Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对LLM后门攻击进行系统性的特征级机理分析，揭示脏标签与干净标签后门在特征编码上的本质差异，并通过特征钳制实现统一且可解释的防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型的后门防御方法碎片化严重，无法同时有效抵御脏标签和干净标签攻击，而缺乏对后门内部机制的深入理解是导致这一问题的根本原因。

Method: 从干净/中毒模型在干净/触发输入上的2×2对照实验出发，追踪后门导致的logit偏移至贡献最高的SAE特征，将其分类为交互、抑制、混合和权重修改四类角色，并提出推理时特征钳制方法进行验证。

Result: 特征钳制在大多数脏标签设置下将攻击成功率降至10.8%以下，在大多数干净标签设置下降至15.4%以下，同时保持良性任务性能不降。

Conclusion: SAE特征分析能够解释现有防御碎片化的原因，并为后门缓解提供可解释的统一指导，推动可审计的LLM安全机制发展。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to large language models (LLMs), but existing defenses remain fragmented, failing to pro?vide unified defense against both dirty-label and clean-label attacks. To investigate why such fragmentation arises, we present the first systematic feature-level mechanistic analysis of LLM backdoors using sparse autoencoders (SAEs). Starting from a 2 x 2 comparison of clean and poisoned models on clean and triggered inputs, we trace backdoor-induced logit shifts to high-contributing SAE features and categorize them into four roles: interac?tion, suppressed, mixed, and weight-modified features. This taxonomy reveals system?atic encoding differences: dirty-label back?doors are dominated by isolated interaction features, whereas clean-label backdoors rely more on heterogeneous mixtures of mixed and weight-modified features. These differ?ences explain why existing defenses remain fragmented across attack paradigms. We val?idate this hypothesis through inference-time feature clamping, which reduces ASR to at most 10.8% in most dirty-label settings and at most 15.4% in the majority of clean-label settings, while preserving benign-task perfor?mance. These results show that SAE-based analysis can explain defense fragmentation and guide interpretable backdoor mitigation.

</details>


### [217] [ECLIPSE: Self-Evolving Stealthy Prompt Injection Attack against Long-Horizon Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30441)
*Shiqian Zhao,Yangfan Zhou,Xinfeng Li,Runyi Hu,Yechao Zhang,Yi Xie,Tianwei Zhang,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出ECLIPSE框架，通过自进化与隐蔽的提示注入攻击长周期智能体系统，并构建LASE-Bench基准验证其高成功率与现有防御的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入攻击要么将恶意目标置于显式指令中易被检测，要么将意图分散到多个执行阶段导致完成不可靠，缺乏针对长周期智能体系统的隐蔽且可靠攻击方法。

Method: ECLIPSE结合直接用户提示注入与间接工具侧注入，包括两个组件：Stealthy Attack Trajectory Synthesis在沙盒中生成并迭代验证候选工具链，渲染为自然单次提示作为直接指令；Tool-Chain Steering通过静态工作流编码（SWE）在目标工具描述中嵌入状态转换线索，以及动态轨迹校正（DTC）在执行偏离时提供修正信号。

Result: 在LASE-Bench（120个恶意任务、198个工具，96.7%任务至少5次工具调用）上，ECLIPSE无防御时攻击成功率最高达96.7%，在常见安全过滤下为69.2%，比最强基线在防御场景下高27.5%；现有防御无法可靠抵御。

Conclusion: ECLIPSE在长周期智能体系统中实现高效且隐蔽的注入攻击，揭示现有安全机制不足，亟需更有效的防御策略。

Abstract: Recently, large language model (LLM) agents, such as Codex, Claude Code, and OpenClaw, have become capable of planning and executing long-horizon tasks through repeated tool calls. This capability also creates new opportunities for prompt injection. Existing attacks either place the malicious objective in one explicit instruction, making it easy to detect, or distribute the intent across multiple execution stages, making successful completion unreliable.
  In this work, we propose ECLIPSE, a self-evolving and stealthy prompt-injection framework for long-horizon agentic systems. ECLIPSE combines direct user-prompt injection with indirect tool-side injection through two components. On the one hand, Stealthy Attack Trajectory Synthesis uses a sandbox to generate and iteratively verify candidate tool chains, then renders a verified chain as a natural one-shot prompt to serve as the direct instruction. Then, Tool-Chain Steering transfers this plan to the target environment through Static Workflow Encoding (SWE), which embeds state-transition cues in target-tool descriptions, and Dynamic Trajectory Correction (DTC), which supplies corrective signals when execution deviates from the planned chain.
  To enable systematic evaluation, we further introduce LASE-Bench, a long-horizon agent-safety benchmark with 120 malicious tasks and 198 unique tools; 96.7% of its tasks make at least five tool calls. The experimental results show that ECLIPSE is highly effective: it achieves up to 96.7% attack success without defense and 69.2% under the common safety filter, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5% in the defended setting. Evaluations against representative defenses further show that existing safeguards do not reliably defend it, which raises the need for more effective defenses.

</details>


### [218] [Behavioral Latency as Weak Event-Time Supervision for EEG Reaction-Time Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29428)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Ayana Mussabayeva,Yedige Mussabayev,Xue Liu,Kun Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 将单试次EEG中的反应时预测重新建模为事件时间后验分布估计，通过分布监督而非标量回归，在多个时间骨干网络上一致提升预测性能，并提供了后验几何与时间定位的解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG反应时预测将RT视为固定刺激锁定窗口上的标量回归标签，忽略了RT作为反应相关动态的时间证据特性，导致模型无法充分利用事件时间信息。

Method: 提出事件时间后验建模框架，模型估计响应相关事件时间的后验分布p(t_event|X)，以其均值作为RT估计；训练时使用分布级监督，并通过控制实验分离分布监督与期望读取的贡献。

Result: 在Healthy Brain Network数据集上，分布事件时间监督在5个随机种子下一致优于标量回归和时间读取控制；效果跨4种时间骨干网络成立且与模型规模无关；后验几何与移位裁剪推断揭示了浓度、对齐及时间定位特性，但存在等变性差距。

Conclusion: 事件时间后验建模为联系单试次EEG动态与行为时序提供了概率化、可解释的框架，但当前模型仍未完全实现理想的裁剪相对定位，存在改进空间。

Abstract: Single-trial EEG analyses are often organized around events and latencies, yet EEG-based reaction-time (RT) prediction is posed as scalar regression on a fixed stimulus-locked window. RT is treated as a window-level label rather than timing evidence about response-relevant dynamics. Here we reformulate trial-wise RT decoding as event-time posterior modeling. Instead of predicting RT directly, the model estimates a posterior over response-relevant event times, $p(t_{\mathrm{event}}\mid X)$, and uses its mean as the RT estimate. This treats behavioral latency as a weak observation of latent response-relevant timing. We evaluate this formulation on the Healthy Brain Network contrast change detection EEG task under a subject-disjoint, release-separated protocol. Across five seeds, distributional event-time supervision consistently improves held-out RT prediction relative to scalar regression and temporal-readout controls. Controlled objective comparisons isolate supervision of the event-time distribution, rather than expectation-based readout alone, as the source of this gain. Architecture controls show that the effect persists across four temporal backbones and is not explained by model scale. Beyond point prediction, posterior geometry characterizes concentration, target alignment, and interval behavior, while observation-noise calibration separates latent concentration from predictive uncertainty over RT. Shifted-crop inference probes shortcut use versus temporal localization. Matched shift-jitter improves robustness, increases mean sensitivity, and moves predictions more often in the expected crop-relative direction. Sensitivity remains below ideal crop-relative localization, leaving a clear equivariance gap. Together, these results establish event-time posterior modeling as a probabilistic and interpretable formulation for linking single-trial EEG dynamics to behavioral timing.

</details>


### [219] [Towards Operator-Empowered Vulnerability Hotfixing for 5G Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30615)
*Dong Hyeok Kim,Xin Zhe Khooi,Hocheol Nam,Seungjin Baek,Mun Choon Chan,CheolJun Park,Min Suk Kang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Buckler框架，让移动网络运营商在漏洞修复窗口期内部署临时、本地、可逆的热修复，通过标准化的L2/L3信道钩子和匹配-动作接口，覆盖了20种攻击，验证了其实用性和架构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝协议漏洞可能数月甚至数年无法完全修复，而标准制定、厂商和运营商协调修复过程漫长，需要一种运营商可自主部署的临时防御手段来填补暴露窗口期。

Method: 在RAN的标准化L2/L3信道边界放置可复用钩子，提供有状态匹配-动作接口，支持DROP、MODIFY和RELEASE三种预防动作；基于23篇论文提取64种攻击，筛选出43种可干预点，构建20种热修复。

Result: 20种热修复全部使用统一规则词汇和五个标准化信道钩子；在srsRAN和OpenAirInterface上实现改动小且结构相似，成功演示三种动作应对典型可用性和隐私攻击。

Conclusion: 运营商自助热修复是实用且可移植的临时防御手段，同时明确了RAN单独预防的架构性局限，为后续防御设计提供边界参考。

Abstract: Cellular protocol vulnerabilities can remain exploitable for months or years while standards bodies, vendors, and mobile network operators (MNOs) coordinate permanent fixes. We present Buckler, a framework that enables an MNO to deploy temporary, local, and reversible hotfixes in its radio access network (RAN) during this exposure window. Buckler places reusable hooks at standardized L2/L3 channel boundaries and exposes a closed, stateful match-action interface with three preventive actions: DROP, MODIFY, and RELEASE. We evaluate whether this bounded design provides useful coverage without requiring extensive changes to existing RANs. From 23 papers, we identify 64 attacks rooted in standard L2/L3 protocol behavior, of which 43 provide a preventive intervention point at the RAN, and we construct Buckler hotfixes for 20 of them. All 20 hotfixes use the same rule vocabulary and only five standardized channel hooks, while the unsupported attacks expose endpoint dependencies that a RAN cannot satisfy alone. We implement the five hooks on srsRAN and OpenAirInterface with small, structurally similar changes, and demonstrate all three actions against representative availability and privacy attacks. These results establish operator-empowered hotfixing as a practical and portable interim defense and delineate the architectural limits of RAN-only prevention.

</details>


### [220] [Automated Researchers Can Reliably Mitigate Alignment Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28945)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Jiaxin Wen,Jan Hendrik Kirchner*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动化对齐研究者（AARs）能否通过后训练同时优化多个安全基准并保持通用能力，结果显示AARs在10项对齐失败上显著优于人类基线，且能泛化到未见基准和更大模型，表明近期内自动化对齐研究是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐研究自动化可能加速实现对齐AI，但其效果难以衡量。由于许多对齐失败（如欺骗、谄媚、越狱）已有公开基准可测，本文旨在探究AARs是否能基于这些基准自动提出训练方法和数据，以缓解对齐失败。

Method: AARs通过后训练方式，提出训练方法和数据，同时优化多个安全基准，并保持通用能力。作为对照，28位经验丰富的研究人员获得最多八小时来开发方法，此外还测试了将人类想法作为AARs初始研究方向的效果。

Result: 最强AAR方法显著减少目标对齐失败，并泛化到留出基准、多轮行为审计及比目标模型大4.7倍的模型。人类基线方法表现不如最佳AAR方法，且人类想法作为初始方向未提升AAR性能。

Conclusion: 当前AARs在特征明确的对齐失败上可能无需人类专家指导，自动化对齐研究在近期内或可实用，尤其在已良好定义的失败模式上。

Abstract: Automating alignment research may accelerate progress toward aligned AI, but whether it does is hard to measure. Luckily, many alignment failures, such as deception, sycophancy, and jailbreaks, are already measurable by public benchmarks. We study whether automated alignment researchers (AARs) can post-train to mitigate alignment failures by proposing training methods and data to simultaneously optimize multiple safety benchmarks, while preserving general capability. Across 10 alignment failures, the strongest AAR methods significantly reduce the targeted alignment failures and generalize to a held-out benchmark, multi-turn behavioral audits, and models up to 4.7 times larger than the target model. As a human baseline, 28 experienced researchers receive up to eight hours to develop methods for the same benchmarks, but their methods underperform the best AAR methods. Using human ideas as the AARs' initial research direction does not improve performance, suggesting current AARs may not need guidance from experienced researchers. These results suggest that automating alignment research on well-characterized failures may be practical in the near term.

</details>


### [221] [Lie to Me: Finding Bugs in ZK DSL Toolchains with Adversarial Witness Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30648)
*Sebastian Watzinger,Christoph Hochrainer,Valentin Wüstholz,Maria Christakis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Liezz框架，通过对抗性见证注入（将两次不同输入的见证拼接）测试ZK DSL工具链，发现13个漏洞（其中7个影响可靠性），有效补充了有效执行测试的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZK DSL工具链的可靠性漏洞（约束弱于程序语义）难以通过有效执行测试发现，因为有效执行仍能正确运行，需要新的测试方法来暴露此类缺陷。

Method: 生成确定性ZK DSL程序，执行两组不同输入并拼接见证（输入取自一次、输出取自另一次），构造无效见证；通过受控分歧和多种拼接策略保持一致性，并生成参数化标准库调用以覆盖复杂功能。

Result: 支持Circom、Corset、Gnark和Noir，发现13个漏洞（7个影响可靠性），部分仅通过标准库调用触发；在相同测试预算下，有效执行基线未发现任何由注入见证暴露的可靠性失败。

Conclusion: 对抗性见证注入能有效发现有效执行测试遗漏的可靠性漏洞，可作为ZK工具链测试的关键补充方法。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge domain-specific language (ZK DSL) toolchains compile programs into constraint systems and generate witnesses for cryptographic proofs. Bugs in these toolchains can leave the enforced constraints weaker than the source-program semantics, admitting proofs for invalid executions. Such soundness bugs may remain invisible to valid-execution testing because all valid executions still behave correctly.
  We present Liezz, a testing framework that generates ZK DSL programs and exposes these bugs through adversarial witness injection. For each generated deterministic program, Liezz executes two public input assignments with different outputs and splices their witnesses, combining the input of one execution with the output of the other. The resulting witness is invalid by construction. A correct toolchain must reject it; acceptance exposes a soundness bug. Controlled divergence and multiple witness-splicing strategies preserve enough consistency to expose missing constraints. Liezz also generates parameterized standard-library calls to reach complex functionality.
  Liezz supports Circom, Corset, Gnark, and Noir. It finds 13 bugs, including seven with soundness impact. Several are reachable only through generated standard-library calls. Under the same testing budget, a valid-execution baseline does not expose any of the soundness failures revealed by accepted injected witnesses, showing that adversarial witness injection reaches failures missed by valid-execution testing.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond the Payload: How User Invocation Shapes Coding Agent Vulnerability to Repository Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30686)
*Fukang Zhu,Binbin Zhao,Ruixiao Lin,Ping He,Tianyu Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次提出CIPR基准，系统研究用户侧提示配置（任务类型、提示风格、技能规则）对编码智能体在受污染仓库中攻击成功率（ASR）和告警率（AR）的影响，发现漏洞具有高度上下文依赖性，且测试执行任务形成高ASR低AR的隐蔽攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库投毒研究主要关注攻击者注入和伪装，忽略了开发者在任务委派、请求措辞和技能/规则选择等日常调用环节对风险的影响；为解决这一盲区，需要系统评估用户侧提示级配置如何影响编码智能体的漏洞暴露。

Method: 构建CIPR基准，包含20个真实仓库、四种任务类型、三种基于社交媒体的提示风格及三种技能/规则条件，共1,920个实例，采用自动化运行时和基于轨迹的oracle来量化攻击成功率（ASR）和智能体告警率（AR）。

Result: 任务类型导致ASR最大4.5倍差异；测试执行任务表现为高ASR低AR的隐蔽攻击面。提示表述间接影响风险：欠指定提示通过截断执行深度降低ASR，而嘈杂提示呈抑制告警的趋势，使恶意内容更不显眼。

Conclusion: 编码智能体漏洞并非静态属性，而是由日常用户配置动态塑造的结果；需将用户侧上下文纳入安全评估，以提升实际部署中的鲁棒性与告警有效性。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, including bootstrapping projects from third-party repositories whose integrity cannot be assumed. Prior work on repository poisoning largely focuses on attacker-controlled injection and disguise, but developers also shape risk through everyday invocation choices: what task to delegate, how to phrase the request, and which skills or rules to supply. We term these user-side choices Prompt-Level Configurations (PLCs) and introduce CIPR (Coding In Poisoned Repos), the first benchmark that systematically varies PLCs in poisoned real-world repositories. CIPR comprises 1,920 instances across 20 repositories, four task types, three social-media-grounded prompt styles, and three skill/rule conditions, and measures attack success rate (ASR) and agent alert rate (AR) using automated runtime and trace-based oracles. Our evaluation reveals two key insights: (1) Vulnerability is highly context-dependent, with task type creating up to a 4.5-fold difference in ASR, with test-execution task forming a silent attack surface (high ASR, low AR). (2) Prompt expression shifts risk indirectly: underspecified prompts reduce ASR by truncating execution depth; noisy prompts exhibit a directional trend toward suppressing alerts by making malicious content less conspicuous. These findings highlight that coding agent vulnerability is not a static property, but a dynamic outcome shaped by everyday user configurations.

</details>


### [223] [Reference-Grafting Matches Fine-Tuning at Eliciting Sandbagged Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29458)
*Linh Le,Hong Kiat Tan,David Williams-King*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 参考嫁接（reference-grafting）通过主动学习选择少量电路，将激活坐标设为诚实参考值，在不更新权重或使用标签的情况下，恢复沙袋模型（sandbagging）隐藏能力达94-101%，优于加法激活引导，且与微调激发效果相当；其机制是微调锁为阈值门，嫁接需跨越阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙袋行为（模型故意低估能力）威胁前沿模型治理的安全评估。既有方法中微调可激发隐藏能力但加法激活引导失败，需要一种无需权重更新或训练标签的高效激发方法，并理解其工作机制。

Method: 参考嫁接：在对比方向上，将激活坐标设为诚实参考值，通过主动学习选择少量电路（circuits）进行定向修改；用少量配对样本（2-5个）拟合方向，测试于11个密码锁定模型（1.5-32B，三种架构）及RL诱导沙袋和代码生成场景。

Result: 恢复诚实-沙袋差距的94-101%，与微调激发匹配；嫁接方向取决于诱导方式（提示、微调、RL各有不同方向），而非隐藏内容；电路中断（circuit-breaking）会每前向传递重路由激活，使固定编辑失效。

Conclusion: 参考嫁接是一种有效且高效的沙袋能力激发方法，揭示微调锁为阈值门机制，解释加法引导和归零失败的原因；但面对每步动态重路由的防御（如电路中断）时，固定编辑无法维持效果，提示需要更动态的干预策略。

Abstract: Sandbagging, in which a model deliberately underperforms on an evaluation despite retaining the underlying capability, threatens the safety evaluations that frontier-model governance depends on. The Elicitation Game found that fine-tuning elicits hidden capability from sandbagging model organisms whereas additive activation steering fails. We revisit that verdict with reference-grafting, which sets an activation's coordinate along a contrast direction to the value it takes in an honest reference, at a small set of circuits chosen by active learning. Across eleven fine-tuned password-locked models (1.5-32B, three architecture lineages), it recovers +94 to +101% of the honest-sandbagging gap, matching fine-tuning elicitation without weight updates or training labels; two to five paired examples suffice to fit the direction. Similar recovery holds for reinforcement-learning-induced sandbagging and for password-locked code generation. Grafting works because the fine-tuned lock is a thresholded gate: held-out accuracy stays at the sandbagged level until the grafted coordinate crosses a threshold near the honest reference, which is why additive steering and zeroing the coordinate often fail. The direction tracks how the sandbagging was induced rather than what is withheld -- fit on grade-school science (ARC) it elicits withheld hazardous knowledge (WMDP), yet prompting, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning each carry a different direction. Circuit-breaking marks the boundary: it reroutes activations on every forward pass, so the fixed edits we test are re-broken downstream and do not restore coherent generation.

</details>


### [224] [SingProbe Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30703)
*Sing Team*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SingProbe，一种轻量级内在运行时护栏，直接复用LLM推理时的隐藏状态，在解码过程中以极低开销持续预测查询意图、响应安全性和幻觉风险，并在流式基准SingStreamBench上验证其性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时护栏依赖独立外部模型，带来额外推理成本、延迟安全信号以及与基础模型能力不匹配的问题。

Method: SingProbe在统一框架内，基于LLM内部隐藏状态进行令牌级连续预测，支持查询意图、响应安全和幻觉风险检测；并引入SingStreamBench基准评估流式护栏在良性前缀不触发、危险内容及时触发的性能；进一步扩展到医疗场景SingProbe-Med，选择性激活风险定向解码干预。

Result: SingProbe仅约2M参数和<0.5%额外开销，性能优于或媲美更大的独立护栏和专门幻觉检测器；其分数还可预判未来生成风险并引导约束安全解码。

Conclusion: 内部模型表示为生成时监控和控制提供了高效且有效的接口，实现“免费午餐”式护栏。

Abstract: Runtime guardrails are essential for reliable large language model (LLM) deployment, yet existing approaches typically rely on independent, external models that introduce additional inference cost, delayed safety signals, and a capacity mismatch with increasingly capable base models. To address these issues, we introduce SingProbe, a lightweight intrinsic runtime guard that directly reuses hidden states produced during LLM inference and operates alongside autoregressive decoding. Within a unified framework, SingProbe continuously predicts query intent, response safety, and hallucination risk at the token level with negligible additional guardrail inference overhead, offering a "free-lunch" solution. We further introduce SingStreamBench, a benchmark designed to assess whether streaming guardrails remain inactive on benign prefixes while promptly detecting emerging unsafe content. Extensive experiments show that SingProbe achieves competitive or superior performance compared with substantially larger standalone guardrails and specialized hallucination detectors, with only $\approx$2M parameters and $<0.5\%$ extra overhead. Beyond passive detection, we also show that SingProbe scores can anticipate future generation risk and guide constrained safe decoding. We further extend this paradigm to medical generation through SingProbe-Med, which selectively activates risk-directed decoding interventions only when clinically relevant risks emerge. Together, these results demonstrate that internal model representations provide an effective and efficient interface for generation-time monitoring and control.

</details>


### [225] [The Fragility of Jailbreak Robustness Across Operational States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30748)
*Yuna Park,Hwang Youn Kim,Yujin Kim,Won Woo Ro,Suhyun Kim,Jae-In Hwang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 现有越狱评估仅用默认配置下的单一攻击成功率（ASR）衡量鲁棒性，但用户与LLM的交互会产生多种非默认操作状态，导致ASR剧烈变化。研究发现，仅修改无关安全的系统提示，即可让ASR提升高达56个百分点，且状态相关的鲁棒性变化与隐藏表示中拒绝相关轴上的投影强相关，表明单一评估无法全面刻画鲁棒性，需扩展至非默认状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前越狱评估默认采用单一操作状态（如标准系统提示）下的ASR作为鲁棒性指标，忽视了实际交互中可能出现的多种操作状态（如不同系统提示或上下文）。这种简化可能高估模型的安全性，因为攻击在非默认状态下可能显著更有效，导致安全评估失真。

Method: 系统选取七个对齐模型和三种代表性越狱攻击，对比默认（vanilla）与非默认操作状态下的ASR，并分析状态变化时隐藏表示中拒绝相关轴的投影差异，评估该投影对越狱成功率的预测能力。

Result: 非默认操作状态下ASR出现大幅波动，单案例中从2%升至58%（增加56个百分点）；即使攻击是针对默认状态优化设计的，仍出现增长。隐藏表示沿拒绝相关轴的投影与越狱成败强相关，可有效预测结果。

Conclusion: 默认状态下的单次评估不足以全面反映模型的越狱鲁棒性，应引入多操作状态测试，以更真实地评估安全性并避免低估风险。

Abstract: Existing jailbreak evaluations typically characterize robustness using a single attack success rate (ASR) measured in a default configuration (the vanilla state). However, user-LLM interactions can induce diverse operational states beyond the vanilla state. In this work, we find that jailbreak robustness is highly fragile to operational-state variation: even when the attack remains fixed, changing only an ordinary system prompt not designed to affect safety can dramatically alter attack success rates. We systematically investigate this phenomenon across seven aligned models and three representative jailbreak attacks, observing substantial differences in ASR between vanilla and non-vanilla operational states. In one case, ASR increases by up to 56 percentage points (2% to 58%) solely due to a change in operational state. Remarkably, these increases occur even for attacks originally designed and optimized under vanilla-state evaluation. We further show that state-dependent robustness variation is systematically associated with differences in hidden representations along a refusal-related axis, and that projections onto this axis strongly predict jailbreak outcomes. Our results show that a single vanilla-state evaluation may not fully characterize jailbreak robustness, motivating evaluations that also examine how robustness changes across non-vanilla operational states.

</details>


### [226] [Adversarial Online Classification with a Preview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29503)
*Roi Livni,Sahil Singla*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究带随机预览的在线分类问题，证明VC维为d的二元类最优超额损失为Θ(d/p+√dT)，多类情形为Õ(d_DS/p+√d_Nat T)，表明随机预览可将最坏情况序贯复杂度替换为经典统计维度，且无需随机化在线顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最坏情况在线分类受Littlestone维度等序贯复杂度制约，即使VC维为1的阈值类也可能无法学习。本文提出预览模型，通过随机预览部分样本，探索能否用经典统计复杂度（如VC维）替代序贯复杂度，从而在对抗性顺序下实现可学习性。

Method: 针对二元类提出ChainedPrediction算法，采用在线链式（chaining）的多尺度聚合实现，而非仅作为分析工具；对多类情形给出相应边界，并利用预览样本降低序贯复杂度至统计维度。

Result: 二元类最优超额损失为Θ(d/p+√dT)（上限截断至T）；多类获得Õ(d_DS/p+√d_Nat T)边界，且不依赖类别数。

Conclusion: 随机预览能以统计维度替代最坏情况序贯复杂度，且二元情形下边界严格紧，算法实现有效，为在线学习提供新视角。

Abstract: Worst-case online classification is governed by sequential complexity, such as Littlestone dimension, and can be impossible even for statistically simple classes, such as thresholds of VC dimension one. We study a preview model in which an oblivious adversary fixes an entire labeled sequence of length $T$, a uniformly random subset of size $pT$ is revealed before prediction begins, and the remaining $(1-p)T$ examples are then presented in their original adversarial order.
  Against the best full-sequence hypothesis evaluated on the unrevealed examples, we characterize the dependence on the preview rate $p$: for binary classes of VC dimension $d$, the optimal excess loss is $Θ(d/p+\sqrt{dT})$, up to the trivial cap at $T$; for multiclass classes we obtain the corresponding $\widetilde O(d_{\rm DS}/p+\sqrt{d_{\rm Nat}T})$ bound with no dependence on the number of labels. Thus a random preview can replace worst-case sequential complexity by classical statistical dimensions without randomizing the online order. To achieve the sharp binary bound, our ChainedPrediction algorithm uses an online analogue of chaining, implemented as a multiscale aggregation algorithm rather than only as an analytic argument.

</details>


### [227] [Let Prompts Bridge Defense Knowledge: Transferable Graph Purification via Vulnerability-Aware GPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29054)
*Shuomin Xue,Jingyuan Li,Ju Jia,Jingxuan Yu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于漏洞感知图提示学习的可迁移图净化方案ProGAP，通过预训练边检测器和提示微调，实现跨域防御知识迁移，在提升对抗净化效果的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗净化方法本质上是领域受限的防御，存在单域数据多样性不足和从头训练专用防御策略计算开销大的问题。

Method: 首先在数据丰富的图上预训练一个扰动捕获边检测器，联合建模拓扑和语义信息；然后设计漏洞感知提示，为偏置节点注入针对性净化指导，使预训练检测器适应下游图分布偏移而无需大规模参数更新。

Result: 与最先进基线相比，ProGAP在性能上提升1%-9%，时间消耗降低最多2.2倍。

Conclusion: ProGAP验证了通过图提示学习实现跨域对抗防御知识迁移的可行性，为实际多媒体场景中的图对抗鲁棒性提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a cornerstone for representing complex relational dependencies in diverse multimedia tasks, particularly in cross-platform user interest modeling and cross-modal semantic alignment.
