Method: PAC-LLM融合学习到的相空间特征与文本信息，通过辅助特征模块和门控加权机制实现多变量耦合信息的融合与选择。

Result: 在代表性混沌系统上，该方法在短期和长期预测中均优于微调与零样本基线，消融实验验证各关键组件有效性。

Conclusion: PAC-LLM有效增强LLM对混沌时序的预测能力，为短观测下的长时混沌预测提供新途径。

Abstract: Chaotic time series forecasting is a challenging task due to its sensitivity to initial conditions and long-term unpredictability. Traditional methods typically rely on sufficient temporal trajectories to learn long-term dynamics, which limits their applicability when only short-term observations are available. While recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great potential for time series forecasting, their temporal representations are not explicitly tailored to the phase-space structure and nonlinear evolution of chaotic systems. To address these issues, we propose PAC-LLM, a phase-space-aware adaptive fusion framework for long-term chaotic time series forecasting powered by LLMs. PAC-LLM leverages learned phase-space features and textual information to fully enable LLM's time series forecasting capacity. In particular, we design an auxiliary feature module and a gated weighting mechanism for multivariate coupling information fusion and selection. Extensive experiments on representative chaotic systems demonstrate that our method outperforms existing fine-tuned and zero-shot baselines in both short-term and long-term predictions. Our ablation study further confirms the effectiveness of each key component in PAC-LLM.

</details>


### [302] [Revolutionizing Turn-by-Turn Navigation with Cloud-Edge Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29073)
*Yiming Yang,Hao Fu,Fanxiang Zeng,Xikai Yang,Yue Liu,Ning Guo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出首个大规模深度学习驱动的逐向导航音频指令生成框架，结合Transformer与MoE，通过云端协同架构实现实时上下文感知指令，显著降低偏航率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航音频指令依赖规则方法，难以平衡信息量与认知负荷，易致驾驶员困惑或错过转弯。

Method: 将指令生成建模为多任务学习，分解为模块化元素组合；用Transformer提取时空特征，MoE增强多任务能力；采用云边协同架构保障实时性。

Result: 实车实验中偏航率（偏离导航路线车辆比例）较传统方法显著降低，指令更清晰有效。

Conclusion: 这是深度学习在驾驶音频导航中的首次大规模应用，推动智能交通与驾驶辅助技术发展。

Abstract: Turn-by-turn (TBT) navigation systems are integral to modern driving experiences, providing real-time audio instructions to guide drivers safely to destinations. However, existing audio instruction policy often relies on rule-based approaches that struggle to balance informational content with cognitive load, potentially leading to driver confusion or missed turns in complex environments. To overcome these difficulties, we first model the generation of navigation instructions as a multi-task learning problem by decomposing the audio content into combinations of modular elements. Then, we propose a novel deep learning framework that leverages the powerful spatiotemporal information processing capabilities of Transformers and the strong multi-task learning abilities of Mixture of Experts (MoE) to generate real-time, context-aware audio instructions for TBT driving navigation. A cloud-edge collaborative architecture is implemented to handle the computational demands of the model, ensuring scalability and real-time performance for practical applications. Experimental results in the real world demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces the yaw rate (the proportion of vehicles deviating from navigation routes) compared to traditional methods, delivering clearer and more effective audio instructions. This is the first large-scale application of deep learning in driving audio navigation, marking a substantial advancement in intelligent transportation and driving assistance technologies.

</details>


### [303] [Unsupervised Multi-Scale Gromov-Wasserstein Hypergraph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29635)
*Lutz Oettershagen,Honglian Wang,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于过滤与跨尺度最优传输的无监督超图对齐方法，无需节点特征或种子，通过多尺度Gromov-Wasserstein目标联合对齐多个过滤级别，兼顾高阶结构与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图对齐方法要么直接建模高阶交互但计算昂贵且不适用于非均匀超图，要么通过图约简损失结构信息；团展开丢失超边细节，二分展开引入额外节点增大规模。

Method: FALCON构建过滤诱导的团共现不相似度矩阵序列，用共享传输计划联合优化多尺度Gromov-Wasserstein目标，避免二分展开的辅助节点，同时保持超边信息。

Result: 在真实世界超图扰动基准上，几乎全面优于强图对齐和超图对齐基线，对结构噪声鲁棒。

Conclusion: 多尺度过滤结合共享最优传输是超图对齐的高效稳健方案，兼顾高阶保真与可扩展性。

Abstract: We study unsupervised hypergraph alignment, where the goal is to infer node correspondences between two hypergraphs using only structural information, without node features, labels, seed matches, or side information. Direct higher-order formulations can represent hyperedge interactions faithfully, but they can be computationally demanding and cumbersome for non-uniform hypergraphs. Graph-reduction approaches introduce a different challenge: clique expansions keep the alignment problem on the original node set but collapse all hyperedge evidence into one pairwise graph, whereas bipartite expansions preserve incidence structure but enlarge the problem from nodes to nodes plus hyperedges. We introduce FALCON (Filtration-based hypergrAph aLignment via Cross-scale Optimal traNsport), an unsupervised optimal-transport framework for hypergraph alignment. Instead of representing each hypergraph by a single collapsed clique graph, FALCON constructs a filtration-induced sequence of clique-based co-occurrence dissimilarity matrices and jointly aligns all levels through one shared multi-scale Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) objective. The shared transport plan enforces a globally consistent node correspondence across filtration levels while avoiding the auxiliary hyperedge nodes introduced by bipartite expansion. Experiments on perturbation benchmarks derived from real-world hypergraphs show that FALCON is robust to structural noise and in almost all cases outperforms strong graph- and hypergraph-alignment baselines.

</details>


### [304] [Clustering as Approximation by Constrained Projectors: Theory and Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29102)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一理论框架，证明k-means、模糊c-means、核k-means、核FCM和谱聚类等聚类方法均可表示为作用于信号派生矩阵的结构化低秩投影器，并建立了统一优化模板，推导出测地凸性、扰动界和精确恢复保证等理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法虽形式多样，但缺乏统一的代数视角，难以从理论上比较其行为。作者旨在通过结构化投影器建立共同基础，揭示各方法间的深层联系，并为稳定性与恢复性提供统一分析。

Method: 将每种聚类方法形式化为 min_{B∈C} ||M - M P_B||_F^2，其中C为不同约束集（如硬划分、模糊权重、核诱导、正交投影），从而将问题统一为低秩投影优化，并基于此推导理论性质。

Result: 获得了非平凡的理论成果：投影流形上的测地凸性、对矩阵噪声的扰动界、理想块模型下的精确恢复条件，并解释了不同聚类族在何种条件下收敛到相同最优子空间及小簇间泄漏导致的偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作为聚类分析提供了一个以理论为先的统一基础，强调结构化投影器的核心作用，有助于深入理解聚类方法的本质联系与差异。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified theoretical framework showing that a broad family of clustering methods, including k-means, fuzzy c-means, kernel k-means, kernel FCM, and spectral clustering, can all be expressed as structured low-rank projectors acting on a signal-derived matrix. By formulating each method as an instance of min over B in C of ||M - M P_B||_F^2, with different constraint sets C, we establish a common optimization template that clarifies the algebraic links among hard, fuzzy, kernel-induced, and orthonormal projections. Within this framework, we derive non-trivial theoretical results, including geodesic convexity properties on the projection manifold, perturbation bounds quantifying stability to matrix noise, and exact recovery guarantees under ideal block-model conditions. The analysis further explains when different clustering families collapse to the same optimal subspace and how deviations arise under small inter-cluster leakage. Overall, the work provides a coherent, theory-first foundation for understanding clustering through structured projectors.

</details>


### [305] [An Explainable Coherence Score for Detecting Temporal Inconsistencies in Political News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29175)
*Marius Nicusor Pantea,Adrian Groza*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出时间一致性得分（TCS），通过四阶段流水线检测新闻中的时间不一致信息，在100篇文章基准上达到0.909精确率，并提供可解释的细粒度证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于风格（lexical）的假新闻检测器无法识别时间不一致类政治虚假信息，因为一篇措辞良好但带单一错误日期的文章不携带词汇上的虚假信号。

Method: 四阶段流水线：抽取时间事实→构建时间知识图谱→通过内部一致性规则（基于Allen区间代数的8个检查器）与外部参考源（本地1,256条事实库到Wikidata SPARQL）进行分层验证→聚合得分并生成自然语言解释。

Result: 在100篇注入时间错误的新闻文章基准上，选定操作阈值下精确率达0.909，仅有一个残留假阳性，阈值专为人在回路核查场景调整（假报警代价高于漏检）。

Conclusion: TCS提供连续、可解释、精细的时间一致性度量，优于仅输出二元标签的词汇基线，适合辅助人工事实核查。

Abstract: Temporal inconsistencies, such as mandates attributed outside their real interval, events presented as past before they occurred, or inverted causal sequences, are a form of political disinformation that evades style-based fake news detectors: a well-written article with a single wrong date carries no lexical signal of falsehood. This paper introduces the Temporal Coherence Score (TCS), a continuous, intrinsically interpretable metric that quantifies the temporal coherence of a news article, computed by a four-stage pipeline: extraction of temporal facts, construction of a temporal knowledge graph, hierarchical verification against internal consistency rules and external reference sources, and score aggregation with automatically generated explanations. Verification combines eight internal checkers derived from Allen's interval algebra with a five-level external hierarchy ranging from a locally stored reference knowledge base of 1{,}256 curated political facts to live Wikidata SPARQL queries. On a benchmark of 100 political news articles with injected temporal errors, the system reaches a precision of 0.909 at the selected operating threshold, with a single residual false positive, a profile deliberately tuned for human-in-the-loop fact-checking assistance, where false alarms are costlier than missed detections. Unlike lexical baselines that output only a binary label, every flagged article is accompanied by the inconsistency type, the entities involved, and the reference source that contradicts the claim.

</details>


### [306] [How Identity and Opinion Shape Political Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29198)
*Li-Ni Fu,Chang-Chih Meng,Chien-Hua Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个框架，区分大语言模型政治谄媚的两种触发机制（观点与身份），通过450个政治困境测试13个模型，发现两者相互独立且影响呈次加性，系统设定仅改变基线立场，表明模型政治立场是交互可塑的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政治行为基准多依赖封闭式问题，未充分捕捉模型在用户提供背景信息时立场调整的动态性，且未区分观点与身份线索的独立影响。

Method: 构建450个人工校验的政治困境作为控制探针，分离观点（显性叙述）与身份（人口统计标签）两种谄媚触发因素，评估13个指令微调LLM，并分析系统级人设的作用。

Result: 发现观点与身份引发的谄媚不相关；两者同时出现时效应呈次加性；系统人设主要改变基线立场，对用户观点或身份导致的立场变化影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM政治立场并非固定特征，而是互动中可操纵的，个性化服务可能放大身份或观点条件性的行为转变。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly encourage users to disclose personal profiles for tailored assistance, measuring their political alignment becomes increasingly important. However, many existing benchmarks for assessing political behavior rely on closed-ended questions and do not fully capture how a model's stance may adapt to user-provided context during interaction. We introduce a framework that disentangles two distinct triggers of political sycophancy: opinion (aligning with explicit narratives) and identity (stereotyping based on demographic labels). Using 450 manually-checked political dilemmas as controlled probes, we evaluate 13 instruction-tuned LLMs. We uncover a dissociation: a model's susceptibility to explicit opinions does not necessarily predict its susceptibility to identity cues, and vice versa. When both signals are present, their effects are generally sub-additive rather than simply additive. Additionally, system-level personas primarily shift a model's baseline stance while having limited effect on the stance shift caused by user opinion or identity. Ultimately, our results suggest that LLM political stance is interactively and steerably vulnerable rather than being a fixed trait, highlighting how personalization may amplify identity- or opinion-conditioned shifts in the model's behaviors.

</details>


### [307] [Imag-Eval: a language-grounded framework for interpretable Text-to-Image instruction following evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29210)
*Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis,David Jaramillo Duque*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了Imag-Eval基准，通过独立变化实例数量和规则组合来解耦语言复杂性与组合难度，发现组合困难主要由规则数量及其绑定决定而非提示长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I评估受限于难以解释且诊断性不足的基准，忽视了全局不连贯等关键失败模式，且提示难度控制单一维度。

Method: 构建1,140个提示和8,842条组合规则的受控基准，分别独立变化实例数量和约束组合，避免错误传播，并辅以2,000多条并发基准提示进行验证。

Result: 在多个SOTA模型上评估，结果显示结构化技能的组合难度主要受接地规则数量及其实例绑定影响，而提示长度作用有限。

Conclusion: Imag-Eval提供细粒度可解释的跨模态指令跟随失败分析，为T2I评估提供新视角，强调规则与实例绑定的核心作用。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models have recently achieved impressive visual fidelity, yet their evaluation remains constrained by benchmarks that are often difficult to interpret and insufficiently diagnostic. Existing skill-based evaluations tend to overlook critical failure modes that strongly impact usability but fall outside standard taxonomies, such as global incoherence arising from missing parts or physically implausible configurations (e.g., floating objects). In addition, prompt difficulty is typically controlled along a single dimension; either prompt length or the number of elements to generate. To address these limitations, we introduce Imag-Eval, a controlled benchmark designed to assess how T2I models ground compositional natural-language instructions into visual outputs. Unlike prior work that conflates surface linguistic complexity with compositional difficulty, Imag-Eval explicitly seeks to disentangles these factors by independently varying both the number of instances and the combination of constraints (rules), while avoiding error propagation. This design enables fine-grained and interpretable analysis of where cross-modal instruction following fails. Our benchmark comprises 1,140 prompts and 8,842 combined rules, and we evaluate it on several state-of-the-art models. Complementing this analysis with an additional study of over 2,000 prompts from a concurrent benchmark, our results suggest that, for structured skills, compositional difficulty is primarily governed by the number of grounded rules and their binding to instances,, rather than by prompt length alone.

</details>


### [308] [Computational Depth Measurement in Thermographic Video: Overcoming Spatial Overfitting via Spatio-Temporal Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29223)
*Zain Ul Abidin,Habeeban Memon,Junaid Ahmed*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出时空解耦架构，通过正则化XGBoost实现CFRP亚表面分层深度的高精度测量，MAE为0.056 mm，并生成三维缺陷模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学脉冲热成像只能提供二维热视频，深度需从时间热扩散推断，但校准缺陷按规则网格分布时回归模型易记忆空间几何而非物理关系，导致跨样本泛化失败。

Method: 先分割定位缺陷区域，再对热响应空间平均并提取16个物理信息特征（时间、能量、统计、几何），隐藏像素坐标，用四种回归模型（RF、GBM、Adv-MLP、XGBoost）进行深度预测，其中XGBoost采用L1/L2正则化和列采样，最后将预测深度与掩膜合并生成Delaunay三角化三维模型。

Result: 未正则化树和过参数化Adv-MLP在几何偏移下误差超0.5 mm；正则化XGBoost保持跨样本校准，MAE为0.056 mm，RMSE为0.085 mm，三维建模耗时3-5秒/样本。

Conclusion: 数学正则化和时空解耦可有效减少热视频深度回归中的空间记忆，提高跨样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate through-thickness measurement of subsurface delamination depth in Carbon Fiber Reinforced Polymer (CFRP) is important for structural assessment because defect location determines affected load-bearing layers. Optical pulsed thermography (OPT) provides a two-dimensional thermal video rather than volumetric measurements, so depth must be inferred from temporal heat-diffusion responses. A challenge is spatial dataset bias: when calibration defects follow regular grids, regression models may memorize their geometry instead of learning physical relationship between thermal decay and depth. This work introduces a spatio-temporal decoupling architecture that separates spatial defect localization from temporal depth measurement. Defect regions are first localized using segmentation methods, after which thermal responses are spatially averaged and converted into sixteen physics-informed temporal, energy, statistical, and geometric features. These features expose the one-dimensional heat-conduction relationship while withholding pixel coordinates from the depth model. Four regression models are evaluated using specimen-level cross-validation: Random Forest (RF), Gradient Boosting Machine (GBM), Advanced Multi-Layer Perceptron (Adv-MLP), and XGBoost. Unregularized trees and over-parameterized Adv-MLP exhibit calibration collapse under geometric shifts, with errors exceeding 0.5 mm. In contrast, regularized XGBoost with L1/L2 penalties and column sampling maintains cross-specimen calibration, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.056 mm and root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.085 mm. Predicted depths are merged with masks to generate Delaunay-triangulated three-dimensional defect models in three to five seconds per specimen. Results show that mathematical regularization and spatio-temporal decoupling reduce spatial memorization in thermal-video depth regression.

</details>


### [309] [Partially Linear Autoencoders for Manifold Learning and Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29867)
*Louen Pottier,Louis Lesueur,Anders Thorin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 自编码器中，线性编码器在解码器保持非线性时，能保留大部分表示能力，且重建质量接近全非线性自编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自编码器中编码器的具体作用，以及在不降低精度的前提下，编码器可被约束为线性的程度。

Method: 对比四种自编码器架构（全非线性AE、线性编码器Lenc-AE、线性解码器Ldec-AE、全线性LAE），在合成流形、计算力学数据集和MNIST等真实图像数据上评估。

Result: Lenc-AE一致优于Ldec-AE和LAE，重建质量与全非线性AE相当，同时具有更简洁和可解释的潜在表示。

Conclusion: 非线性解码器是流形学习的关键组件，而非编码器；并给出了线性编码器适用的几何条件及其局限性。

Abstract: Autoencoders are widely used for nonlinear dimensionality reduction and manifold learning. While most common implementations rely on both nonlinear encoders and decoders, we investigate the specific role of the encoder and the extent to which it can be constrained to be linear without reducing accuracy. We conduct a comparative study on four autoencoder architectures: standard fully nonlinear autoencoders (AE), linear-encoder autoencoders (Lenc-AE), linear-decoder autoencoders (Ldec-AE), and fully linear autoencoders (LAE), evaluated on synthetic manifolds, computational mechanics data sets, and real-world image data sets including MNIST. We demonstrate that imposing a linear encoder preserves most of the representational capacity of the autoencoder, provided the decoder remains nonlinear. In particular, Lenc-AE consistently outperforms both Ldec-AE and LAE, and achieves reconstruction quality comparable to fully nonlinear AE, while offering advantages in terms of parsimony and interpretability of the latent representation. These results suggest that the nonlinear decoder is the critical component for manifold learning, rather than the encoder. A geometric interpretation of this finding is developed, which identifies the precise conditions under which a linear encoder is sufficient, and the specific manifold configurations that expose its limitations.

</details>


### [310] [Localizing Emergent Failures in Agentic AI: Recovering Minimal Repair Families via Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29228)
*Bingjie Li,Yumeng Song,Zhongming Yao,Tianyi Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出最小修复族恢复（MRFR）任务，并设计图约束联合重放（GCJR）方法，通过执行依赖图切片和候选验证，精确恢复所有包含最小事件集的修复方案，在基准和LLM试点中实现100%家族精确匹配同时显著减少重放/模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐点归因无法区分联合必要的修复与单一替代修复，导致多智能体交互失败根因识别不完整。

Method: 从执行依赖图提取失败相关事件，构建图可行的单事件和双事件候选，通过与干净对应事件配对重放验证，在声明的图域内对固定重放结果保持精确性。

Result: 在120-DAG基准的90个范围内案例中，家族精确匹配达1.000，平均重放次数从56.3降至25.3（降55.1%）；在24例四智能体LLM试点中同样达1.000，平均模型调用从21.0降至10.0（降52.4%），且单事件重放会遗漏联合必要修复。

Conclusion: GCJR有效弥补点式归因不足，以更高效方式精确恢复最小修复族，验证了图约束联合重放在多智能体故障诊断中的价值。

Abstract: Failures in agentic AI systems can arise from interactions among messages exchanged by multiple large language model (LLM) agents. Pointwise attribution cannot distinguish a jointly necessary repair from alternative singleton repairs. We formulate Minimal Repair Family Recovery (MRFR): recovering all inclusion-minimal event sets whose counterfactual replay restores task success within a declared size bound. We propose Graph-Constrained Joint Replay (GCJR), which slices failure-relevant events from an execution dependency graph, constructs graph-feasible singleton and pair candidates, and verifies them by replay with paired clean counterparts. For fixed replay outcomes, GCJR is exact within its declared graph domain. On 90 in-scope cases from a 120-DAG controlled benchmark, GCJR achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match while reducing mean replay calls from 56.3 to 25.3 (55.1%) relative to exhaustive search. On a 24-case, four-agent LLM pilot, it again achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match and reduces mean model calls from 21.0 to 10.0 (52.4%); single-event replay misses jointly necessary repairs.

</details>


### [311] [Validating FKG.in: Soundness Assessment in LLM-Augmented Indian Food Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29249)
*Saransh Kumar Gupta,Armaan Shah,Lipika Dey,Partha Pratim Das,Ramesh Jain*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个半自动化的声音评估流程，用于验证由LLM从非正式烹饪来源提取和增强的结构化食谱数据，通过多阶段方法解决结构、语义和逻辑不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的食谱内容可能存在幻觉成分、数量错误或不合理的文化组合，限制其在下游应用和知识图谱构建中的可用性。

Method: 多阶段流水线，结合形式语法、基于词汇的检查、统计启发式、基于Set Transformer的连贯性建模和基于检索的验证，并以FKG.in印度食物知识图谱为开发背景。

Result: 在印度食谱上评估，但方法可推广到更广泛的多语言和多文化烹饪领域，提供实用、可审计且应用无关的验证框架。

Conclusion: 该框架通过验证LLM增强的食谱数据，加强了机器可读食物知识基础设施的基础，适用于LLM生成内容时代。

Abstract: The online culinary ecosystem is increasingly populated by recipe content generated, modified, or summarized by Large Language Models (LLMs). While often plausible, such outputs may contain hallucinated ingredients, misrepresented quantities, or culturally implausible combinations, limiting their suitability for downstream applications and knowledge graph construction. In this paper, we present a semi-automated soundness assessment workflow for validating structured recipe data extracted and augmented by LLMs from informal culinary sources. Developed as part of FKG.in, a knowledge graph of Indian food, the pipeline identifies and addresses common failure modes, including structural inconsistencies, semantic and logical incoherence, and deviations from the source text, through a multi-stage process combining formal grammars, vocabulary-based checks, statistical heuristics, Set Transformer-based coherence modeling, and retrieval-based verification. Although evaluated on Indian recipes, the proposed methods are applicable to broader multilingual and multicultural culinary domains. We provide a practical, auditable, and application-agnostic framework for validating LLM-augmented recipe data, thereby strengthening the foundations of machine-readable food knowledge infrastructures in the era of LLM-generated content.

</details>


### [312] [Structural Hierarchy and Geometry in Molecular Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29886)
*David Sulu,Lorenzo Di Fruscia,Jana M. Weber*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分子自监督学习中显式使用Bemis-Murcko骨架作为监督信号对分子嵌入的影响，并比较了欧几里得与洛伦兹对比学习目标下的效果差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子自监督学习仅依赖化学结构决定嵌入相似性，但未充分探讨显式引入骨架信息是否能改变模型所学内容及其与嵌入几何的关系。

Method: 通过对比两种增强强度下，使用骨架监督与无监督基线模型，并分别采用欧几里得和洛伦兹对比目标进行训练，分析嵌入空间的组织方式及下游任务性能。

Result: 骨架监督使模型一致地按相同或结构相关的骨架组织分子，并提升多个性质预测任务的表现，但增益因任务而异；洛伦兹目标下骨架的组织效果更强，但两种几何无整体优劣。

Conclusion: 显式教授分子与结构核心的关系能可靠塑造嵌入空间组织，但该组织的实用性取决于具体下游任务。

Abstract: Molecular self-supervised learning uses chemical structures to guide which molecular embeddings should be similar. We study whether explicitly encoding a molecule's Bemis-Murcko scaffold and using it to supervise the molecular embedding changes what the model learns. We further test whether this effect depends on the embedding geometry by comparing Euclidean and Lorentz contrastive objectives. Across two augmentation strengths, scaffold-supervised models consistently organize molecules according to both identical and structurally related scaffolds. The resulting embeddings also improve molecular property prediction on several tasks, while the exact gains depend on the predicted property. The effect of scaffold supervision on molecular organization is stronger under Lorentz objectives, but neither geometry provides a consistent overall advantage. These results show that explicitly teaching the relation between a molecule and its structural core can reliably shape the organization of molecular embedding space, while the extent of usefulness of this organization remains task dependent.

</details>


### [313] [Joint Spatiotemporal Spectral Neural Operators for Learning PDEs on Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29892)
*Abdolmehdi Behroozi,Chaopeng Shen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出GSNO，结合空间图拉普拉斯谱分解与时间傅里叶变换，实现对不规则几何域上PDE解算子的高效学习，无需域变形或自回归滚动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱方法受限于规则域，神经方法需域扭曲/插值或昂贵几何嵌入，难以高效处理不规则几何依赖域上的全局交互。

Method: 将空间图拉普拉斯谱基替代学习型几何嵌入，并与时间傅里叶变换统一为时空谱核，形成图谱神经算子。

Result: 在稳态与非稳态不规则域基准上，以更少参数和运行时间达到高精度，并在网格分辨率与几何族间实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: GSNO为不规则几何域提供几何感知、低参数复杂度的神经算子方案，兼顾精度与泛化效率。

Abstract: Learning solution operators for partial differential equations (PDEs) on irregular and geometry-dependent domains remains a central challenge in scientific machine learning. While spectral methods provide strong inductive biases for modeling global interactions, they are typically limited to regular domains, and existing neural approaches often require domain warping, interpolation, or costly geometric embeddings. We introduce the \textbf{Graph Spectral Neural Operator (GSNO)}, a neural operator that combines spatial graph spectral decompositions with temporal Fourier transforms through a unified space--time spectral kernel. This formulation enables globally coherent operator learning on non-Cartesian discretizations without domain warping or autoregressive rollouts. By replacing learned geometric embeddings with a graph Laplacian spectral basis, GSNO provides geometry-aware spectral learning with low parameter complexity. Across steady and unsteady PDE benchmarks on irregular and geometry-dependent domains, GSNO achieves strong accuracy with reduced runtime and parameter counts, while demonstrating robust zero-shot generalization across mesh resolutions and geometry families.

</details>


### [314] [Understanding Deep Learning via Entropy Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29279)
*Li Li,Tong Zhang,Wentao Yu,Zuobin Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出熵空间理论，为深度学习提供统一的数学先验框架，可覆盖模型所有状态并依据信息熵压缩进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习理论落后于实践，缺乏易于理解的统一数学基础。

Method: 引入熵空间，利用拓扑结构覆盖模型所有可能性，设计基本运算与范数，在形式公理框架下证明其为赋范空间，并提出统一坐标系。

Result: 证明了熵空间是赋范空间，坐标系能对模型状态进行坐标化并依据最大信息熵压缩值排序。

Conclusion: 熵空间理论为深度学习数学基础提供了全新先验框架，有助于弥合理论与实践的差距。

Abstract: Deep learning is often criticized for its theoretical research lagging behind practice. To make deep learning easier to understand, the entropy space theory is first introduced here. The entropy space can cover all the possibilities of any deep learning model by topological structure. It is independent of network parameters. Through the designed fundamental operations and norm, entropy space is proven to be a normed space within the formal axiomatic framework. Based on the theory, a unified coordinate system is proposed. It can coordinatize every state of a model and rank them by compression of the maximal value of information entropy. The theory offers a novel priori framework for mathematical fundamentals of deep learning.

</details>


### [315] [Predicting Future Organ Dysfunction in ICU Patients Using Temporal Convolutional Networks on MIMIC-IV Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29301)
*Razan Albouq,Asra Aslam*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究使用时间卷积网络（TCN）预测ICU患者次日SOFA评分，五折交叉验证R²为0.740，优于朴素基线；但SHAP分析显示模型主要依赖最近观测日，本质上是严重度锚定而非序列建模；无监督聚类识别出改善型（58.9%）和持续严重型（41.1%）两种临床表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法多将SOFA评分作为二分类死亡预测的输入特征，而非将其视为连续的临床结局进行直接预测，缺乏对器官功能障碍动态演变的建模。

Method: 使用残差TCN在MIMIC-IV多变量ICU时间序列数据上，以三天滑动窗口预测次日SOFA总分；采用五折交叉验证评估，SHAP进行可解释性分析，并利用无监督轨迹聚类识别患者表型。

Result: TCN取得R²=0.740±0.013、MAE=1.431±0.022，RMSE和R²均优于朴素持久性基线；心血管系统是横断面严重度和急性恶化的最强判别因子；聚类获得改善组（58.9%）和持续严重组（41.1%），两组在心血管、肝脏、凝血和肾脏受累上显著不同。

Conclusion: TCN能从ICU生理数据中提取有效预测信号，但短输入窗口和完整病例选择偏倚限制了临床实用性；未来需探索更长输入视野、改进缺失数据处理策略，并进行外部验证。

Abstract: Predicting future organ dysfunction in Intensive Care Unit (ICU) patients is critical for early clinical intervention, yet existing machine learning approaches have largely treated the Sequential Organ Failure Assessment (SOFA) score as an input to binary mortality prediction rather than as a continuous clinical outcome in its own right. We investigate the extent to which a Temporal Convolutional Net work (TCN) can predict next-day SOFA scores from multivariate ICU time-series data extracted from MIMIC-IV, characterise the relative contribution of each organ system to total SOFA variance and deterioration, and identify distinct trajectory patterns across ICU stays. A residual TCN trained on three-day sliding windows achieved a five-fold cross-validation R2 of 0.740 +- 0.013 and MAE of 1.431 +- 0.022, outperforming a naive persistence baseline on RMSE and R2. SHAP interpretability analysis revealed that the model functions primarily as a severity-anchoring mechanism rather than a true sequence model, with predictions dominated almost entirely by the most recent observation day. Cardiovascular dysfunction emerged as the strongest discriminator of both cross-sectional severity and acute deterioration, and unsupervised trajectory clustering identified two clinically meaningful phenotypes, an improving group (58.9%) and a persistently severe group (41.1%), differentiated by cardiovascular, hepatic, coagulation, and renal involvement. We conclude that TCNs can extract meaningful predictive signal from ICU physiological data, but that short input windows and complete-case selection bias currently limit their clinical utility, motivating future work on longer input horizons, alternative missing-data strategies, and external validation.

</details>


### [316] [When 3D Gaussian Splatting Recovers Real Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30054)
*Songhe Wang,David Johnathan Miller*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过首次命中渲染抽象，数学上证明了几何错位会将空间纹理转为高频角信号，从而界定3DGS恢复真实表面的条件：角容量受限时表面解优先，无限时错误广告牌几何可完美解释图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS常过度拟合视角依赖外观，却缺乏对何时恢复真实表面的理论理解。

Method: 建立基于首次命中渲染的数学框架，分离几何与外观，分析视差导致的频率转换，界定可识别窗口。

Result: 合成测试证实广告牌失败出现在高角容量；真实数据在标准捕获下即使高SH阶仍保持表面一致。

Conclusion: 几何可识别性取决于角容量与空间纹理的博弈，实际采集协议下表面解稳定，但需警惕无限角容量的退化风险。

Abstract: When does 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) recover the true scene surface rather than just overfitting view-dependent appearance? We answer this by developing a mathematical framework based on a first-hit rendering abstraction that cleanly isolates geometry from appearance. We prove that geometric misalignment forcefully converts spatial textures into high-frequency angular signals via parallax. This establishes a strict identifiability window: if angular capacity is bounded, surface-consistent solutions are mathematically preferred; if unrestricted, the same images can be perfectly explained by an incorrect, opaque billboard geometry. Experiments on synthetic stress tests confirm this prediction, showing billboard failures emerge precisely at high angular capacities. Conversely, in the real-world datasets we evaluate under standard capture protocols, reconstructions remain surface-consistent even at high SH degrees, which is consistent with the prediction that rich spatial texture can push billboard solutions outside the tested angular-capacity range.

</details>


### [317] [SMOTE-VAR: An Uncertainty-Aware Oversampling Method for Predicting Depression Remission in University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30102)
*Dang Nguyen,Arun Kumar A,Taylor A. Braund,Wu Yi Zheng,Debopriyo Bal,Leonard Hoon,Jill Newby,Helen Christensen,Svetha Venkatesh,Alexis Whitton,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯过程方差的新型过采样方法，用于缓解大学生抑郁症缓解预测中的类别不平衡问题，减少SMOTE生成的无效合成样本，提高非缓解者识别的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学生抑郁症高发，但现有生活方式干预常无法达到症状缓解，且机器学习预测模型面临类别不平衡，SMOTE可能产生无效样本导致误判风险分层，延误治疗。

Method: 利用高斯过程的方差函数估计生成少数类样本的不确定性，过滤或调整高风险合成样本，以降低假阳性。

Result: 在大学生抑郁症数据集上验证，该方法的预测性能优于现有过采样方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能更可靠地识别非缓解者，支持临床快速调整辅助治疗，实现个性化心理保健路径。

Abstract: University students experience disproportionately high rates of common mental health conditions, such as depression, which can impair learning, social functioning, and overall well-being. Although lifestyle interventions such as mindfulness and physical activity can reduce the symptoms, many do not achieve symptomatic remission. Developing new approaches to identify students with poor outcomes could enable earlier and more targeted intervention. Machine learning (ML) methods have increasingly been used to predict remission in depressive patients. However, these ML models often suffer from class imbalance, where there may be an unequal proportion of people in the remitted group relative to the non-remitted group. This imbalance can reduce model accuracy and bias predictions. To address this, studies commonly employ the popular oversampling strategy SMOTE. However, SMOTE has a notable limitation: it may generate invalid synthetic minority samples. In a clinical context, these false positives can lead to incorrect risk stratification, potentially delaying necessary escalated care for patients unlikely to remit. In this paper, we introduce a novel and effective oversampling method that addresses this shortcoming. Our approach leverages the variance function of a Gaussian process to estimate the uncertainty of generated minority samples to reduce false positives. We validate our method on a depression dataset collected from university students and demonstrate that it is better than existing oversampling approaches in predicting remission (i.e., treatment outcome). By improving the reliable identification of non-responders, our method provides a robust computational tool to help clinicians rapidly pivot to adjunctive therapies, thereby personalizing and optimizing mental health care pathways.

</details>


### [318] [Reviving our data foundations is the most disruptive step to data maturity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29368)
*Valentina Carapella,Ernesto Jimenez-Ruiz*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 中小企业应回归知识基础层建设，以低影响数据策略和模块化知识图谱应对AI落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数中小企业受AI炒作影响，急于采用新技术而忽略数据根基，导致AI价值难以实现；同时需用证据说服管理层并减少对现有流程的冲击。

Method: 提出低影响数据策略，适应现存且动态变化的数据流；强调知识图谱技术需以模块化、动态和跨功能方式设计，作为企业AI数据战略的基石。

Result: 未提供实证结果，属于愿景声明，旨在倡导知识基础层优先及知识图谱的务实部署路径。

Conclusion: 知识图谱将成为AI驱动企业数据战略的必需元素，但其设计须灵活整合现有流程，以最小干扰实现长期价值。

Abstract: The most disruptive step that enterprises of small-medium size and maturity can take to make the most of the latest technological advances in AI is to step back from the hype and focus on establishing or reviving a good knowledge foundation layer. It is a hard message to present to the executive team; therefore, it needs to be backed by evidence, and its implementation needs to be of minimal impact on the existing processes. In this vision statement, we discuss how we need to rethink what evidence speaks to the decision-makers and propose a low-impact data strategy that adapts to the existing and ever-changing data flows and processes across the company. We firmly believe that knowledge graph techniques will increasingly become non-negotiable in the data strategy of an AI-powered enterprise, provided that we approach their design in a modular, dynamic and cross-functional way.

</details>


### [319] [Supraglacial Lake Fate Is Knowable Long Before the Season Ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30113)
*Emam Hossain,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 通过截断输入时间窗口，发现格陵兰冰盖冰上湖的四种消融结局（快速排水、慢速排水、原地再冻结、被雪掩埋）可按固定顺序提前识别，其中快速排水最早可在7月15日以92天提前量被分类，且该顺序对多种学习器和评价方式保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星分类器只能在季节结束后准确判断冰上湖的消融结局，而每种结局实际需要多少观测时间从未被量化，这限制了融水是否到达冰床的实时监测能力。

Method: 固定表征和分类器，将输入从5月1日至12月31日按14个截止点截断，每个截止点重新训练，记录各类别F1达到固定目标的最早时间；同时使用多种基线学习器（多数类、54维统计量、触发器方法）和留一盆地验证，并替换机器标签为专家标签测试稳健性，确保所有特征仅利用截止日前的数据。

Result: 结局按固定顺序解析：快速排水在7月15日达到目标（提前92天），慢速排水8月1日（提前75天），掩埋和再冻结分别在44天和30天；五种学习器均再现该顺序，端季准确率差异达18个百分点时依然成立，但用机器标签替代专家标签时顺序失效；截断造成的准确率损失最多1.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 监测系统不应采用单一发布时间，而应针对不同结局设置不同预警日期：快速排水可在7月15日标记，比全季节管线可计算时间早三个月，从而显著提升融水路径的实时监测能力。

Abstract: A supraglacial lake on the Greenland Ice Sheet ends its melt season in one of four ways: it drains rapidly through a hydrofracture, drains slowly across the surface, refreezes in place, or is buried by late-season snowfall. Which one occurs decides whether the meltwater reaches the ice bed. Satellite classifiers recover the outcome accurately but only after the season closes, and how much of a season each outcome actually requires has never been measured. We measure it directly: holding the representation and the classifier fixed, we truncate the input at $14$ cutoffs from 1 May to 31 December, retrain at each, and record the earliest cutoff at which each outcome's per-class $F_1$ reaches a fixed target. The outcomes resolve in a consistent order, two of them months early: rapid drainage by 15 July and slow drainage by 1 August, $92$ and $75$ days ahead of the earliest date a full-season pipeline can be computed at all, with buried and refreeze following at $44$ and $30$ days. Five further learners, from a majority-class floor and $54$ summary statistics to a trigger-based early classifier, leave the ordering intact: every learner that produces a per-class trajectory reproduces it despite end-of-season accuracies differing by up to $18$ percentage points, and it survives leave-one-basin-out evaluation, though not the substitution of machine labels for expert ones in an unseen season. Every feature we compute at day $t$ reads only days up to $t$, at a cost of at most $1.3$ percentage points. A monitoring system should therefore not have one release date: rapid drainage can be flagged on 15 July, three months before a full-season pipeline can be computed at all.

</details>


### [320] [TPR-Attention for Combinatorial Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30124)
*Melisa Civelekoğlu,Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于张量积表示（TPR）的注意力机制，通过在深度学习中嵌入结构化归纳偏置，显著提升了组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型依赖统计相关性，缺乏显式结构表示，在组合泛化任务上表现不佳；人类却能轻松处理已知因素的新组合。因此需要引入能支持系统性泛化的结构组件。

Method: 设计了一种新架构组件——张量积表示注意力机制，将输入分解为角色和填充符的张量积，并通过注意力操作在组合空间中动态绑定和组合这些表示，从而显式利用组合结构。

Result: 在受控的组合任务实验中，TPR注意力机制在组合泛化性能上优于现有架构组件。

Conclusion: 将显式组合结构融入神经注意力是提升系统性泛化的有效途径，为构建能进行系统泛化的模型提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Systematic generalization remains a significant challenge in deep learning. In particular, combinatorial generalization - generalizing to new configurations of known factors of variation - is effortless for humans but difficult for standard neural architectures that rely on statistical correlations rather than explicit structural representations. We introduce a new architectural component that embeds structured inductive bias into deep learning: an attention mechanism operating over tensor-product representations (TPRs). Through controlled experiments on compositional tasks, we show that this TPR-attention mechanism outperforms existing architectural components in combinatorial generalization. These results highlight the value of integrating explicit compositional structure into neural attention and point toward a promising path for models capable of systematic generalization.

</details>


### [321] [EvoGenUI-Bench: Evaluating LLMs as Multi-Turn Generative UI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29387)
*Yue Peng,Lanke Xia,Zihan Wang,Jiahao Ye,Ke Ning,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出EvoGenUI-Bench基准，用于多轮界面维护评估，发现最强模型仅完成37.3%的五轮任务，跨轮保留率低至52.4%，强调生成式UI需从单轮输出转向持续同步维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型能生成交互式网页，但可靠生成式UI要求在用户请求变化时维持可执行工件，缺乏针对多轮界面维护的系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建含150个五轮任务、750轮的三场景基准（信息展示、可执行交互、工具接地外部状态），在浏览器中执行生成工件，结合截图、源码/DOM证据、执行轨迹和运行时日志评估，并提出相邻轮次保留率指标。

Result: 八模型中最强模型轮级通过率74.9%，但五轮完整完成率仅37.3%；工具接地任务APR降至52.4%；诊断显示展示失败源于信息架构，交互失败源于派生状态传播和动作绑定，工具任务额外涉及外部状态接地和需求分解。

Conclusion: 生成式UI评估应重新定义为对界面行为、派生状态、外部状态及助手声明在工件演化中同步性的持续测试，而非孤立输出的质量判断。

Abstract: Large language models can generate interactive web interfaces, but reliable generative UI requires maintaining an executable artifact as user requests evolve. We introduce EvoGenUI-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn interface maintenance comprising 150 five-turn tasks and 750 turns across three scenarios: information presentation, executable interaction, and tool-grounded external state. We execute generated artifacts in a browser and evaluate them using screenshots, source and DOM evidence, actor traces, and runtime logs. Beyond turn-level and episode-level success, we measure cross-turn retention with Adjacent Pass Retention. Across eight models, even the strongest achieves 74.9% Turn Pass while completing only 37.3% of five-turn episodes; APR further falls to 52.4% on tool-grounded tasks. Diagnostic analysis shows that presentation failures center on information architecture, interaction failures on derived-state propagation and affordance binding, and tool-grounded failures additionally involve external-state grounding and requirement decomposition. These results reframe generative UI evaluation from judging isolated outputs to testing whether interface behavior, derived state, external state, and assistant claims remain synchronized as the artifact evolves.

</details>


### [322] [Converse and Collision-Based Achievability for Node Localization with Hybrid Distance-Spectral Graph Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30152)
*Zimo Yan,Yifan Li,Hao Li,Zheng Xie,Chang Liu,Zheming Tu,Yuan Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究混合距离-谱图位置编码的节点可辨识性，提出精确碰撞分解与信息度量，并在随机正则图和真实树结构上验证其优于单一编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图位置编码广泛应用但缺乏理论明确性，需回答何时编码能唯一识别节点，并设计更有效的混合编码。

Method: 结合锚点距离分布与量化低频拉普拉斯能量坐标，建立观测映射，推导碰撞因子分解及条件充分性，并引入高斯波代理分析。

Result: 在随机正则图上给出显式判据，实验显示I_H/log n校准定位成功率，树结构探测中混合编码恢复语法几何更优。

Conclusion: 混合距离-谱编码在理论上更可辨识，实践中对结构恢复有显著优势，为图编码设计提供指导。

Abstract: Graph positional encodings are widely used in graph neural networks and graph Transformers, yet it remains unclear when the code itself can identify nodes. We study a hybrid distance-spectral encoding that combines anchor-distance profiles with quantized low-frequency Laplacian-energy coordinates. Treating the encoding as an observation map yields a simplex-refined converse, an exact collision factorization \(κ_H=κ_Dκ_{S|D}\), and the collision information \(I_H=-\logκ_D-\logκ_{S|D}\). On random regular graphs, the criterion is made explicit through a bounded-correlation Gaussian-wave surrogate; for actual Laplacian-energy coordinates, we give the distance-conditioned spectral collision condition sufficient for conditional actual-coordinate achievability. Experiments show that \(I_H/\log n\) calibrates localization success, and PE-only structural task probes on Universal Dependencies trees show that hybrid encodings better recover syntactic-tree geometry than distance-only or spectral-only baselines.

</details>


### [323] [Diffusion-Based Refinement for Kilometer-Scale Probabilistic Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30205)
*Dohyun Park,Changhoon Song,Tengyuan Chang,Yoo-Geun Ham,Youngjoon Hong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种条件残差扩散框架，将确定性雷达临近预报转为1公里概率集合，修正系统误差并提升极端降水捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部极端降水引发城市洪涝和滑坡，但现有预报难以同时具备精细空间细节与概率不确定性。

Method: 基于条件残差扩散，以预报和先前雷达观测为条件，使集合均值修正基线而非扰动，成员表征未解析的小尺度变率。

Result: 30成员集合在2023年两次事件中恢复38-47%漏报强降水像素，保留约95%正确检测，误报率低于1%；单GPU单小时预报仅需3.4秒，法国数据集上表现一致提升。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持计算效率的同时显著提升极端降水概率预报质量，具有跨区域适用性。

Abstract: Localized extreme precipitation is a major trigger of urban flash floods and landslides, yet producing nowcasts that combine fine spatial detail with probabilistic uncertainty remains challenging. Here we introduce exPreCast-ENS, a conditional residual diffusion framework that transforms the deterministic 4 km radar nowcaster exPreCast into a 1 km probabilistic ensemble while correcting systematic forecast errors. Conditioning on both the forecast and preceding radar observations lets the ensemble-mean correct the baseline rather than perturb it, while members represent unresolved fine-scale variability. Over the Korean Peninsula, skill improves with ensemble size. In two high-impact events in 2023, a 30-member ensemble recovers 38-47% of heavy-rain pixels missed by exPreCast while retaining approximately 95% of its correct detections and alarming on under 1% of the pixels it correctly left clear. The method generates a 1-h forecast in 3.4 s on a single GPU and yields consistent improvements on the French regional MeteoNet radar dataset.

</details>


### [324] [Exact Recovery Thresholds for Weighted Data Selection in Vector-Valued Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30254)
*Guangjian Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文解决了COLT 2025开放问题中回归任务数据选择的阈值部分，证明向量值线性回归中最小加权样本预算为n*=(m+1)d，并确定了加权选择轮廓的几个关键值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在精确刻画在向量值线性回归中，需要多少加权样本才能在所有有限数据集上恢复全数据损失，填补标量情形之外的空白，并纠正先前预印本中的错误。

Method: 采用经验风险最小化（最小Frobenius范数），通过固定基锥压缩引理、最大证书的行列式面刚性定理和零均值加权点系统的稀疏化引理等新技术，结合对偶性和矩问题分析。

Result: 证明了阈值n*=(m+1)d；近阈值时F_w=1+1/(dm^2)，跨距预算时F_w=d+1；对(2,2)情形给出上下界[13/8,15/8]和[5/4,3/2]，并将猜想简化为至多七个原子的圆上矩问题；还构造了反例反驳错误声称。

Conclusion: 完整解决了阈值部分，提出独立价值的证明技术，为中间单元提供了强结构证据，并修正了文献中的错误结论。

Abstract: We resolve the threshold part of Question 4 of the COLT 2025 open problem "Data Selection for Regression Tasks" of Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich and Yehudayoff. In vector-valued linear regression with square loss $\ell_{(x,y)}(W)=|Wx-y|_2^2$, where $x\in\mathbb{R}^d$, $y\in\mathbb{R}^m$ and the learner is the empirical risk minimizer of minimal Frobenius norm, we prove that the minimal budget of weighted examples that recovers the full-data loss on every finite dataset is exactly $n^*(d,m)=(m+1)d$. We further determine two more values of the weighted selection profile $F_w(d,m,n)$: at the near-threshold budget, $F_w(d,m,(m+1)d-1)=1+\frac{1}{dm^2}$, and at the spanning budget, $F_w(d,m,d)=d+1$ for every $m$, while $F_w(d,m,n)=\infty$ for $n<d$. For the smallest open intermediate cell $(d,m)=(2,2)$ we prove $F_w(2,2,3)\in[13/8,15/8]$ and $F_w(2,2,4)\in[5/4,3/2]$, reduce the conjectured exact values $13/8$ and $5/4$ to a finite moment problem on the circle with at most seven atoms, and establish strong structural evidence for the conjecture. The upper-bound techniques (a fixed-basis conic compression lemma, a determinant-facet rigidity theorem for maximal certificates, and sharp sparsification lemmas for zero-mean weighted point systems) are of independent interest. As a byproduct we correct an erroneous claim circulating in a recent unrefereed preprint, exhibiting an explicit dataset with $m=2$ on which no weighted selection of $2d$ points recovers the optimal loss. All results are new only for $m\ge 2$; the scalar case $m=1$ is due to Hanneke et al.

</details>


### [325] [Multivariate Scientific Data Compression with Learned Cross-Variable Latent Decorrelation and Autoregressive Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30262)
*Liangji Zhu,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CAESAR-LDAR，一种误差受控的多变量科学数据学习压缩器，通过可逆正交变换和因果自回归先验分别捕获潜空间中的全局跨通道与局部空间结构，在不同数据上实现最优或近最优率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟产生统计特性和依赖关系异构的多物理场，现有学习压缩器要么独立编码各场，要么用共享编码器却未显式建模潜空间剩余结构，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: 在共享CAESAR-V骨干上增加两个互补模块：一是用矩阵指数参数化的可训练正交变换，在潜通道间重组依赖且精确可逆；二是因果自回归分层先验，捕获变换后剩余的局部空间结构；所有变体统一使用残差校正阶段以满足重建容差。

Result: 在燃烧、气候和湍流数据上，潜空间去相关在存在显著线性跨通道依赖时最有效，自回归建模在剩余结构以局部/空间为主时有效；两者组合在多数数据集上达到最强或近最强率失真性能，全局变换计算开销小，自回归编码吞吐成本较高。

Conclusion: 多变量科学压缩的实用设计原则是：当潜空间中存在可测量的全局跨通道依赖时加以利用，并用局部概率上下文作为跨数据范围的补充机制。

Abstract: Scientific simulations generate collections of physical fields with heterogeneous statistics and dependencies, yet learned compressors often encode those fields independently or rely on a shared encoder without explicitly modeling the structure that remains in latent space. We present CAESAR-LDAR, an error-controlled multivariate learned compressor that augments a shared CAESAR-V backbone with two complementary mechanisms: a trainable orthogonal transform that reorganizes dependence across aligned latent channels, and a causal autoregressive hierarchical prior that captures local spatial structure left after transformation. Orthogonality is maintained through a matrix-exponential parameterization, making the transform exactly invertible without an additional penalty. A common residual-correction stage is applied uniformly to all variants to enforce the requested reconstruction tolerance.
  Experiments across combustion, climate, and turbulence data show that the two mechanisms are useful in different regimes. Latent decorrelation helps most when substantial linear cross-channel dependence survives the nonlinear encoder, whereas autoregressive modeling remains effective when the remaining structure is primarily local or spatial. Their combination provides the strongest or near-strongest rate-distortion performance across the evaluated datasets. The global transform adds little computational overhead, while autoregressive coding introduces a larger throughput tradeoff. More broadly, the results suggest a practical design principle for multivariate scientific compression: exploit global cross-channel dependence when it is measurably present in latent space, and use local probabilistic context as a complementary mechanism across a wider range of data regimes.

</details>


### [326] [Mode Connectivity Beyond Classifiers: Evidence from Generative and Contrastive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30366)
*Chengzheyi Yao,Yongzhao Zhang,Yongding Tian*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首次将模式连通性扩展到生成模型（DDPM）与对比学习模型（NanoCLIP），提出架构感知的连接构建算法，实证发现独立训练的模式间存在低损失路径，为理解现代复杂模型的损失景观几何特性提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式连通性研究主要局限于分类模型，对于生成模型和对比学习模型等现代复杂架构，其损失景观是否具有类似的几何性质尚不清楚，因此需要扩展研究边界。

Method: 针对DDPM和CLIP的独特架构，设计架构感知的连接构建算法，用于在独立训练的模式间寻找连续低损失路径。

Result: 实验首次成功在独立训练的DDPM和NanoCLIP模式间发现模式连通性，验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明现代生成与对比模型同样存在模式连通性，为理解其损失景观几何性质提供了新视角，并为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The loss landscape of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) exhibits highly complex and non-convex properties. Recent studies have revealed the phenomenon of mode connectivity, demonstrating that independently trained network modes can be connected via a continuous low-loss path. However, existing mode connectivity research is predominantly confined to classifier-based models, leaving it an open question whether similar geometric properties exist in modern complex models. In this paper, we extend the boundaries of mode connectivity to generative and contrastive domains (specifically DDPM and NanoCLIP). Addressing the unique architecture of DDPM and CLIP, we propose an architecture-aware connection building algorithm. Extensive empirical results demonstrate for the first time that we successfully discover mode connectivity between independently trained DDPM and NanoCLIP modes. Our work provides a novel perspective for understanding the geometric properties of the loss landscapes in modern generative and contrastive models.

</details>


### [327] [An Open-Source, Event-Driven Pipeline for Cryptocurrency Market Data: Ingestion, Forecasting, and On-Chain Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29973)
*Basil Sajid Shaikh,Melrick Mascarenhas,Nuzhat Faiz Shaikh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全开源的加密货币数据处理管道，模拟云原生事件驱动系统，利用Kafka和文件监听在普通硬件上实现历史数据分区、异步消费与ETL，并基于结果进行比特币价格预测和以太坊欺诈检测建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场产生高频多源数据，处理成本高，除非团队已有商业级流处理和仓储基础设施，否则难以开展研究。

Method: 采用Apache Kafka和文件系统监听轮询器替代托管云触发器，构建事件驱动管道。数据按小时/分钟分区，通过两组独立消费者（审计日志与Spark触发ETL）异步处理，结果存入PostgreSQL仓库，含历史、聚合模式和资产专用数据集。

Result: 使用比特币数据集成ARIMA与单层LSTM进行价格预测对比；对以太坊公共欺诈检测基准应用随机森林和梯度提升分类器，并报告架构、方法及指标。

Conclusion: 研究明确讨论了不同预测时域比较和静态已标注数据集评估欺诈检测的局限性，为低成本加密货币数据分析提供参考案例。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets generate high-frequency, multi-source data that is expensive to work with unless a team already has commercial-grade streaming and warehousing infrastructure in place. This paper describes a fully open-source pipeline that reproduces the behavior of a cloud-native, event-driven system -- file arrival triggering a message, a message triggering compute -- entirely on commodity hardware, using Apache Kafka and a filesystem-watching poller in place of managed cloud triggers. The pipeline partitions historical Gemini exchange data into hourly and minutely files, ingests them asynchronously through two independently grouped Kafka consumers (one for audit logging, one for Spark-triggered ETL), and lands cleaned output in a PostgreSQL warehouse with historical and aggregated schemas plus asset-specific data marts. We use the resulting Bitcoin data mart to compare a seasonal ARIMA model against a single-layer LSTM network for price forecasting, and separately apply Random Forest and Gradient Boosting classifiers, with additional engineered features, to the public Ethereum fraud detection benchmark introduced by Farrugia et al. We report the architecture, the modeling methodology, and the resulting metrics, and we are explicit about the limitations of comparing forecasts issued at different horizons and of evaluating fraud detection on a static, already-labeled dataset.

</details>


### [328] [Can LLM Agents Discover? Evaluating Creativity on ML Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30047)
*Shitanshu Bhushan,Yunxiang Zhang,Lu Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出评估多轮LLM研究代理创造力的框架，通过P-Creativity、H-Creativity和Usefulness三个维度，在Kaggle式ML任务上测试两个代理框架，发现代理在探索后创造力下降，且虽能产生历史新颖解但性能不如人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统声称能自主科学发现，但对其创造力（新颖且有用）的评估仍不明确，需要系统化的框架来检验。

Method: 使用MLE-Bench的10个Kaggle式ML任务，评估AIDE和AIRA-Dojo两个代理框架，开发LLM-as-a-Judge流水线并与人类判断验证相关性，以测量P-Creativity、H-Creativity和Usefulness。

Result: 所有代理在从探索转向利用时P-Creativity下降；LLM在H-Creativity上超过获得奖牌的人类，但任务性能较低。

Conclusion: 当前代理能探索新颖解空间，但无法将新颖性有效转化为性能提升，表明其创造力在实用性方面存在局限。

Abstract: Recent AI systems promise autonomous scientific discovery, claiming to discover algorithms and produce research papers, yet understanding whether they exhibit creativity, the capacity to produce solutions that are both novel and useful, remains an open question. We present a framework for evaluating multi-turn LLM research agents' creativity using ML engineering tasks as a testbed, through three dimensions: P-Creativity (psychological novelty: novel relative to the agent's own prior solutions within a run), H-Creativity (historical novelty: novel relative to the corpus of human solutions), and Usefulness (task performance). Evaluating two agent frameworks, AIDE and AIRA-Dojo, on 10 Kaggle-style machine learning tasks from MLE-Bench, we develop an LLM-as-a-Judge pipeline and verify its strong correlation with human creativity judgments, providing a reliable automated metric for P-Creativity evaluation at scale. Applying this pipeline to agent trajectories, we find: (1) all agents exhibit declining P-Creativity as they transition from exploration to exploitation; (2) LLMs exhibit greater H-Creativity than medal-winning humans, yet achieve lower performance. Our findings reveal that current agents can explore novel regions of the solution space but lack the capacity to convert this novelty into improved task performance.

</details>


### [329] [No Equivariant Architecture Covers All Equivariant Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30417)
*Tīkun Ông*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了等变多头自注意力（MHSA）的结构：其对称群只能通过置换头簇作用，且QK/OV矩阵需满足特定等变约束。进一步证明，任何固定架构若通过多项式参数化实现精确等变，必然导致等变映射类的表达性损失；以D4群和8个注意力头为例，等变轨迹可分解为Ω(C^64)个不可约分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多针对特定群构造等变注意力层，但缺乏对等变MHSA通用结构及其表达性代价的系统性理解。本文旨在回答两个核心问题：等变MHSA的完整结构形式是什么？固定架构实现精确等变是否必然牺牲表达性？

Method: 通过群论和代数几何分析，首先推导等变MHSA的充要条件（头簇置换+QK/OV约束），进而将等变参数集视为Zariski闭集，研究其在约化参数空间中的不可约分解，并借助组合计数（对D4作用的正则表示副本）下界估计不可约分量数量。

Result: 证明了等变参数轨迹由极多不可约分量组成（如D4情形下8个头对应Ω(C^64)个分量），而任何单一固定架构只能覆盖其中一个分量，因此无法完全表达所有等变映射。

Conclusion: 精确等变与表达性之间存在根本性权衡：固定参数化架构无法同时实现完整等变覆盖和等变函数类的充分表达，这为设计等变注意力机制提供了理论边界参考。

Abstract: We give a complete characterization of equivariant multi-head self-attention (MHSA): if an MHSA layer is equivariant to a symmetry group $G$, then $G$ can only act by permuting head-clusters, with QK and OV matrices satisfying an equivariance constraint tied to the group action. As a consequence, we prove that any fixed MHSA architecture that achieves exact equivariance by polynomially parameterizing unconstrained MHSA parameters inevitably leads to expressivity loss within the class of equivariant maps: the equivariance locus of unconstrained MHSA forms a union of extremely many Zariski-irreducible components in a reduced parameter space, and any single architecture covers at most one. For $G=D_4$ acting on $C$ copies of the regular representation as the token feature space, we show that there are $Ω(C^{64})$ components for eight attention heads.

</details>


### [330] [LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Completion Combining Discrete Structural Coding with Similar Entity Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30235)
*Jiaqi Wang,Dongying Lin,Yang Yang,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CoSC框架，结合离散结构编码与相似实体信息，提升基于大语言模型的知识图谱补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的知识图谱补全方法要么将结构编码为离散token，要么仅对受限候选实体进行精炼，两者分离研究，未充分利用结构相似实体信息。

Method: LLM先根据离散结构编码生成初始候选实体排名，再利用与查询实体结构相似的实体信息精炼该排名。

Result: 在FB15k-237上，CoSC在MRR和Hits@10上优于现有基线，Hits@1上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CoSC有效融合离散结构与相似实体信息，为LLM-based KGC提供新思路。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion requires models to use both textual descriptions and relational structure. Existing LLM-based methods either encode KG structure as discrete tokens or refine a restricted set of candidate entities, and these two directions have largely been studied separately. We propose CoSC for LLM-based KGC, which combines discrete structural coding with similar entity information. Specifically, an LLM generates an initial candidate entity ranking from discrete structural codes, after which information from entities with structures similar to that of the query entity refines the ranking. Experiments on FB15k-237 show that CoSC outperforms existing baselines on MRR and Hits@10 while remaining competitive on Hits@1.

</details>


### [331] [Q-Strata: Hierarchical Bit Allocation for Mixed-Precision Quantization of Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30564)
*Deokjae Lee,Sihun Chu,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Q-Strata，一种针对MoE LLM的混合精度量化分配方法，通过两级优化直接优化模型级目标，在低比特下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中每个专家均含线性层，MPQ分配空间远大于稠密模型，现有方法无法直接优化模型级目标，忽略了跨块耦合。

Method: 采用双层分配器：内层用廉价代理为每块生成不同预算下的帕累托前沿候选集，外层为每块设置一个预算，并直接优化模型级目标，捕获跨块耦合。

Result: 在Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上，低比特时WikiText2困惑度一致低于均匀位宽GPTQ及SOTA方法MxMoE和GEMQ。

Conclusion: Q-Strata通过将搜索空间缩减为每块一个预算，直接优化模型级目标，有效解决MoE MPQ中的跨块耦合问题，提升低比特量化性能。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization (MPQ) assigns a different bitwidth to each linear layer of a large language model (LLM) to minimize the quantization-induced quality loss under a fixed budget, but Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models contain these layers in every expert of every MoE block, so the allocation space grows far larger than in a dense model. Existing methods either allocate within each block under a uniform per-block budget, or allocate across blocks through an additive proxy, and neither directly optimizes a model-level objective over the choices that couple the blocks. We propose Q-Strata, a bi-level allocator that ranks within-block assignments with a cheap proxy and allocates across blocks with a model-level objective evaluated on the assembled quantized model. Its inner stage caches a Pareto frontier of candidates per block over finely spaced budgets, leaving the outer stage to set one budget per block instead of a bitwidth for every linear layer. With the search reduced to one budget per block, the outer stage optimizes this model-level objective directly, capturing the inter-block coupling that additive proxies miss. On Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct, Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite, Q-Strata consistently achieves lower WikiText2 perplexity than uniform-bitwidth GPTQ and the state-of-the-art MoE MPQ methods MxMoE and GEMQ in the low-bit regime. The code is available at https://github.com/snu-mllab/Q-Strata/tree/main.

</details>


### [332] [Collapsibility of Performance Metrics in Clinical Predictive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30568)
*João Matos,Ben Van Calster,Richard D. Riley,Paula Dhiman,Gary S. Collins*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了15种预测AI性能指标的可折叠性，发现AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差和Nagelkerke R²不可折叠，而其他10种可折叠；不可折叠性可能导致公平性评估中的虚假差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口层面的AI性能评估可能掩盖亚组间的表现差异，公平性评估常依赖亚组性能分析，但部分性能指标不可折叠，即总体值不等于亚组值的加权平均，这可能误导公平性判断。

Method: 通过将每种指标表示为分层值的线性组合来检验可折叠性，或对不可折叠指标提供基于辛普森悖论的反例进行正式反驳。

Result: 证明了5种指标（AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差、Nagelkerke R²）不可折叠，10种指标（O:E比、logloss、Brier分数、准确率、F1分数、真阳性率、真阴性率、阳性预测值、阴性预测值、净收益）可折叠；AUC因分解为组内和组间项而可能超出亚组AUC范围。

Conclusion: 性能指标的不可折叠性对报告、模型评估和公平性评价有重要影响，可能产生亚组与总体性能间的虚假差异，明确报告可折叠性可提高公平性评估的透明度和可解释性。

Abstract: Background: Population level assessments of predictive artificial intelligence (AI) can conceal performance disparities across subgroups. Fairness evaluations commonly rely on performance analyses across subgroups. However, some performance metrics are non-collapsible, meaning that the overall population performance value does not equal the weighted average of subgroup specific values.
  Objective: To examine the collapsibility properties of commonly reported performance metrics in predictive AI, with a focus on the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC, also known as c-statistic).
  Methods: We investigate the collapsibility of 15 performance metrics, either by expressing each metric as a linear combination of its stratum specific values or, where non-collapsible, by providing a counterexample inspired by Simpson's paradox as a formal disproof.
  Results: Five performance metrics (AUC, calibration intercept, calibration slope, expected calibration error, and Nagelkerke R^2) are shown to be non-collapsible, and ten (O:E ratio, logloss, Brier score, accuracy, F1-score, true positive rate, true negative rate, positive predictive value, negative predictive value, and net benefit) are shown to be collapsible. The AUC is shown to be non-collapsible because it decomposes into within- and cross-group AUC terms when subpopulations coexist, such that its overall value may fall outside the range of subgroup specific AUCs.
  Conclusions: Non-collapsibility of performance metrics has important consequences for reporting, model appraisal, and fairness evaluation. It can generate spurious differences between subgroup and overall performance, which may mislead fairness evaluations. Explicitly acknowledging and reporting the collapsibility properties of performance metrics improves both the interpretability and transparency of fairness assessments.

</details>


### [333] [MolLedger: An Additive Graph Neural Network with Chemically Grounded ADME Attributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30636)
*Christina X. Ji*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MolLedger图神经网络架构，通过原子级加性分数实现预测的精确可解释性，在保持性能的同时提供比现有方法更符合化学性质的归因解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADME性质预测对药物发现至关重要，但现有机器学习模型预测解释困难，缺乏既能保持性能又能提供化学上合理的原子级归因的方法。

Method: 采用加性框架使预测等于各原子分数之和，利用全局上下文向量为加性头提供足够信息；引入辅助损失将原子分数锚定到化学性质，增强归因的化学合理性。

Result: MolLedger在性能上不逊于其他方法，且其归因结果在分子对案例研究中比现有可解释性方法更忠实于化学性质，能合理解释预测性质变化。

Conclusion: MolLedger实现了无需牺牲性能的精确可解释性，为ADME预测提供了更可信的化学层面解释，有望提升药物设计效率。

Abstract: Optimizing absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion (ADME) is an important part of small molecule drug discovery. Many machine learning models have been built to predict ADME properties to facilitate this optimization process, but explaining model predictions is challenging. We propose a new graph neural network architecture with built-in meaningful per-atom attributions. Our model MolLedger outputs predictions that are the sum of per-atom scores. MolLedger's additive framework obtains exact interpretability at no cost to performance because the global context vector gives the additive head enough context to produce good per-atom scores. Furthermore, MolLedger produces attributions that are more faithful to chemical properties than other interpretability methods because the auxiliary loss in MolLedger anchors the atom scores to chemical properties. Our case studies comparing interpretations from multiple methods on molecular pairs reveal that MolLedger is much better at producing sensible explanations for predicted property changes.

</details>


### [334] [Season-Aware Hybrid Convolutional-Transformer for Antarctic Sea Ice Concentration Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30654)
*Danyang Li,John Taylor,Thang Bui,Quanling Deng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种混合卷积-Transformer框架用于南极海冰浓度月度预测，通过因子化自注意力建模时空依赖，并引入两种季节先验机制，在分类和回归指标上优于卷积和循环基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极SIC预测面临复杂空间结构、长时间依赖和强季节变化三重挑战，传统卷积模型擅长局部空间模式但难以建模长期时间演化。

Method: 构建混合卷积-Transformer框架：卷积编码提取空间特征，因子化自注意力建模时空依赖；引入月感知位置编码注入日历信息，以及季节时间偏置增强对周期性历史状态的关注。

Result: 该框架在分类和回归指标上均优于卷积和循环基线，消融实验表明季节先验机制在短长期预测中均带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 结合卷积结构、注意力机制和周期先验信息能有效提升南极SIC预测性能，为类似强季节性时空预测任务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Antarctic sea ice concentration (SIC) forecasting is an important yet challenging task due to the coexistence of complex spatial structure, long-range temporal dependencies, and strong seasonal variability. Conventional convolution-based models are effective at capturing local spatial patterns, but often have limited ability to model long-term temporal evolution. To address these challenges, we build on a hybrid Convolutional-Transformer forecasting framework for monthly Antarctic SIC forecasting. This framework combines convolutional encoding for spatial feature extraction with factorised self-attention for spatio-temporal dependency modelling. We further introduce two seasonal prior mechanisms: a month-aware positional encoding that injects calendar-month information into the token representation, and a seasonal temporal bias that encourages attention to periodically related historical states. Experimental results show that the proposed framework achieves better performance than convolutional and recurrent baselines across both classification and regression metrics. Ablation studies further indicate that the seasonal prior mechanisms provide consistent additional gains in both short- and long-horizon prediction. These results demonstrate the value of combining convolutional structures, attention mechanisms, and periodic prior information for Antarctic SIC forecasting.

</details>


### [335] [Learning Materials Properties from Scarce Labels and Unlabeled Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30682)
*Wentao Li,Yizhe Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Leyi Zhao,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SemiMat基准和MatRank方法，在稀缺标签的半监督材料性质回归任务上，以固定目标实现最低聚合NMAE（0.896）和最佳平均方法排名（2.208）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性质预测常面临标签稀缺，现有半监督方法缺乏统一基准与可靠的伪标签不确定性处理，导致性能评估不一致且模型提升不可控。

Method: SemiMat固定数据划分、图骨干接口、验证集选择与测试报告；MatRank构建伪目标，按局部可靠性和弱预测一致性加权，训练强弱视图一致，并加入排序信号同时优化数值与候选顺序。

Result: 在24个骨干-任务块中，MatRank取得最低聚合NMAE 0.896和最佳平均方法排名2.208；组件、OOD和生成池诊断进一步揭示可靠性边界。

Conclusion: MatRank在稀缺标签材料回归中稳定领先，但OOD与生成池场景仍需更严格的筛选评估；基准与代码公开促进可复现比较。

Abstract: Learning materials properties from scarce labels and unlabeled crystals is a central challenge for data-driven materials discovery. We present SemiMat, a controlled benchmark for semi-supervised materials property regression, and MatRank, a reliability-weighted objective for continuous pseudo-label uncertainty. SemiMat fixes labeled and unlabeled crystal inputs, graph-backbone interfaces, validation-only checkpoint selection, held-out test reporting, normalized MAE (NMAE), and method-rank summaries across six scarce-label tasks, four graph backbones, and five predefined split runs. MatRank builds pseudo-targets from labeled anchors, weights them by local reliability and weak-prediction agreement, trains weak and strong graph views consistently, and adds ranking signals so that unlabeled crystals shape both values and candidate order. Across the retained 24 backbone-task blocks, one fixed MatRank objective gives the lowest aggregate held-out test NMAE (0.896) and best average method rank (2.208). The component, OOD, and generated-pool diagnostics identify where the gain is reliable and where further screening evaluation remains necessary. Code is available at https://github.com/littlepeachs/SemiMat.

</details>


### [336] [CHASE: How Content Ecosystems Are Reshaped When Ranking Is the Only Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30466)
*Qianwen Gao,Zichang Su,Yiwen Hou,Arlen Kumar,Leanid Palkhouski*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CHASE框架，模拟创作者反复针对LLM排序信号优化内容导致的生态同质化，发现排序-质量对齐度在六域中一致下降（平均Spearman rho变化-0.068），且此效应源于对排序激励的适应而非单纯改写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEO研究多关注单轮优化效果，缺乏对重复优化下内容生态群体效应的理解，尤其不清楚创作者持续适应LLM排序信号会如何重塑内容分布与质量激励。

Method: 构建CHASE受控模拟框架，以排序作为可见性代理（经六域rank-citation AUC 0.853±0.093验证），迭代执行排序→特征判别→重写→评估共20轮，并设置随机目标对照组分离重写本身与适应排序激励的效应。

Result: 六域中排序-质量对齐度从R0到R20均下降，Spearman rho变化范围-0.107至-0.018，平均-0.068；随机目标对照证实该下降主要源于对排序信号的适应性优化而非单纯迭代改写；生态动态呈现强领域依赖性。

Conclusion: 重复对固定LLM排序信号优化会重塑内容种群结构并改变创作者激励，导致质量与排名脱钩，提示需关注GEO的长期生态副作用并设计更稳健的排序机制。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is increasingly used to improve content visibility in LLM-based retrieval systems, yet its population-level effects under repeated optimization remain poorly understood. We introduce Content Homogenization under rAnking Signal Exploitation (CHASE), a controlled simulation framework for studying how content ecosystems are reshaped when creators repeatedly adapt documents to an LLM ranking signal. We use ranking as a proxy for source visibility and validate this abstraction against citations in grounded generated responses, obtaining a rank-citation AUC of 0.853 $\pm$ 0.093 across six domains. CHASE then iterates ranking, feature discrimination, rewriting, and evaluation over 20 rounds across different domains. Quality-ranking alignment decreases in all six domains: from R0 to R20, the change in Spearman's rho ranges from -0.107 to -0.018, with a mean change of -0.068, which means documents closer to the ranking feature profile become less aligned with independently judged document quality over the simulation horizon. A random-target control has shown that it is associated with adaptation toward ranking-derived incentives rather than iterative rewriting alone. The resulting ecosystem dynamics are strongly domain-dependent. Together, these findings show how repeated optimization against a fixed LLM ranking signal can reshape both content populations and the incentives faced by content creators.

</details>


### [337] [Learning Dynamics of Logits Debiasing for Long-Tailed Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30699)
*Yue Cheng,Jiajun Zhang,Xiaohui Gao,Weiwei Xing,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过学习动力学视角统一解释了长尾半监督学习中的隐式logit去偏机制，并提出了基于梯度预算重分配的动态剪枝框架DyTrim，在多个基准上验证了其有效性和理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾分布普遍存在于现实半监督学习中，伪标签倾向于偏向多数类导致泛化性能下降。尽管已有多种LTSSL方法，但其隐式去偏机制缺乏理论理解，亟需统一的分析框架和原理性改进方案。

Method: 从学习动力学角度推导logit更新的逐步分解，证明任务无关基线图像的logits收敛到类别先验，以此揭示类不平衡偏差的累积特性；将现有LTSSL方法统一视为重塑梯度动力学，进而提出DyTrim动态剪枝框架，在标注数据上执行类别感知剪枝、在未标注数据上执行置信度软剪枝，并给出理论保证。

Result: 在标准LTSSL基准上的大量实验表明，DyTrim在不同架构和方法上均取得一致性性能提升，且实验验证了理论分析的有效性，表明其能有效降低类别偏差并改善泛化。

Conclusion: 该工作为LTSSL提供了统一的理论视角，并验证了基于梯度预算重分配的动态剪枝策略的可行性，为后续研究提供了原则性框架和可复现的代码实现。

Abstract: Long-tailed distributions are prevalent in real-world semi-supervised learning (SSL), where pseudo-labels tend to favor majority classes, leading to degraded generalization. While many long-tailed semi-supervised learning (LTSSL) methods have been proposed, the mechanisms by which they implicitly debias logits remain poorly understood. In this work, we revisit LTSSL through the lens of learning dynamics and provide a theoretical characterization of logits debiasing. Specifically, we derive a step-wise decomposition of the logits updates, showing that predictions are dominated by class-imbalance bias that reliably reflects label priors. To expose this effect, we use the logits of a task-irrelevant baseline image as an indicator of accumulated bias and prove that they converge to the class prior. This provides a unified view where LTSSL remedies such as logit adjustment, reweighting, and resampling correspond to reshaping gradient dynamics. Based on this insight, we propose DyTrim, a principle-based dynamic pruning framework that reallocates gradient budget through class-aware pruning on labeled data and confidence-based soft pruning on unlabeled data. We provide theoretical guarantees that DyTrim reduces class bias and improves generalization. Extensive experiments on standard LTSSL benchmarks show consistent gains across architectures and methods. Code available at: https://jiajun0425.github.io/DyTrim

</details>


### [338] [E-Commerce Bench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Business Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30730)
*Wei Fan,Xinjie Shen,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Yang Su,Yinger Zhang,Lianghao Deng,Fengyu Wang,Baohua Dong,Yangqiu Song,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出E-Commerce Bench，首个集成多轮谈判与动态事件的长期电商运营基准，评估18个前沿LLM在365天内的资产管理与适应性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注短任务链，缺乏对长期动态环境、经验积累与策略自适应能力的评测，需构建真实且可复现的长期智能体测试平台。

Method: 基于真实电商数据构建模拟市场，含固定需求模型与确定性谈判内核，LLM仅负责语言化，智能体需同时管理采购、销售、履约、现金流等全年运营。

Result: 无单一模型全面占优；GPT-5.6 Sol收益最高（10万增至143万），但防欺诈排名16/18；开源模型中Qwen3.8-Max-Preview资产领先（41.6万），且学习能力最强。

Conclusion: 长期运营能力与短期收益并不一致，现有模型在资源分配、风险规避和动态优化上仍有显著短板，该基准可推动更全面的智能体评估。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks go beyond chaining short tasks over more interaction turns. Their evolving dynamic environments and long-range dependencies require Large Language Models (LLMs) to continually explore, learn from experience, and adapt their policies over thousands of steps. We introduce E-Commerce Bench, the first open-source benchmark that integrates multi-round counterpart negotiation and dynamic events into a year-long business operation. Over a 365-day year, an LLM agent concurrently runs multiple online stores, researching the market, negotiating with suppliers to source inventory, optimizing sales strategies, fulfilling orders, handling returns, and managing cash flow to maximize its end-of-year total assets. To construct a realistic merchant-side operating environment, the product and supplier data are derived from a real e-commerce platform, while a year-long calendar of promotions, natural disasters, and supply-chain shocks continually reshapes demand. For reproducibility, both sides of the market are deterministic: customer purchases and returns follow a fixed demand model, while a negotiation kernel determines supplier pricing, concessions, and decisions, with an LLM used only to verbalize them. We evaluate 18 frontier models across seven dimensions, including year-end assets, and find that no single model dominates. GPT-5.6 Sol earns the most, growing the 100,000 opening stake into 1,431,425, yet it ranks 16th of 18 on fraud avoidance and trails Fable5 in operational efficiency. Among open-weight models, Qwen3.8-Max-Preview leads with 416,252, 38% above GLM 5.2 (high), and achieves the strongest learning over the horizon, progressively bargaining down prices across repeated orders. Our code is available at https://github.com/QwenLM/E-CommerceBench.

</details>


### [339] [GarmentWeaver: Schema-Aware Structured Synthesis for Multimodal Sewing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30550)
*Yinwen Lu,Weihao Luo,Yueqi Zhong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出GarmentWeaver，一种面向多模态缝纫图案生成的模式感知框架，通过构建紧凑的层次化目标并结构化预测可执行缝纫图案，显著提升生成精度与仿真兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将服装规格建模为扁平长序列，导致结构纠缠、冗余组件、局部细节不准确以及仿真兼容性差，难以有效利用草图与文本等多模态设计线索。

Method: 采用模式感知的目标构建策略激活服装相关结构分支，基于预训练视觉-语言模型构建生成器以增强多模态理解，并引入可行性感知正则化确保输出结构有效且可仿真。

Result: 在多项实验中，GarmentWeaver相比强基线生成更精确、更可执行的缝纫图案，并取得更优的仿真结果。

Conclusion: 模式感知的结构化生成策略能有效提升多模态缝纫图案预测的可靠性与实用性，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Sewing pattern generation aims to infer executable sewing patterns from design cues such as sketches and textual descriptions. As an interpretable and simulation-compatible representation, sewing patterns are particularly valuable for digital garment creation. However, existing methods often model garment specifications as flat long sequences, which entangles garment structure with detailed parameters and leads to redundant components, inaccurate local details, and poor simulation compatibility. In this paper, we present GarmentWeaver, a schema-aware framework for multimodal Sewing pattern generation. GarmentWeaver constructs compact hierarchical targets by activating garment-relevant structural branches and predicts executable Sewing patterns in a structured manner. Specifically, we introduce a schema-aware target construction strategy, build the generator on top of a pretrained vision-language model for multimodal garment understanding, and impose feasibility-aware regularization to encourage structurally valid and simulation-compatible outputs. Extensive experiments show that GarmentWeaver produces more accurate and more executable sewing patterns than strong baselines, while also yielding better simulation results. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of schema-aware structured generation for reliable multimodal Sewing pattern prediction.

</details>


### [340] [Reciprocity Separates Gradient Flow from Rotation in Conservative Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30778)
*Ruiwu Niu,Xiaowen Bi,Michaël Antonie van Wyk*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理学习系统中，系统遵循传统梯度下降还是演化出不同学习轨迹的决定因素，通过有向分层传输网络模型，区分了守恒、互易性和非互易性的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习允许可训练材料或网络利用自身的物理响应来传递误差信号，减少了单独编程反向计算的需求，但尚不清楚这类系统何时遵循传统梯度下降，何时会沿完全不同的学习轨迹演化，本文旨在探究这一决定因素。

Method: 采用有向分层传输网络作为典型模型，每个节点重新分配固定流量，学习过程保持正性和总质量；在匹配响应类中通过伴随匹配使物理输出响应对称，引入非负模态反馈产生互易闭环响应和加权梯度流，并添加反对称边界分量使闭环响应旋转，结合数值一致性检查验证结构并预测局部效应符号。

Result: 守恒仅约束允许的学习方向；伴随匹配产生对称响应，非负模态反馈导致互易闭环和重加权梯度流；反对称边界使闭环响应旋转，学习路径可转向且误差同时下降；转向的有限步效应由局部曲率决定，累积效应依赖步选择和路径上的新状态；数值检验重现精确响应结构，预测新网络族局部效应符号，显示轨迹漂移可抵消局部优势。

Conclusion: 研究分离了守恒、互易性和非互易性在物理学习中的作用，明确了非互易性导致旋转学习轨迹但并非自动有益，其效果受局部曲率和路径选择影响，为理解物理学习中的学习轨迹偏离梯度下降提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Physical learning lets a trainable material or network use its own physical response to carry error signals, reducing the need for a separately programmed backward computation. We ask what determines whether such a system follows conventional gradient descent or evolves along a genuinely different learning trajectory. Our canonical model is a directed layered transport network in which every node redistributes a fixed amount of flow, so learning preserves positivity and total mass. In this model, conservation constrains only the allowable learning directions. Within the matched response class studied here, adjoint matching gives the physical output response a symmetric form. Non-negative mode-wise feedback then produces a reciprocal closed-loop response and a reweighted gradient flow. Adding an antisymmetric boundary component makes the closed-loop response rotational: the learning path can turn while the error driving that update still decreases at that moment. Turning is not automatically beneficial. Its finite-step effect is set by local curvature, and its accumulated effect also depends on step selection and on the new states visited along the path. Numerical consistency checks reproduce the exact response structure, predict the sign of the local effect across new network families, and show how trajectory drift can negate a local advantage.
